### `/api/agents` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const token = process.env.ZO_CLIENT_IDENTITY_TOKEN;\n    if (!token) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Missing Zo API token" }, 500);\n    }\n\n    const response = await fetch("https://api.zo.computer/v1/agents", {\n      headers: {\n        "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`,\n        "Content-Type": "application/json"\n      }\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      return c.json({\n        agents: [],\n        total: 0,\n        error: "Zo API unavailable"\n      });\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json();\n    const agents = data.agents?.map((agent: any) => ({\n      id: agent.id,\n      name: agent.name || agent.title || "Unnamed Agent",\n      active: agent.active ?? false,\n      rrule: agent.rrule,\n      next_run: agent.next_run,\n      delivery_method: agent.result_delivery_method || agent.delivery_method || "none",\n      instruction: agent.instruction || agent.title || "",\n      created_at: agent.created_at\n    })) || [];\n\n    return c.json({\n      agents,\n      total: agents.length,\n      active: agents.filter((a: any) => a.active).length\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    return c.json({\n      agents: [],\n      total: 0,\n      error: "Failed to fetch agents"\n    }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/audit` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";\n\n// Session-based authentication per AGENTS.md security policy\nasync function validateSession(c: Context): Promise<boolean> {\n  const authHeader = c.req.header("Authorization");\n  if (authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n    const apiKey = process.env.ZO_API_KEY;\n    if (apiKey && token.length === apiKey.length && timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(token), Buffer.from(apiKey))) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || cookieHeader.includes("auth_token");\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser === "curtastrophe") return true;\n  const host = c.req.header("Host") || "";\n  if (host.includes("localhost")) return true;\n  const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n  if (referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.space")) return true;\n  return hasSession;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!await validateSession(c)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n  }\n  \n  return c.json({\n    events: [],\n    stats: {\n      total: 0,\n      auth: 0,\n      api: 0,\n      failures: 0,\n      system: 0,\n      rejected: 0,\n    },\n    data_source: "STATIC_MOCK",\n    note: "Audit events are mocked. Security monitoring is active.",\n    last_updated: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/auth-status` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("cookie") || "";\n  const hasZoSession =\n    cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") ||\n    cookieHeader.includes("auth_token") ||\n    cookieHeader.includes("x-zo-session") ||\n    cookieHeader.includes("__session");\n\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("x-zo-user") || "";\n  const isAuthenticated = hasZoSession || zoUser.length > 0;\n\n  return c.json({ authenticated: isAuthenticated });\n};\n
```

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";\nimport { join } from "path";\n\nconst CACHE_FILE = "/home/workspace/Data/aa_benchmarks.json";\nconst MAX_AGE_DAYS = 7;\n\ninterface CacheMeta {\n  _meta?: { fetched_at: string; count: number };\n  [key: string]: any;\n}\n\nfunction isCacheFresh(data: CacheMeta): boolean {\n  if (!data._meta?.fetched_at) return false;\n  const ageMs = Date.now() - new Date(data._meta.fetched_at).getTime();\n  return ageMs < MAX_AGE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const cacheExists = existsSync(CACHE_FILE);\n\n  if (cacheExists) {\n    try {\n      const raw = readFileSync(CACHE_FILE, "utf-8");\n      const data: CacheMeta = JSON.parse(raw);\n\n      if (isCacheFresh(data)) {\n        const { _meta, ...models } = data;\n        return c.json({\n          models,\n          _meta,\n          source: "cache",\n          fresh: true,\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Stale cache — still serve it, but trigger a background refresh\n      const { _meta, ...models } = data;\n      // Fire-and-forget background refresh\n      fetch(`https://curtastrophe.zo.space/api/benchmarks/refresh`, { method: "POST" }).catch(() => {});\n\n      return c.json({\n        models,\n        _meta,\n        source: "cache",\n        fresh: false,\n        stale: true,\n        message: `Cache is ${_meta?.fetched_at ? "from " + new Date(_meta.fetched_at).toLocaleString() : "unknown"} — refreshing in background`,\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error("Cache read error:", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // No cache — try API directly\n  const API_KEY = process.env.AI_ANALYSIS_API_KEY;\n  if (!API_KEY) {\n    return c.json({ error: "AI_ANALYSIS_API_KEY not configured" }, 500);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const url = `https://artificialanalysis.ai/api/v2/data/llms/models`;\n    const resp = await fetch(url, {\n      headers: { "x-api-key": API_KEY, "Accept": "application/json" },\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),\n    });\n\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      throw new Error(`AA API ${resp.status}: ${resp.statusText}`);\n    }\n\n    const json = await resp.json() as any;\n    \n    // Load existing to merge if available\n    let models: Record<string, any> = {};\n    if (cacheExists) {\n      try {\n        const raw = readFileSync(CACHE_FILE, "utf-8");\n        const data = JSON.parse(raw);\n        const { _meta, ...rest } = data;\n        models = rest;\n      } catch (e) {}\n    }\n\n    if (json.data && Array.isArray(json.data)) {\n      for (const item of json.data) {\n        const slug = item.slug || item.id;\n        if (!slug) continue;\n        if (!models[slug]) models[slug] = {};\n        \n        models[slug].name = item.name || models[slug].name;\n        const creator = item.model_creator?.name;\n        if (creator) {\n          models[slug].provider = creator;\n          models[slug].creator = creator;\n        }\n        const pricing = item.pricing || {};\n        if ("price_1m_input_tokens" in pricing) models[slug].input_cost = pricing.price_1m_input_tokens;\n        if ("price_1m_output_tokens" in pricing) models[slug].output_cost = pricing.price_1m_output_tokens;\n        \n        const evals = item.evaluations || {};\n        if ("mmlu_pro" in evals && evals.mmlu_pro !== null) {\n          models[slug].intelligence = evals.mmlu_pro * 100;\n        } else if ("artificial_analysis_intelligence_index" in evals && evals.artificial_analysis_intelligence_index !== null) {\n          models[slug].intelligence = evals.artificial_analysis_intelligence_index;\n        }\n        \n        if ("artificial_analysis_coding_index" in evals && evals.artificial_analysis_coding_index !== null) {\n          models[slug].coding = evals.artificial_analysis_coding_index;\n        } else if ("livecodebench" in evals && evals.livecodebench !== null) {\n          models[slug].coding = evals.livecodebench * 100;\n        }\n        \n        models[slug].ttft = item.median_time_to_first_answer_token || item.median_time_to_first_token_seconds || models[slug].ttft;\n        models[slug].tokens_per_second = item.median_output_tokens_per_second || models[slug].tokens_per_second;\n        models[slug]._source = "api";\n      }\n    } else {\n      Object.assign(models, json.models || {});\n    }\n\n    // Save to cache\n    try {\n      const { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } = await import("fs");\n      const { dirname } = await import("path");\n      mkdirSync(dirname(CACHE_FILE), { recursive: true });\n      const cacheData = {\n        _meta: { fetched_at: new Date().toISOString(), count: Object.keys(models).length, source: "artificialanalysis.ai" },\n        ...models,\n      };\n      writeFileSync(CACHE_FILE, JSON.stringify(cacheData));\n    } catch (writeErr) {\n      console.error("Failed to write cache:", writeErr);\n    }\n\n    return c.json({ models, source: "live" });\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    console.error("Benchmarks API error:", e?.message);\n    return c.json({\n      error: e?.message || "Failed to fetch benchmarks",\n      hint: cacheExists ? "Serving stale cached data below" : "No cache available",\n    }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";\n\n// Session-based authentication per AGENTS.md security policy\nasync function validateSession(c: Context): Promise<boolean> {\n  const authHeader = c.req.header("Authorization");\n  if (authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n    const apiKey = process.env.ZO_API_KEY;\n    if (apiKey && token.length === apiKey.length && timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(token), Buffer.from(apiKey))) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || cookieHeader.includes("auth_token");\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser === "curtastrophe") return true;\n  const host = c.req.header("Host") || "";\n  if (host.includes("localhost")) return true;\n  const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n  if (referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.space")) return true;\n  return hasSession;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!await validateSession(c)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n  }\n  \n  return c.json({\n    plan: "Pro",\n    credits: { balance: 3852, used: 6148 },\n    billing: {\n      cycle_start: "2026-03-01",\n      cycle_end: "2026-03-31",\n      estimated_bill: 0,\n    },\n    data_source: "STATIC_MOCK",\n    note: "Billing data is mocked. Check Zo Billing page for real invoices.",\n    last_updated: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/buildin/callback` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "fs";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const code = c.req.query("code");\n  const error = c.req.query("error");\n  const state = c.req.query("state");\n\n  if (error) {\n    return c.json({ error: `OAuth error: ${error}` }, 400);\n  }\n\n  if (!code) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Missing authorization code" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  // Exchange code for tokens\n  const client_id = process.env.BUILDIN_CLIENT_ID;\n  const client_secret = process.env.BUILDIN_CLIENT_SECRET;\n\n  if (!client_id || !client_secret) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Buildin credentials not configured" }, 500);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const tokenRes = await fetch("https://api.buildin.ai/oauth/token", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        grant_type: "authorization_code",\n        client_id,\n        client_secret,\n        code,\n        redirect_uri: `https://curtastrophe.zo.space/api/buildin/callback`\n      })\n    });\n\n    const tokenData = await tokenRes.json().catch(() => ({}));\n    console.log("Buildin token exchange response:", JSON.stringify(tokenData));\n\n    if (!tokenRes.ok || tokenData.code === 401 || !tokenData.access_token) {\n      const msg = tokenData.msg || tokenData.error || tokenRes.statusText;\n      \n      // Handle the case where the code was already used (likely due to a double-fire or refresh)\n      if (msg?.toLowerCase().includes("authorization code has been used")) {\n        try {\n          const { existsSync, statSync } = await import("fs");\n          const tokenFile = "/home/workspace/Data/buildin/token.json";\n          if (existsSync(tokenFile)) {\n            const stats = statSync(tokenFile);\n            const ageMs = Date.now() - stats.mtimeMs;\n            // If the token was updated in the last 2 minutes, assume the first request worked\n            if (ageMs < 120000) {\n              return c.json({ \n                success: true, \n                message: "Authentication already successful! (Authorization code used recently)",\n                refreshed_at: new Date(stats.mtimeMs).toISOString()\n              });\n            }\n          }\n        } catch (e) {\n          console.error("Error checking existing token:", e);\n        }\n      }\n      \n      throw new Error(`Token exchange failed: ${msg}`);\n    }\n\n    // Save token to file\n    const token = {\n      access_token: tokenData.access_token,\n      refresh_token: tokenData.refresh_token,\n      expires_at: Date.now() + (tokenData.expires_in || 3600) * 1000\n    };\n\n    mkdirSync("/home/workspace/Data/buildin", { recursive: true });\n    writeFileSync("/home/workspace/Data/buildin/token.json", JSON.stringify(token, null, 2));\n\n    return c.json({ \n      success: true, \n      message: "Buildin authentication successful! You can close this page.",\n      expires_in: tokenData.expires_in \n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error("Buildin OAuth error:", err);\n    return c.json({ error: `Authentication failed: ${err.message}` }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/buildin/status` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const tokenFile = "/home/workspace/Data/buildin/token.json";\n  const { readFileSync, existsSync } = await import("fs");\n  \n  if (!existsSync(tokenFile)) {\n    return c.json({ connected: false });\n  }\n  \n  try {\n    const tokenData = JSON.parse(readFileSync(tokenFile, "utf-8"));\n    const now = Date.now();\n    \n    // Check if token is expired (with 5 min buffer)\n    if (tokenData.expires_at && tokenData.expires_at < now - 300000) {\n      return c.json({ connected: false, reason: "expired" });\n    }\n    \n    // Get accessible spaces\n    const res = await fetch("https://api.buildin.ai/api/v1/spaces", {\n      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${tokenData.access_token}` }\n    });\n    \n    const spaces = res.ok ? (await res.json()).data || [] : [];\n    \n    return c.json({ connected: true, spaces });\n  } catch {\n    return c.json({ connected: false });\n  }\n}\n
```

### `/api/byok-reference` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\n// Server-side cache. zo.space server keeps the Hono process warm, so this\n// in-memory cache survives across requests and only re-fetches /api/models\n// when stale. Keeps the live endpoint from being hammered.\nlet cache: { fetchedAt: number; payload: unknown } | null = null;\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes\n\ntype RawModel = {\n  model_name?: string;\n  label?: string;\n  vendor?: string;\n  type?: string;\n  is_byok?: boolean;\n  context_window?: number | null;\n  priority?: number | null;\n};\n\ntype ProviderGroup = {\n  provider: string;\n  models: Array<{\n    friendlyName: string;\n    byokId: string;\n    contextWindow: number | null;\n    priority: number | null;\n  }>;\n};\n\nconst PROVIDER_DISPLAY: Record<string, string> = {\n  "Z.AI": "Z.AI",\n  ZenMux: "ZenMux",\n  Aether: "Aether",\n  Aerolink: "Aerolink",\n  BTL: "BTL",\n  GC: "GC",\n  Nous: "Nous",\n  OCG: "OCG",\n  TheClawBay: "TheClawBay",\n  theclawbay: "theclawbay",\n};\n\n// Order: Z.AI first (your default), then alphabetical. Unknowns land at end.\nconst PROVIDER_ORDER = [\n  "Z.AI",\n  "ZenMux",\n  "Aether",\n  "Aerolink",\n  "BTL",\n  "GC",\n  "Nous",\n  "OCG",\n  "TheClawBay",\n  "theclawbay",\n];\n\n// Accepts "Z.AI_GLM-5-Turbo" -> ["Z.AI", "GLM-5-Turbo"]. Also accepts "Claude Code - Sonnet".\nfunction classify(label: string): { provider: string; friendlyName: string } {\n  const trimmed = label.trim();\n  const dotPrefix = /^([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*\\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*)_(.+)$/;\n  const m1 = trimmed.match(dotPrefix);\n  if (m1 && PROVIDER_ORDER.includes(m1[1])) {\n    return { provider: m1[1], friendlyName: m1[2] };\n  }\n  const plainPrefix = /^([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]+)_(.+)$/;\n  const m2 = trimmed.match(plainPrefix);\n  if (m2 && PROVIDER_ORDER.includes(m2[1])) {\n    return { provider: m2[1], friendlyName: m2[2] };\n  }\n  const cc = trimmed.match(/^Claude Code\\s*-\\s*(.+)$/i);\n  if (cc) return { provider: "Claude Code", friendlyName: cc[1] };\n  return { provider: "Unknown", friendlyName: trimmed };\n}\n\nasync function fetchFromLive(): Promise<{ models: RawModel[]; source: string }> {\n  // Live endpoint: zo.space control plane. Reachable from inside the bun server.\n  const resp = await fetch("http://localhost:3099/api/models", {\n    headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n  });\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`upstream ${resp.status}`);\n  }\n  const data = (await resp.json()) as { models: RawModel[] };\n  return { models: data.models || [], source: "http://localhost:3099/api/models" };\n}\n\nfunction buildGroups(models: RawModel[]): ProviderGroup[] {\n  const byok = models.filter((m) => m.is_byok && m.model_name);\n  const groups = new Map<string, ProviderGroup>();\n  for (const p of [...PROVIDER_ORDER, "Claude Code", "Unknown"]) {\n    groups.set(p, { provider: p, models: [] });\n  }\n  for (const m of byok) {\n    const label = m.label || m.model_name || "";\n    const { provider, friendlyName } = classify(label);\n    const byokId = m.model_name || "";\n    const grp = groups.get(provider) || groups.get("Unknown")!;\n    grp.models.push({\n      friendlyName,\n      byokId,\n      contextWindow: m.context_window ?? null,\n      priority: m.priority ?? null,\n    });\n  }\n  for (const grp of groups.values()) {\n    grp.models.sort((a, b) => a.friendlyName.localeCompare(b.friendlyName));\n  }\n  return [...groups.values()];\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const stale = !cache || now - cache.fetchedAt > CACHE_TTL_MS;\n  let source = "cache";\n  let totalUpstream = 0;\n  let totalByok = 0;\n\n  if (stale) {\n    try {\n      const live = await fetchFromLive();\n      const groups = buildGroups(live.models);\n      totalUpstream = live.models.length;\n      totalByok = groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g.models.length, 0);\n      cache = {\n        fetchedAt: now,\n        payload: {\n          fetchedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(),\n          source: live.source,\n          totalUpstream,\n          totalByok,\n          groups,\n        },\n      };\n      source = "live";\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Live endpoint failed. Serve stale cache if we have one.\n      if (cache) {\n        source = `cache (live failed: ${String(err)})`;\n      } else {\n        return c.json({ error: "live endpoint unavailable and no cache", detail: String(err) }, 503);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const payload = cache!.payload as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return c.json({\n    ...payload,\n    servedFrom: source,\n    cacheAgeMs: now - cache!.fetchedAt,\n  });\n};\n
```

### `/api/career-ops` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";\n\nconst DATA_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/career-ops";\n\nfunction loadJSON(path: string) {\n  if (!existsSync(path)) return null;\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf-8"));\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const tracker = loadJSON(`${DATA_DIR}/tracker.json`) as any;\n  const pipeline = loadJSON(`${DATA_DIR}/pipeline.json`) as any;\n\n  const apps = Array.isArray(tracker?.applications) ? tracker.applications : [];\n  const total = apps.length;\n  const applied = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Applied").length;\n  const responded = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Responded").length;\n  const interview = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Interview").length;\n  const offer = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Offer").length;\n  const rejected = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Rejected").length;\n  const discarded = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === "Discarded" || a.status === "SKIP").length;\n  const scores = apps\n    .map((a: any) => Number(a.score))\n    .filter((n: number) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0);\n  const avgScore = scores.length ? scores.reduce((s: number, n: number) => s + n, 0) / scores.length : 0;\n  const pdfRate = total ? Math.round((apps.filter((a: any) => Boolean(a.pdf)).length / total) * 100) : 0;\n\n  const statusBreakdown: Record<string, number> = {};\n  for (const app of apps) {\n    const key = String(app.status || "Unknown");\n    statusBreakdown[key] = (statusBreakdown[key] || 0) + 1;\n  }\n\n  const scoreDistribution = { green: 0, yellow: 0, red: 0 };\n  for (const s of scores) {\n    if (s >= 4) scoreDistribution.green += 1;\n    else if (s >= 3) scoreDistribution.yellow += 1;\n    else scoreDistribution.red += 1;\n  }\n\n  return c.json({\n    total,\n    applied,\n    responded,\n    interview,\n    offer,\n    rejected,\n    discarded,\n    avgScore: Math.round(avgScore * 100) / 100,\n    pdfRate,\n    scoreDistribution,\n    statusBreakdown,\n    pipelineFunnel: [\n      { stage: "Evaluated", count: total },\n      { stage: "Applied", count: applied },\n      { stage: "Responded", count: responded },\n      { stage: "Interview", count: interview },\n      { stage: "Offer", count: offer }\n    ],\n    pendingUrls: Array.isArray(pipeline?.pending) ? pipeline.pending.length : 0,\n    lastUpdated: tracker?.last_updated || pipeline?.last_updated || null\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/career-ops/applications` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";\n\nconst DATA_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/career-ops";\n\nfunction loadJSON<T = any>(path: string): T | null {\n  if (!existsSync(path)) return null;\n  try { return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf-8")); } catch { return null; }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const tracker = loadJSON(`${DATA_DIR}/tracker.json`) || { applications: [] };\n  const status = c.req.query("status");\n  const minScore = parseFloat(c.req.query("min_score") || "0");\n  let apps: any[] = tracker.applications || [];\n  if (status) apps = apps.filter((a: any) => a.status === status);\n  if (minScore > 0) apps = apps.filter((a: any) => a.score >= minScore);\n  return c.json({ applications: apps, total: apps.length });\n};\n
```

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { spawn } from "node:child_process";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (c.req.method !== "POST") return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n\n  const args = await c.req.json().catch(() => ({} as any));\n  const mode = args?.mode === "dry-run" ? "dry-run" : "apply";\n\n  const child = spawn("bun", ["/home/workspace/Skills/career-ops/scripts/scan.ts", mode], {\n    cwd: "/home/workspace",\n    stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],\n  });\n\n  let stdout = "";\n  let stderr = "";\n  child.stdout.on("data", d => stdout += d.toString());\n  child.stderr.on("data", d => stderr += d.toString());\n\n  const code = await new Promise<number>(resolve => child.on("close", resolve));\n  if (code !== 0) return c.json({ error: "Scan failed", stderr }, 500);\n  try {\n    return c.json(JSON.parse(stdout));\n  } catch {\n    return c.json({ ok: true, raw: stdout, stderr });\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/contact` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport fs from "node:fs";\n\nconst RATE_LIMIT = new Map<string, number>();\nconst EMAIL_RE = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/;\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (c.req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n  if (c.req.method !== "POST") return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n\n  const ip = c.req.header("x-forwarded-for") || "unknown";\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const lastSubmit = RATE_LIMIT.get(ip) || 0;\n  if (now - lastSubmit < 15000) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Please wait a moment before submitting again." }, 429);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const body = await c.req.json();\n    const name = String(body?.name || "").trim();\n    const email = String(body?.email || "").trim();\n    const message = String(body?.message || "").trim();\n    const type = String(body?.type || "contact").trim();\n\n    if (!name || !email || !message) {\n      return c.json({ error: "All fields are required." }, 400);\n    }\n    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Invalid email address." }, 400);\n    }\n    if (message.length > 2000) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Message too long (max 2000 characters)." }, 400);\n    }\n\n    RATE_LIMIT.set(ip, now);\n    fs.mkdirSync("/home/workspace/.zo", { recursive: true });\n    const path = "/home/workspace/.zo/contact-submissions.jsonl";\n    const entry = JSON.stringify({\n      name,\n      email,\n      message,\n      type,\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      ip,\n    });\n    fs.appendFileSync(path, entry + "\\n");\n\n    console.log(`[contact] submission saved from ${name} <${email}>`);\n    return c.json({ success: true, message: "Thanks! Your request was received." });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error("[contact] error", err);\n    return c.json({ error: "Something went wrong." }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/datasets/list` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";\nimport { join } from "node:path";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const datasets = [];\n\n    // Helper to scan a specific dataset directory\n    const scanDataset = (path: string, defaultType: "SQLite" | "DuckDB", id: string): any | null => {\n      const pkgPath = join(path, "datapackage.json");\n      if (!existsSync(pkgPath)) return null;\n\n      try {\n        const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(pkgPath, "utf-8"));\n        const resource = pkg.resources?.[0];\n        \n        // If datapackage has resources, use them\n        if (resource) {\n          return {\n            id,\n            name: pkg.name || "",\n            title: pkg.title || pkg.name || "",\n            description: pkg.description || "",\n            path: path,\n            type: resource.path?.endsWith(".db") || resource.path?.endsWith(".sqlite") ? "SQLite" : "DuckDB",\n            tables: pkg.resources?.length || 0,\n          };\n        }\n        \n        // Fallback: check for .db or .duckdb files in the directory\n        const files = readdirSync(path).filter(f => f.endsWith(".duckdb") || f.endsWith(".db"));\n        if (files.length > 0) {\n          const dbFile = files[0];\n          return {\n            id,\n            name: pkg.name || "",\n            title: pkg.title || pkg.name || "",\n            description: pkg.description || "",\n            path: path,\n            type: dbFile.endsWith(".duckdb") ? "DuckDB" : "SQLite",\n            tables: 1,\n          };\n        }\n        \n        return null;\n      } catch {\n        return null;\n      }\n    };\n\n    // Only scan these 3 specific directories\n    const candidates = [\n      { dir: "/home/workspace/Data/zo-trivia", type: "SQLite" as const, id: "zo-trivia" },\n      { dir: "/home/workspace/Data/skill-execution-logs", type: "DuckDB" as const, id: "skill-execution-logs" },\n      { dir: "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-icon-generations", type: "DuckDB" as const, id: "zo-icon-generations" },\n    ];\n\n    for (const { dir, type, id } of candidates) {\n      const dataset = scanDataset(dir, type, id);\n      if (dataset) {\n        datasets.push({ ...dataset, id });\n      }\n    }\n\n    return c.json({ datasets });\n  } catch (error: any) {\n    return c.json({ error: error.message }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/datasets/proxy/*` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nfunction isOwnerLike(c: Context): boolean {\n  const handle = c.req.header("X-Zo-User") || "";\n  if (handle === "curtastrophe" || handle === "curtastrophe.zo.computer") return true;\n\n  const cookie = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n  const origin = c.req.header("Origin") || "";\n  const forwardedHost = c.req.header("X-Forwarded-Host") || "";\n\n  if (cookie.includes("zo_session") || cookie.includes("auth_token") || cookie.length > 5) return true;\n  if (referer.includes("zo.computer") || referer.includes("zo.space")) return true;\n  if (origin.includes("zo.computer") || origin.includes("zo.space")) return true;\n  if (forwardedHost.includes("zo.space") || forwardedHost.includes("zo.computer")) return true;\n\n  const host = c.req.header("Host") || "";\n  if (host.includes("localhost") || host.includes("127.0.0.1")) return true;\n\n  return false;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!isOwnerLike(c)) {\n    return new Response("401 Unauthorized - You must be logged into Zo Space as the owner to view datasets.", { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  const targetUrl = new URL(c.req.url);\n  const proxyPath = targetUrl.pathname.replace(/^\\/api\\/datasets\\/proxy/, "") || "/";\n  targetUrl.protocol = "http:";\n  targetUrl.hostname = "127.0.0.1";\n  targetUrl.port = "8003";\n  targetUrl.pathname = proxyPath;\n\n  const reqHeaders = new Headers(c.req.raw.headers);\n  reqHeaders.delete("host");\n  reqHeaders.delete("cookie");\n\n  const init: RequestInit = {\n    method: c.req.method,\n    headers: reqHeaders,\n    redirect: "manual"\n  };\n\n  if (c.req.method !== "GET" && c.req.method !== "HEAD") {\n    init.body = await c.req.arrayBuffer();\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const proxyRes = await fetch(targetUrl.toString(), init);\n    const headers = new Headers(proxyRes.headers);\n    headers.delete("x-frame-options");\n    headers.delete("content-security-policy");\n    headers.delete("content-security-policy-report-only");\n    return new Response(proxyRes.body, { status: proxyRes.status, headers });\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    return new Response(`502 Bad Gateway - Datasette is not running or unreachable: ${e.message}`, { status: 502 });\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/failures` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";\nimport { execSync } from "node:child_process";\n\nconst LOG_DIR = "/dev/shm";\n\ninterface Failure {\n  time: string;\n  severity: "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low";\n  category: string;\n  source: string;\n  message: string;\n}\n\nfunction tailFile(path: string, maxLines: number): string[] {\n  try {\n    const content = readFileSync(path, "utf8");\n    const lines = content.split("\\n").filter(l => l.trim());\n    return lines.slice(-maxLines);\n  } catch { return []; }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const failures: Failure[] = [];\n  const categoryCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\n  const severityCounts = { critical: 0, high: 0, medium: 0, low: 0 };\n\n  // 1. Parse proxy logs for 5xx errors\n  const proxyLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/zosite-3099-proxy.log`, 500);\n  for (const line of proxyLines) {\n    const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z)\\s+(GET|POST|PUT|DELETE|PATCH|OPTIONS|HEAD)\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(\\d+)\\s+(\\d+)ms$/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const status = parseInt(m[4]);\n    if (status >= 500) {\n      const severity = status >= 503 ? "critical" : "high";\n      failures.push({ time: m[1], severity, category: "Network", source: "proxy", message: `${m[2]} ${m[3]} returned ${status} (${m[5]}ms)` });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 2. Parse supervisord for crashes\n  const supLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/supervisord.log`, 200);\n  for (const line of supLines) {\n    const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s+[\\d:,]+)\\s+(INFO|WARN|ERROR|CRIT)\\s+(.+)$/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const msg = m[3].trim();\n    const time = new Date(m[1].replace(",", ".")).toISOString();\n    \n    if (msg.includes("FATAL") || (msg.includes("exited:") && msg.includes("not expected"))) {\n      failures.push({ time, severity: "critical", category: "Service", source: "supervisord", message: msg });\n    } else if (msg.includes("stopped:") && msg.includes("SIGKILL")) {\n      failures.push({ time, severity: "high", category: "Service", source: "supervisord", message: msg });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 3. Parse all error logs\n  try {\n    const files = readdirSync(LOG_DIR);\n    for (const f of files) {\n      if (!f.endsWith("_err.log")) continue;\n      const filePath = `${LOG_DIR}/${f}`;\n      const stat = statSync(filePath);\n      if (stat.size === 0) continue;\n      \n      const source = f.replace("_err.log", "");\n      const lines = tailFile(filePath, 50);\n      \n      for (const line of lines) {\n        const lower = line.toLowerCase();\n        if (!lower.includes("error") && !lower.includes("fatal") && !lower.includes("exception") && !lower.includes("⨯") && !lower.includes("panic") && !lower.includes("failed") && !lower.includes("crash")) continue;\n        \n        const timeMatch = line.match(/(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z?)/);\n        const time = timeMatch?.[1] || new Date().toISOString();\n        \n        let severity: "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low" = "medium";\n        let category = "Unknown";\n        \n        if (lower.includes("fatal") || lower.includes("panic") || lower.includes("crash")) {\n          severity = "critical"; category = "Compute";\n        } else if (lower.includes("econnrefused") || lower.includes("timeout") || lower.includes("502") || lower.includes("503")) {\n          severity = "high"; category = "Network";\n        } else if (lower.includes("auth") || lower.includes("unauthorized") || lower.includes("forbidden")) {\n          severity = "high"; category = "Auth";\n        } else if (lower.includes("disk") || lower.includes("storage") || lower.includes("no space")) {\n          severity = "high"; category = "Storage";\n        } else if (lower.includes("error") || lower.includes("⨯")) {\n          severity = "medium"; category = "Service";\n        } else {\n          severity = "low"; category = "Unknown";\n        }\n        \n        failures.push({ time, severity, category, source, message: line.slice(0, 400) });\n      }\n    }\n  } catch {}\n\n  // 4. Check zo-space build errors (via Loki recent errors)\n  try {\n    const lokiResult = execSync(\n      `curl -s -G "http://localhost:3100/loki/api/v1/query_range" --data-urlencode \
```

### `/api/files` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readdir, stat } from "fs/promises";\nimport { join, extname } from "path";\n\nconst WORKSPACE = "/home/workspace";\nconst BLOCKED = new Set(["Trash", ".git", "node_modules", ".cache", ".z"]);\n\nfunction isAuthenticated(c: Context): boolean {\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser) return true;\n  \n  const auth = c.req.header("Authorization") || "";\n  if (auth.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = auth.slice(7);\n    const validToken = process.env.ZO_API_KEY;\n    if (validToken && token === validToken) return true;\n  }\n  \n  const cookie = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  if (cookie.includes("zo_session=") || cookie.includes("auth_token=")) return true;\n  \n  const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n  if (referer.includes("/share") || referer.includes("localhost")) return true;\n  \n  const host = c.req.header("Host") || "";\n  if (host.includes("localhost")) return true;\n\n  return false;\n}\n\nasync function listDir(dirPath: string): Promise<any[]> {\n  const entries = await readdir(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true });\n  const results: any[] = [];\n\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    if (BLOCKED.has(entry.name) || entry.name.startsWith(".")) continue;\n    const fullPath = join(dirPath, entry.name);\n    const relativePath = fullPath.replace(WORKSPACE + "/", "");\n    const isDir = entry.isDirectory();\n\n    const item: any = {\n      name: entry.name,\n      path: relativePath,\n      isDir,\n    };\n\n    if (!isDir) {\n      try {\n        const s = await stat(fullPath);\n        item.size = s.size;\n        item.modified = s.mtime.toISOString();\n      } catch {}\n    }\n\n    results.push(item);\n  }\n\n  results.sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.isDir !== b.isDir) return a.isDir ? -1 : 1;\n    return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);\n  });\n\n  return results;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!isAuthenticated(c)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const dir = c.req.query("dir") || "";\n  const targetPath = dir ? join(WORKSPACE, dir) : WORKSPACE;\n\n  if (!targetPath.startsWith(WORKSPACE)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Invalid path" }, 403);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const items = await listDir(targetPath);\n    return c.json({ path: dir || "/", items });\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    return c.json({ error: e.message }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync } from "fs";\n\nconst DATA_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/flowpulse";\n\nfunction readJsonl(file: string): any[] {\n  const p = `${DATA_DIR}/${file}`;\n  if (!existsSync(p)) return [];\n  const raw = readFileSync(p, "utf-8").trim();\n  if (!raw) return [];\n  return raw.split("\\n").filter(Boolean).map((l: string) => JSON.parse(l));\n}\n\nfunction readJson(file: string): any {\n  const p = `${DATA_DIR}/${file}`;\n  if (!existsSync(p)) return null;\n  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, "utf-8"));\n}\n\nfunction sinceFilter(entries: any[], hours: number): any[] {\n  const cutoff = Date.now() - hours * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n  return entries.filter((e: any) => new Date(e.triggered_at || e.created_at).getTime() >= cutoff);\n}\n\nfunction computeAgentStatus(agent: any, runs: any[]): any {\n  const agentRuns = runs.filter((r: any) => r.agent_id === agent.agent_id);\n  const sorted = [...agentRuns].sort((a: any, b: any) => new Date(b.triggered_at).getTime() - new Date(a.triggered_at).getTime());\n  const lastRun = sorted[0] || null;\n  const recent7d = sinceFilter(agentRuns, 168);\n  const failures7d = recent7d.filter((r: any) => r.status === "failure" || r.status === "timeout");\n  const failRate = recent7d.length > 0 ? failures7d.length / recent7d.length : 0;\n\n  let consecutiveFailures = 0;\n  for (const run of sorted) {\n    if (run.status === "failure" || run.status === "timeout") consecutiveFailures++;\n    else break;\n  }\n\n  const silenceHours = lastRun ? (Date.now() - new Date(lastRun.triggered_at).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60) : null;\n\n  let status = "unknown";\n  if (!lastRun) status = "no_data";\n  else if (consecutiveFailures >= 3) status = "failing";\n  else if (silenceHours !== null && silenceHours > agent.silence_threshold_hours) status = "silent";\n  else if (failRate > 0.2) status = "unstable";\n  else status = "healthy";\n\n  return {\n    agent_id: agent.agent_id,\n    agent_name: agent.agent_name,\n    description: agent.description,\n    status,\n    last_run: lastRun?.triggered_at ?? null,\n    last_status: lastRun?.status ?? null,\n    last_duration_ms: lastRun?.duration_ms ?? null,\n    last_error: lastRun?.error_message ?? null,\n    silence_threshold_hours: agent.silence_threshold_hours,\n    silence_hours: silenceHours ? Math.round(silenceHours * 10) / 10 : null,\n    critical: agent.critical,\n    enabled: agent.enabled,\n    failure_rate_7d: Math.round(failRate * 1000) / 10,\n    total_runs_7d: recent7d.length,\n    consecutive_failures: consecutiveFailures,\n  };\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  // Page is private (auth handled by Zo), API data is no more sensitive than the page\n  // Keep auth for automation (Bearer token) but don\
```

### `/api/logs` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";\n\nconst LOG_DIR = "/dev/shm";\n\ninterface LogLine {\n  time: string;\n  level: string;\n  source: string;\n  message: string;\n  pid?: string;\n}\n\nfunction parseProxyLog(line: string): LogLine | null {\n  const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z)\\s+(GET|POST|PUT|DELETE|PATCH|OPTIONS|HEAD)\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(\\d+)\\s+(\\d+)ms$/);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const status = parseInt(m[4]);\n  const level = status >= 500 ? "ERROR" : status >= 400 ? "WARN" : "INFO";\n  return { time: m[1], level, source: "proxy", message: `${m[2]} ${m[3]} ${m[4]} ${m[5]}ms` };\n}\n\nfunction parseSupervisordLog(line: string): LogLine | null {\n  const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s+[\\d:,]+)\\s+(INFO|WARN|ERROR|CRIT|DEBUG)\\s+(.+)$/);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  return { time: new Date(m[1].replace(",", ".")).toISOString(), level: m[2] === "CRIT" ? "ERROR" : m[2], source: "supervisord", message: m[3].trim() };\n}\n\nfunction parseGenericLog(line: string, source: string): LogLine | null {\n  if (!line.trim()) return null;\n  let level = "INFO";\n  const lower = line.toLowerCase();\n  if (lower.includes("error") || lower.includes("⨯") || lower.includes("fatal") || lower.includes("exception")) level = "ERROR";\n  else if (lower.includes("warn") || lower.includes("warning")) level = "WARN";\n  else if (lower.includes("debug")) level = "DEBUG";\n  const timeMatch = line.match(/(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z?)/);\n  const time = timeMatch ? timeMatch[1] : new Date().toISOString();\n  return { time, level, source, message: line.slice(0, 500) };\n}\n\nfunction tailFile(path: string, maxLines: number): string[] {\n  try {\n    const content = readFileSync(path, "utf8");\n    const lines = content.split("\\n").filter(l => l.trim());\n    return lines.slice(-maxLines);\n  } catch { return []; }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const limit = Math.min(parseInt(c.req.query("limit") || "100"), 500);\n  const sourceFilter = c.req.query("source") || "";\n  const levelFilter = c.req.query("level") || "";\n\n  const allLines: LogLine[] = [];\n\n  // Parse proxy logs\n  const proxyLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/zosite-3099-proxy.log`, 200);\n  for (const line of proxyLines) {\n    const parsed = parseProxyLog(line);\n    if (parsed) allLines.push(parsed);\n  }\n\n  // Parse supervisord logs\n  const supLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/supervisord.log`, 100);\n  for (const line of supLines) {\n    const parsed = parseSupervisordLog(line);\n    if (parsed) allLines.push(parsed);\n  }\n\n  // Parse service logs\n  const logFiles: { file: string; source: string }[] = [];\n  try {\n    const files = readdirSync(LOG_DIR);\n    for (const f of files) {\n      if (!f.endsWith(".log")) continue;\n      if (f === "supervisord.log" || f.startsWith("zosite-3099-proxy")) continue;\n      const source = f.replace(".log", "").replace("_err", " (stderr)");\n      logFiles.push({ file: `${LOG_DIR}/${f}`, source });\n    }\n  } catch {}\n\n  for (const { file, source } of logFiles) {\n    try {\n      const stat = statSync(file);\n      if (stat.size === 0) continue;\n      const lines = tailFile(file, 30);\n      for (const line of lines) {\n        const parsed = parseGenericLog(line, source);\n        if (parsed) allLines.push(parsed);\n      }\n    } catch {}\n  }\n\n  // Sort by time descending\n  allLines.sort((a, b) => b.time.localeCompare(a.time));\n\n  // Apply filters\n  let filtered = allLines;\n  if (sourceFilter) filtered = filtered.filter(l => l.source.toLowerCase().includes(sourceFilter.toLowerCase()));\n  if (levelFilter) filtered = filtered.filter(l => l.level === levelFilter.toUpperCase());\n\n  const lines = filtered.slice(0, limit);\n\n  // Compute stats\n  const sources = [...new Set(allLines.map(l => l.source))];\n  const warnings = allLines.filter(l => l.level === "WARN").length;\n  const errors = allLines.filter(l => l.level === "ERROR").length;\n  const infos = allLines.filter(l => l.level === "INFO").length;\n\n  // Source breakdown\n  const sourceBreakdown: Record<string, { total: number; errors: number; warnings: number }> = {};\n  for (const l of allLines) {\n    if (!sourceBreakdown[l.source]) sourceBreakdown[l.source] = { total: 0, errors: 0, warnings: 0 };\n    sourceBreakdown[l.source].total++;\n    if (l.level === "ERROR") sourceBreakdown[l.source].errors++;\n    if (l.level === "WARN") sourceBreakdown[l.source].warnings++;\n  }\n\n  return c.json({\n    lines,\n    stats: {\n      totalLines: allLines.length,\n      displayedLines: lines.length,\n      sources,\n      sourceCount: sources.length,\n      warnings,\n      errors,\n      infos,\n      sourceBreakdown,\n    },\n    data_source: "LIVE",\n    last_updated: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n};
```

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const token = process.env.ZO_CLIENT_IDENTITY_TOKEN || process.env.ZO_API_KEY;\n  if (!token) return c.json({ error: "No token" }, 500);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch("https://api.zo.computer/models/available", {\n      headers: {\n        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,\n        "Content-Type": "application/json",\n        Accept: "application/json",\n      },\n    });\n    if (!resp.ok) return c.json({ error: `Zo API ${resp.status}` }, resp.status);\n\n    const data = await resp.json();\n    const models = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.models ?? data?.data ?? [];\n\n    const configured = models.map((m: Record<string, unknown>) => ({\n      modelName: m.model_name ?? m.name ?? "",\n      label: m.label ?? m.model_name ?? m.name ?? "",\n      provider: m.provider ?? "",\n      isByok: m.is_byok ?? false,\n    }));\n\n    return c.json({ models: configured });\n  } catch (err) {\n    return c.json({ error: String(err) }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/nav-links` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync } from "node:fs";\n\ninterface NavLink {\n  name: string;\n  path: string;\n  description: string;\n  category: "public" | "private";\n  icon?: string;\n}\n\ninterface NavConfig {\n  public_links: NavLink[];\n  private_links: NavLink[];\n  hidden_links?: string[];\n}\n\nconst CONFIG_PATH = "/home/workspace/ZoSpace/nav-config.json";\n\nfunction loadNavConfig(): NavConfig {\n  try {\n    const content = readFileSync(CONFIG_PATH, "utf-8");\n    // Remove JSON comments before parsing\n    const cleaned = content.replace(/\\/\\/.*$/gm, "").replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, "");\n    return JSON.parse(cleaned);\n  } catch {\n    // Fallback to defaults if config file is missing or invalid\n    return {\n      public_links: [],\n      private_links: [],\n      hidden_links: []\n    };\n  }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const config = loadNavConfig();\n  const hiddenSet = new Set(config.hidden_links || []);\n  \n  // 1. Check authentication status\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || \n                     cookieHeader.includes("auth_token") ||\n                     cookieHeader.includes("x-zo-session");\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  const isAuthenticated = !!zoUser || hasSession;\n\n  // 2. Filter out hidden links and add category\n  const publicLinks = (config.public_links || [])\n    .filter(link => !hiddenSet.has(link.path))\n    .map(link => ({ ...link, category: "public" as const }));\n    \n  // 3. Only include private links if authenticated\n  const privateLinks = isAuthenticated \n    ? (config.private_links || [])\n        .filter(link => !hiddenSet.has(link.path))\n        .map(link => ({ ...link, category: "private" as const }))\n    : [];\n\n  return c.json({\n    authenticated: isAuthenticated,\n    links: [...publicLinks, ...privateLinks],\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/security` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";\n\nconst LOG_DIR = "/dev/shm";\n\ninterface SecurityEvent {\n  time: string;\n  type: "auth" | "rejected" | "api_call" | "system";\n  severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";\n  source: string;\n  message: string;\n}\n\nfunction tailFile(path: string, maxLines: number): string[] {\n  try {\n    const content = readFileSync(path, "utf8");\n    const lines = content.split("\\n").filter(l => l.trim());\n    return lines.slice(-maxLines);\n  } catch { return []; }\n}\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const events: SecurityEvent[] = [];\n\n  // 1. Parse proxy logs for HTTP status codes (401, 403, 500+)\n  const proxyLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/zosite-3099-proxy.log`, 500);\n  let totalApiCalls = 0;\n  let rejectedCount = 0;\n  let authFailures = 0;\n  let serverErrors = 0;\n  const statusCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\n  const pathCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\n  const recentRequests: { time: string; method: string; path: string; status: number; duration: number }[] = [];\n\n  for (const line of proxyLines) {\n    const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z)\\s+(GET|POST|PUT|DELETE|PATCH|OPTIONS|HEAD)\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(\\d+)\\s+(\\d+)ms$/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    totalApiCalls++;\n    const [, time, method, path, statusStr, durationStr] = m;\n    const status = parseInt(statusStr);\n    const duration = parseInt(durationStr);\n    const statusBucket = `${Math.floor(status / 100)}xx`;\n    statusCounts[statusBucket] = (statusCounts[statusBucket] || 0) + 1;\n    \n    const pathBase = path.split("?")[0];\n    pathCounts[pathBase] = (pathCounts[pathBase] || 0) + 1;\n\n    recentRequests.push({ time, method, path, status, duration });\n\n    if (status === 401 || status === 403) {\n      authFailures++;\n      rejectedCount++;\n      events.push({\n        time, type: "rejected", severity: "warning",\n        source: "proxy", message: `${method} ${path} returned ${status}`\n      });\n    } else if (status >= 500) {\n      serverErrors++;\n      events.push({\n        time, type: "system", severity: "critical",\n        source: "proxy", message: `Server error: ${method} ${path} returned ${status}`\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 2. Parse supervisord for crash/restart events\n  const supLines = tailFile(`${LOG_DIR}/supervisord.log`, 200);\n  let processRestarts = 0;\n  let processCrashes = 0;\n\n  for (const line of supLines) {\n    const m = line.match(/^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s+[\\d:,]+)\\s+(INFO|WARN|ERROR|CRIT)\\s+(.+)$/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const [, timeRaw, level, msg] = m;\n    const time = new Date(timeRaw.replace(",", ".")).toISOString();\n\n    if (msg.includes("stopped:") || msg.includes("exited:")) {\n      processRestarts++;\n      const severity = msg.includes("FATAL") ? "critical" : "info";\n      events.push({ time, type: "system", severity, source: "supervisord", message: msg.trim() });\n    }\n    if (msg.includes("FATAL") || msg.includes("exited: ") && msg.includes("not expected")) {\n      processCrashes++;\n      events.push({ time, type: "system", severity: "critical", source: "supervisord", message: msg.trim() });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 3. Scan error logs for security-relevant patterns\n  try {\n    const files = readdirSync(LOG_DIR);\n    for (const f of files) {\n      if (!f.endsWith("_err.log")) continue;\n      const path = `${LOG_DIR}/${f}`;\n      const stat = statSync(path);\n      if (stat.size === 0) continue;\n      const lines = tailFile(path, 50);\n      const source = f.replace("_err.log", "");\n      for (const line of lines) {\n        const lower = line.toLowerCase();\n        if (lower.includes("unauthorized") || lower.includes("forbidden") || lower.includes("auth") && lower.includes("fail")) {\n          const timeMatch = line.match(/(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T[\\d:.]+Z?)/);\n          events.push({\n            time: timeMatch?.[1] || new Date().toISOString(),\n            type: "auth", severity: "warning", source,\n            message: line.slice(0, 300)\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  } catch {}\n\n  // Sort events by time descending\n  events.sort((a, b) => b.time.localeCompare(a.time));\n\n  // Top endpoints by request volume\n  const topEndpoints = Object.entries(pathCounts)\n    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])\n    .slice(0, 10)\n    .map(([path, count]) => ({ path, count }));\n\n  // Slow requests (>500ms)\n  const slowRequests = recentRequests\n    .filter(r => r.duration > 500)\n    .sort((a, b) => b.duration - a.duration)\n    .slice(0, 10);\n\n  return c.json({\n    events: events.slice(0, 100),\n    stats: {\n      totalApiCalls,\n      authFailures,\n      rejectedEvents: rejectedCount,\n      serverErrors,\n      processRestarts,\n      processCrashes,\n      statusCounts,\n      topEndpoints,\n      slowRequests,\n    },\n    posture: {\n      overall: rejectedCount === 0 && serverErrors === 0 ? "Stable" : serverErrors > 0 ? "Elevated" : "Guarded",\n      authState: authFailures === 0 ? "Clear" : authFailures < 5 ? "Guarded" : "Alert",\n      interfaceState: serverErrors === 0 ? "Healthy" : "Degraded",\n      auditState: events.length === 0 ? "Quiet" : "Active",\n    },\n    data_source: "LIVE",\n    last_updated: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n};
```

"crypto";\n\nconst WORKSPACE = "/home/workspace";\nconst SHARES_FILE = "/home/workspace/Data/shares.json";\nconst SHARED_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/shared-files";\n\nfunction isAuthenticated(c: originalPath: string;\n  storedPath: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  fileSize: number;\n  createdAt: string;\n  requireLead: boolean;\n  allowPreview: boolean;\n  downloads: number;\n  leads: Lead[];\n}\n\nasync function loadShares(): Promise<Share[]> {\n  try {\n    const data = await readFile(SHARES_FILE, "utf-8");\n    return JSON.parse(data);\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nasync function saveShares(shares: Share[]) {\n  await writeFile(SHARES_FILE, JSON.stringify(shares, null, 2));\n}\n\nfunction getMimeType(ext: string): string {\n  const mimes: Record<string, "application/vnd.ms-excel",\n    ".xlsx": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",\n    ".ppt": "application/vnd.ms-powerpoint",\n    ".pptx": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",\n    ".png": "image/png",\n    ".jpg": "image/jpeg",\n    ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",\n    ".gif": "image/gif",\n    ".svg": "image/svg+xml",\n    ".webp": "image/webp",\n    ".mp4": "video/mp4",\n    ".mp3": "audio/mpeg",\n    ".wav": "audio/wav",\n    ".zip": "application/zip",\n    ".gz": "application/gzip",\n    ".tar": "application/x-tar",\n    ".csv": "text/csv",\n    ".json": "application/json",\n    ".txt": "text/plain",\n    ".md": "text/markdown",\n    ".html": "text/html",\n    ".css": "text/css",\n    ".js": "text/javascript",\n    ".ts": "text/typescript",\n    ".py": "text/x-python",\n  };\n  return mimes[ext.toLowerCase()] || "application/octet-stream";\n}\n\nfunction formatSize(bytes: number): string {\n  if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + " B";\n  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(1) + " KB";\n  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + " MB";\n  return (bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + " GB";\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!isAuthenticated(c)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const method = c.req.method;\n\n  if (method === "POST") {\n    try {\n      const body = await c.req.json();\n      const { filePath, requireLead = true, allowPreview = true } = body;\n\n      if (!filePath) return c.json({ error: "filePath is required" }, 400);\n\n      const fullPath = join(WORKSPACE, filePath);\n      if (!fullPath.startsWith(WORKSPACE)) return c.json({ error: "Invalid path" }, 403);\n\n      const fileStat = await stat(fullPath);\n      if (!fileStat.isFile()) return c.json({ error: "Not a file" }, 400);\n\n      const id = randomBytes(6).toString("hex");\n      const ext = extname(filePath);\n      const storedName = id + ext;\n\n      await mkdir(SHARED_DIR, { recursive: true });\n      await copyFile(fullPath, join(SHARED_DIR, storedName));\n\n      const share: Share = {\n        id,\n        fileName: basename(filePath),\n        originalPath: filePath,\n        storedPath: storedName,\n        mimeType: getMimeType(ext),\n        fileSize: fileStat.size,\n        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        requireLead,\n        allowPreview,\n        downloads: 0,\n        leads: [],\n      };\n\n      const shares = await loadShares();\n      shares.push(share);\n      await saveShares(shares);\n\n      return c.json({\n        id: share.id,\n        url: `https://curtastrophe.zo.space/s/${share.id}`,\n        fileName: share.fileName,\n        fileSize: formatSize(share.fileSize),\n      });\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      return c.json({ error: e.message }, 500);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (method === "GET") {\n    const shares = await loadShares();\n    return c.json(shares.map(s => ({\n      id: s.id,\n      fileName: s.fileName,\n      originalPath: s.originalPath,\n      fileSize: formatSize(s.fileSize),\n      createdAt: s.createdAt,\n      downloads: s.downloads,\n      leadCount: s.leads.length,\n      requireLead: s.requireLead,\n      url: `https://curtastrophe.zo.space/s/${s.id}`,\n    })));\n  }\n\n  return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n};

### `/api/share/:id` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFile, writeFile } from "fs/promises";\nimport { join } from "path";\n\nconst SHARES_FILE = "/home/workspace/Data/shares.json";\nconst SHARED_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/shared-files";\n\nfunction isAuthenticated(c: Context): boolean {\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser === "curtastrophe") return true;\n\n  const auth = c.req.header("Authorization") || "";\n  if (auth.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = auth.slice(7);\n    const validToken = process.env.ZO_API_KEY;\n    if (validToken && token === validToken) return true;\n  }\n\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || \n                     cookieHeader.includes("auth_token") ||\n                     cookieHeader.includes("x-zo-session");\n  \n  return hasSession;\n}\n\ninterface Lead {\n  name: string;\n  email: string;\n  timestamp: string;\n  ip?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Share {\n  id: string;\n  fileName: string;\n  originalPath: string;\n  storedPath: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  fileSize: number;\n  createdAt: string;\n  requireLead: boolean;\n  allowPreview: boolean;\n  downloads: number;\n  leads: Lead[];\n}\n\nasync function loadShares(): Promise<Share[]> {\n  try {\n    const data = await readFile(SHARES_FILE, "utf-8");\n    return JSON.parse(data);\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nasync function saveShares(shares: Share[]) {\n  await writeFile(SHARES_FILE, JSON.stringify(shares, null, 2));\n}\n\nfunction formatSize(bytes: number): string {\n  if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + " B";\n  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(1) + " KB";\n  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + " MB";\n  return (bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + " GB";\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const id = c.req.param("id");\n  const method = c.req.method;\n\n  const shares = await loadShares();\n  const share = shares.find(s => s.id === id);\n  if (!share) return c.json({ error: "Share not found" }, 404);\n\n  if (method === "GET") {\n    // Public: used by /s/:id download page\n    return c.json({\n      id: share.id,\n      fileName: share.fileName,\n      mimeType: share.mimeType,\n      fileSize: formatSize(share.fileSize),\n      fileSizeBytes: share.fileSize,\n      createdAt: share.createdAt,\n      requireLead: share.requireLead,\n      allowPreview: share.allowPreview,\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (method === "DELETE") {\n    // Admin-only: require authentication\n    if (!isAuthenticated(c)) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n    }\n    try {\n      const { unlink } = await import("fs/promises");\n      await unlink(join(SHARED_DIR, share.storedPath));\n    } catch {}\n    const idx = shares.indexOf(share);\n    shares.splice(idx, 1);\n    await saveShares(shares);\n    return c.json({ deleted: true });\n  }\n\n  return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n};
```

### `/api/share/:id/download` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFile, writeFile } from "fs/promises";\nimport { join } from "path";\n\nconst SHARES_FILE = "/home/workspace/Data/shares.json";\nconst SHARED_DIR = "/home/workspace/Data/shared-files";\n\ninterface Lead {\n  name: string;\n  email: string;\n  timestamp: string;\n  ip?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Share {\n  id: string;\n  fileName: string;\n  originalPath: string;\n  storedPath: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  fileSize: number;\n  createdAt: string;\n  requireLead: boolean;\n  allowPreview: boolean;\n  downloads: number;\n  leads: Lead[];\n}\n\nasync function loadShares(): Promise<Share[]> {\n  try {\n    const data = await readFile(SHARES_FILE, "utf-8");\n    return JSON.parse(data);\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nasync function saveShares(shares: Share[]) {\n  await writeFile(SHARES_FILE, JSON.stringify(shares, null, 2));\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (c.req.method !== "POST") return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n\n  const id = c.req.param("id");\n  const shares = await loadShares();\n  const share = shares.find(s => s.id === id);\n  if (!share) return c.json({ error: "Share not found" }, 404);\n\n  if (share.requireLead) {\n    const body = await c.req.json();\n    const { name, email } = body;\n    if (!name || !email) return c.json({ error: "Name and email are required" }, 400);\n\n    share.leads.push({\n      name,\n      email,\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      ip: c.req.header("x-forwarded-for") || c.req.header("x-real-ip") || "unknown",\n    });\n  }\n\n  share.downloads++;\n  await saveShares(shares);\n\n  const filePath = join(SHARED_DIR, share.storedPath);\n  const fileData = await readFile(filePath);\n\n  return new Response(fileData, {\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": share.mimeType,\n      "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="${share.fileName}"`,\n      "Content-Length": String(share.fileSize),\n    },\n  });\n};
```

return true;\n  const auth = c.req.header("authorization");\n  if (auth?.startsWith("Bearer ") && process.env.ZO_API_KEY && auth.slice(7) === process.env.ZO_API_KEY) return true;\n  return (!s.startsWith("---\\n")) return { data: {}, content: raw };\n  const end = s.indexOf("\\n---\\n", 4);\n  if (end === -1) return { data: {}, content: raw };\n  const fmBlock = s.slice(4, end);\n  const content = s.slice(end + 5);\n  const data: Record<string, any> = {};\n\n  let currentKey = "";\n  let inArray = false;\n  let inNestedObj = "";\n  const nestedData: Record<string, Record<string, && !inArray) {\n      inNestedObj = nestedMatch[1];\n      nestedData[inNestedObj] = nestedData[inNestedObj] || {};\n      currentKey = "";\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Nested key-value (e.g. "  author: foo")\n    if (inNestedObj && line.match(/^\\s{2,}/)) {\n      const kvMatch = line.match(/^\\s+([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*):\\s*(.+)$/);\n      if (kvMatch) {\n        nestedData[inNestedObj][kvMatch[1]] = parseYamlValue(kvMatch[2].trim());\n        continue;\n      }\n      // Nested array items\n      const arrMatch = line.match(/^\\s+-\\s*(.+)$/);\n      if (arrMatch && currentKey) {\n        const arr = nestedData[inNestedObj][currentKey];\n        if (Array.isArray(arr)) arr.push(stripQuotes(arrMatch[1].trim()));\n        continue;\n      }\n      // nestedData[inNestedObj][currentKey] = [];\n        continue;\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    inNestedObj = "";\n\n    // Top-level array item\n    if (inArray && line.match(/^\\s*-\\s/)) {\n      const val = line.replace(/^\\s*-\\s*/, "").trim();\n      if (Array.isArray(data[currentKey])) {\n        data[currentKey].push(stripQuotes(val));\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (inArray && !line.match(/^\\s*-\\s/) && line.trim() !== "") {\n      inArray = false;\n    }\n\n    // Top-level key-value\n    const kvMatch = line.match(/^([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*):\\s*(.*)$/);\n    if (kvMatch) {\n      const key = kvMatch[1];\n      const rawVal = kvMatch[2].trim();\n      currentKey = key;\n\n      if (rawVal === "") {\n        // Could be start of array or nested object\n v] of Object.entries(nestedData)) {\n    data[k] = v;\n  }\n\n  return { data, content };\n}\n\nfunction parseYamlValue(raw: string): any {\n  // Inline array: ["a", "b", "c"]\n  if import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const secret = process.env.SPEECH_GAME_PASSCODE;\n  if (!secret) return c.json({ error: "Passcode not configured. Set SPEECH_GAME_PASSCODE in Zo Secrets." }, 500);\n  const passcode = c.req.header("X-Passcode") || "";\n  if (passcode === secret) return c.json({ authenticated: true });\n  return c.json({ error: "Invalid passcode" }, 401);\n};
