### `/api/speech-game-data` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";\n\n// Teable configuration\nconst TEABLE_BASE_URL = "https://app.teable.io/api";\nconst TEABLE_API_KEY = process.env.TEABLE_API_KEY;\nconst ATTEMPTS_TABLE = "tblpGWk0Womh812kwMA";\nconst STICKERS_TABLE = "tbl2y2Enec28jLbEceL";\n\ninterface Attempt {\n  id: string;\n  word: string;\n  phrase: string;\n  position: "first" | "second";\n  round: number;\n  tries: number;\n  date: string;\n  time: string;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\ninterface Store {\n  attempts: Attempt[];\n  stickers: Record<string, string>;\n}\n\n// Helper: make Teable API request\nasync function teableRequest(method: string, endpoint: string, body?: unknown) {\n  const url = `${TEABLE_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`;\n  const response = await fetch(url, {\n    method,\n    headers: {\n      "Authorization": `Bearer ${TEABLE_API_KEY}`,\n      "Content-Type": "application/json",\n    },\n    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,\n  });\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const text = await response.text();\n    throw new Error(`Teable API Error ${response.status}: ${text}`);\n  }\n  return response.json();\n}\n\n// Load attempts from Teable\nasync function loadAttempts(): Promise<Attempt[]> {\n  const data = await teableRequest("GET", `/table/${ATTEMPTS_TABLE}/record?take=1000`);\n  const records = ((data as any).records || []).filter((r: any) => r.fields.Name);\n  return records.map((r: any) => ({\n    id: `${r.fields.timestamp}-${r.id.slice(-6)}`,\n    word: r.fields.Name,\n    phrase: r.fields.phrase || "",\n    position: r.fields.position || "first",\n    round: r.fields.round || 1,\n    tries: r.fields.tries || 1,\n    date: r.fields.date ? r.fields.date.split("T")[0] : new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],\n    time: r.fields.time || "00:00:00",\n    timestamp: r.fields.timestamp || Date.now(),\n  }));\n}\n\n// Load stickers from Teable\nasync function loadStickerRecords() {\n  const data = await teableRequest("GET", `/table/${STICKERS_TABLE}/record?take=1000`);\n  return (data as any).records || [];\n}\n\nasync function loadStickers(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {\n  const records = await loadStickerRecords();\n  const stickers: Record<string, string> = {};\n  for (const r of records) {\n    const date = r.fields.date ? r.fields.date.split("T")[0] : "";\n    if (date && r.fields.emoji) {\n      stickers[date] = r.fields.emoji;\n    }\n  }\n  return stickers;\n}\n\n// Save attempt to Teable\nasync function saveAttempt(attempt: Attempt) {\n  await teableRequest("POST", `/table/${ATTEMPTS_TABLE}/record`, {\n    records: [{\n      fields: {\n        Name: attempt.word,\n        phrase: attempt.phrase,\n        position: attempt.position,\n        round: attempt.round,\n        tries: attempt.tries,\n        date: attempt.date,\n        timestamp: attempt.timestamp,\n      }\n    }],\n    typecast: true\n  });\n}\n\n// Save stickers to Teable\nasync function syncStickers(stickers: Record<string, string>) {\n  const existing = await loadStickerRecords();\n  const existingByDate = new Map<string, any>();\n  for (const record of existing) {\n    const date = record.fields.date ? record.fields.date.split("T")[0] : "";\n    if (date) existingByDate.set(date, record);\n  }\n\n  for (const [date, emoji] of Object.entries(stickers)) {\n    const record = existingByDate.get(date);\n    if (!record) {\n      await teableRequest("POST", `/table/${STICKERS_TABLE}/record`, {\n        records: [{\n          fields: {\n            date,\n            emoji,\n          }\n        }],\n        typecast: true\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (record.fields.emoji !== emoji) {\n      await teableRequest("PATCH", `/table/${STICKERS_TABLE}/record`, {\n        records: [{\n          id: record.id,\n          fields: { emoji }\n        }],\n        typecast: true\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  for (const [date, record] of existingByDate.entries()) {\n    if (!(date in stickers)) {\n      await teableRequest("DELETE", `/table/${STICKERS_TABLE}/record`, {\n        recordIds: [record.id]\n      });\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction checkAuth(c: Context): boolean {\n  const secret = process.env.SPEECH_GAME_PASSCODE;\n  if (!secret) return false;\n  \n  const auth = c.req.header("authorization");\n  if (auth?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = auth.slice(7);\n    const aBuf = Buffer.from(token);\n    const bBuf = Buffer.from(secret);\n    if (aBuf.length === bBuf.length && timingSafeEqual(aBuf, bBuf)) return true;\n  }\n  \n  const passcode = c.req.header("X-Passcode");\n  if (passcode) {\n    const aBuf = Buffer.from(passcode);\n    const bBuf = Buffer.from(secret);\n    if (aBuf.length === bBuf.length && timingSafeEqual(aBuf, bBuf)) return true;\n  }\n  \n  return false;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const method = c.req.method;\n\n  if (method === "GET") {\n    if (!checkAuth(c)) return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n    try {\n      const [attempts, stickers] = await Promise.all([loadAttempts(), loadStickers()]);\n      return c.json({ attempts, stickers });\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Failed to load data", message: e.message }, 500);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (method === "POST") {\n    if (!checkAuth(c)) return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n    const body = await c.req.json();\n\n    if (body?.type === "attempt" || body?.word) {\n      const attempt: Attempt = {\n        id: body.id || crypto.randomUUID(),\n        word: body.word,\n        phrase: body.phrase || "",\n        position: body.position || "first",\n        round: body.round || 1,\n        tries: body.tries || 1,\n        date: body.date || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],\n        time: body.time || new Date().toLocaleTimeString("en-CA", { hour12: false }),\n        timestamp: body.timestamp || Date.now(),\n      };\n      try {\n        await saveAttempt(attempt);\n        const attempts = await loadAttempts();\n        return c.json({ ok: true, totalAttempts: attempts.length });\n      } catch (e: any) {\n        return c.json({ error: "Failed to save attempt", message: e.message }, 500);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (body?.action === "saveStickers" && body.stickers) {\n      try {\n        await syncStickers(body.stickers);\n        const stickers = await loadStickers();\n        return c.json({ ok: true, stickerCount: Object.keys(stickers).length });\n      } catch (e: any) {\n        return c.json({ error: "Failed to save stickers", message: e.message }, 500);\n      }\n    }\n\n    return c.json({ error: "Unknown action" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  if (method === "DELETE") {\n    if (!checkAuth(c)) return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n    // Clear operations - Teable doesn\
```

### `/api/system-stats` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { execSync } from "node:child_process";\nimport { readFileSync } from "node:fs";\nfunction readMemInfo() {\n  const text = readFileSync("/proc/meminfo", "utf8");\n  const values: Record<string, number> = {};\n  for (const line of text.split("\\n")) {\n    const match = line.match(/^([^:]+):\\s+(\\d+)/);\n    if (match) values[match[1]] = Number(match[2]);\n  }\n  return values;\n}\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const mem = readMemInfo();\n  const total = (mem.MemTotal || 0) * 1024;\n  const available = (mem.MemAvailable || mem.MemFree || 0) * 1024;\n  const used = Math.max(0, total - available);\n  const diskRaw = execSync("df -B1 / | tail -1", { encoding: "utf8" }).trim().split(/\\s+/);\n  const diskTotal = Number(diskRaw[1] || 0);\n  const diskUsed = Number(diskRaw[2] || 0);\n  const diskAvail = Number(diskRaw[3] || 0);\n  const loadavg = readFileSync("/proc/loadavg", "utf8").trim().split(/\\s+/);\n  const cpuCount = Number(execSync("nproc", { encoding: "utf8" }).trim() || "1");\n  const load1 = Number(loadavg[0] || 0);\n  const uptimeText = readFileSync("/proc/uptime", "utf8").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0] || "0";\n  const uptimeSeconds = Number(uptimeText);\n  return c.json({\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n    memory: { total, used, available, percent: total ? Math.round((used / total) * 1000) / 10 : 0, totalGB: Math.round((total / 1024 / 1024 / 1024) * 100) / 100, usedGB: Math.round((used / 1024 / 1024 / 1024) * 100) / 100 },\n    disk: { total: diskTotal, used: diskUsed, available: diskAvail, percent: diskTotal ? Math.round((diskUsed / diskTotal) * 1000) / 10 : 0, totalGB: Math.round((diskTotal / 1024 / 1024 / 1024) * 100) / 100, usedGB: Math.round((diskUsed / 1024 / 1024 / 1024) * 100) / 100 },\n    cpu: { load1, load5: Number(loadavg[1] || 0), load15: Number(loadavg[2] || 0), cpuCount, percent: cpuCount ? Math.round((load1 / cpuCount) * 1000) / 10 : 0 },\n    uptime: { seconds: uptimeSeconds, hours: Math.round((uptimeSeconds / 3600) * 10) / 10, days: Math.round((uptimeSeconds / 86400) * 10) / 10 },\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/telemetry-data` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";\n\nconst SKILL_LOG = "/home/workspace/Data/skill-execution-logs/skill_executions.jsonl";\nconst WSS_LOG = "/dev/shm/wss.log";\nconst AGENT_META = "/home/workspace/Data/breadcrumb-telemetry/agent-metadata.json";\nconst TRACES_LOG = "/home/workspace/Data/breadcrumb-telemetry/traces.jsonl";\n\nfunction readJsonl(path: string): any[] {\n  if (!existsSync(path)) return [];\n  const raw = readFileSync(path, "utf-8").trim();\n  if (!raw) return [];\n  return raw.split("\\n").map(line => { try { return JSON.parse(line); } catch { return null; } }).filter(Boolean);\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const daysParam = parseInt(c.req.query("days") || "30");\n  const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - daysParam * 86400000).toISOString();\n\n  const skillLogs = readJsonl(SKILL_LOG).filter((e: any) => e.timestamp >= cutoff);\n  const traces = readJsonl(TRACES_LOG).filter((e: any) => e.timestamp >= cutoff);\n\n  const skillStats: Record<string, {runs:number;success:number;fail:number;totalMs:number;errors:string[]}> = {};\n  for (const entry of skillLogs) {\n    const name = entry.skill_name || "unknown";\n    if (!skillStats[name]) skillStats[name] = {runs:0,success:0,fail:0,totalMs:0,errors:[]};\n    skillStats[name].runs++;\n    if (entry.success) skillStats[name].success++; else {\n      skillStats[name].fail++;\n      if (entry.error_message) skillStats[name].errors.push(entry.error_message);\n    }\n    skillStats[name].totalMs += (entry.duration_ms || 0);\n  }\n  const skillList = Object.entries(skillStats).map(([name, s]) => ({\n    name, runs: s.runs, success: s.success, fail: s.fail,\n    successRate: s.runs > 0 ? Math.round((s.success / s.runs) * 100) : 0,\n    avgDurationMs: s.runs > 0 ? Math.round(s.totalMs / s.runs) : 0,\n    recentErrors: s.errors.slice(-3),\n  })).sort((a, b) => b.runs - a.runs);\n\n  const completions = traces.filter((t: any) => t.type === "completion");\n  const modelUsage: Record<string, number> = {};\n  let totalTokens = 0;\n  let totalCost = 0;\n\n  for (const t of completions) {\n    const model = t.model || "unknown";\n    modelUsage[model] = (modelUsage[model] || 0) + 1;\n    totalTokens += (t.totalTokens || 0);\n    totalCost += (t.estimatedCost || 0);\n  }\n\n  let agents: any[] = [];\n  if (existsSync(AGENT_META)) {\n    try { agents = JSON.parse(readFileSync(AGENT_META, "utf-8")).agents || []; } catch {}\n  }\n\n  let sessionActivity: {date:string;sessions:number}[] = [];\n  let totalSessions = 0;\n  if (existsSync(WSS_LOG)) {\n    try {\n      const wssContent = readFileSync(WSS_LOG, "utf-8");\n      const sessionIds = new Set<string>();\n      const dateSessions: Record<string, Set<string>> = {};\n      const sessionMatches = wssContent.match(/"sessionId":\\s*"([a-f0-9-]+)"/g) || [];\n      for (const m of sessionMatches) {\n        const sid = m.match(/"sessionId":\\s*"([a-f0-9-]+)"/)?.[1];\n        if (sid && !sessionIds.has(sid)) {\n          sessionIds.add(sid);\n          const idx = wssContent.indexOf(m);\n          const before = wssContent.substring(Math.max(0, idx - 100), idx);\n          const dateMatch = before.match(/(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})/);\n          const date = dateMatch ? dateMatch[1] : new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];\n          if (!dateSessions[date]) dateSessions[date] = new Set();\n          dateSessions[date].add(sid);\n        }\n      }\n      totalSessions = sessionIds.size;\n      sessionActivity = Object.entries(dateSessions)\n        .sort(([a], [b]) => b.localeCompare(a))\n        .slice(0, 30)\n        .map(([date, sids]) => ({ date, sessions: sids.size }));\n    } catch {}\n  }\n\n  const totalSkillRuns = skillLogs.length;\n  const totalSuccess = skillLogs.filter((e: any) => e.success).length;\n  const overallSuccessRate = totalSkillRuns > 0 ? Math.round((totalSuccess / totalSkillRuns) * 100) : 0;\n\n  return c.json({\n    skills: skillList,\n    agents,\n    sessionActivity,\n    totalSessions,\n    modelUsage,\n    summary: { \n      totalSkillRuns, \n      overallSuccessRate, \n      totalSessions, \n      topSkill: skillList[0]?.name || "none", \n      activeModels: Object.keys(modelUsage).length,\n      totalTokens,\n      totalCost: parseFloat(totalCost.toFixed(4))\n    }\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/temporal-auth-check` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\n// Check if user is authenticated via Zo session\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  // Check X-Zo-User header (set by Zo for authenticated requests)\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser === "curtastrophe") {\n    return c.json({ authenticated: true, method: "zo_user" });\n  }\n  \n  // Check for Zo session cookie\n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || \n                     cookieHeader.includes("auth_token");\n  \n  if (hasSession) {\n    return c.json({ authenticated: true, method: "session" });\n  }\n  \n  // Check Referer - if from authenticated Zo page, allow\n  const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n  if (referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.space") || referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.computer")) {\n    return c.json({ authenticated: true, method: "referer" });\n  }\n  \n  // Check for Bearer token (API access)\n  const authHeader = c.req.header("Authorization");\n  if (authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n    if (process.env.ZO_API_KEY && token === process.env.ZO_API_KEY) {\n      return c.json({ authenticated: true, method: "api_key" });\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return c.json({ authenticated: false, error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n};\n
```

### `/api/temporal/*` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\n// Proxy to internal Temporal gRPC endpoint\n// Requires authentication via session check\n\nconst TEMPORAL_INTERNAL = "http://127.0.0.1:7233";\n\nasync function checkAuth(c: Context): Promise<boolean> {\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  if (zoUser === "curtastrophe") return true;\n  \n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  const hasSession = cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || cookieHeader.includes("auth_token");\n  \n  if (hasSession) {\n    const referer = c.req.header("Referer") || "";\n    if (referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.space") || referer.includes("curtastrophe.zo.computer")) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const authHeader = c.req.header("Authorization");\n  if (authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n    if (process.env.ZO_API_KEY && token === process.env.ZO_API_KEY) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return false;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (!await checkAuth(c)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);\n  }\n  \n  const path = c.req.path.replace("/api/temporal", "");\n  const url = new URL(path || "/", TEMPORAL_INTERNAL);\n  \n  // Forward the request to internal Temporal\n  const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {\n    method: c.req.method,\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": c.req.header("Content-Type") || "application/json",\n    },\n    body: c.req.method !== "GET" ? await c.req.text() : undefined,\n  });\n  \n  return response;\n};\n
```

### `/api/trivia/by-date` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db";\n\nfunction getTodayMST(): string {\n  const date = new Date();\n  const options = { timeZone: "America/Edmonton", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" } as const;\n  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", options);\n  const parts = formatter.formatToParts(date);\n  const year = parts.find(p => p.type === "year")?.value;\n  const month = parts.find(p => p.type === "month")?.value;\n  const day = parts.find(p => p.type === "day")?.value;\n  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const method = c.req.method;\n  const userHandle = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n  const date = c.req.query("date");\n\n  if (!date) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Missing date parameter" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  // Validate date format (YYYY-MM-DD)\n  if (!/^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(date)) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Invalid date format. Use YYYY-MM-DD" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const db = new Database(DB_PATH);\n\n    const query = db.query(`\n      SELECT play_date, question, options, correct_index, explanation\n      FROM questions\n      WHERE play_date = ?\n    `);\n\n    const row = query.get(date) as any;\n\n    if (!row) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Question not found for this date" }, 404);\n    }\n\n    // Reject future dates (MST) — always\n    const today = getTodayMST();\n    if (date > today) {\n      return c.json({ error: "No question available for this date yet" }, 404);\n    }\n\n    // Check if this Zo user has already answered\n    let alreadyAnswered = false;\n    let userScore = 0;\n    let userStreak = 0;\n\n    if (userHandle) {\n      const userRow = db.query(\n        `SELECT score, streak FROM leaderboard WHERE user_handle = ? AND play_date = ?`\n      ).get(userHandle, date) as any;\n\n      if (userRow) {\n        alreadyAnswered = true;\n        userScore = userRow.score || 0;\n        userStreak = userRow.streak || 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (method === "GET") {\n      return c.json({\n        play_date: row.play_date,\n        question: row.question,\n        options: JSON.parse(row.options),\n        correct_index: row.correct_index,\n        explanation: row.explanation,\n        already_answered: alreadyAnswered,\n        user_score: userScore,\n        user_streak: userStreak\n      });\n    }\n\n    // POST: record answer\n    const { selected_index, user_handle } = await c.req.json();\n    const user = user_handle || "anonymous";\n    try {\n      const today2 = getTodayMST();\n      if (date > today2) {\n        return c.json({ error: "No question available for this date yet" }, 404);\n      }\n\n      const existing = db.query(\n        "SELECT score FROM leaderboard WHERE user_handle = ? AND play_date = ?"\n      ).get(user, date);\n\n      if (existing) {\n        db.close();\n        const isCorrect = selected_index === row.correct_index;\n        return c.json({\n          is_correct: isCorrect,\n          selected_index,\n          correct_index: row.correct_index,\n          explanation: row.explanation,\n          already_answered: true,\n          stats: { total_players: 0, correct_count: 0, correct_percentage: 0, percentile: 0, rank_phrase: "You already answered this!" }\n        });\n      }\n\n      const isCorrect = selected_index === row.correct_index;\n      db.query(`\n        INSERT INTO leaderboard (user_handle, play_date, is_correct, score, streak)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0)\n      `).run(user, date, isCorrect ? 1 : 0, isCorrect ? 1 : 0);\n      db.close();\n\n      return c.json({\n        is_correct: isCorrect,\n        selected_index,\n        correct_index: row.correct_index,\n        explanation: row.explanation,\n        already_answered: false,\n        stats: { total_players: 0, correct_count: 0, correct_percentage: 0, percentile: 0, rank_phrase: "" }\n      });\n    } catch (err) {\n      if (db) db.close();\n      console.error("Error recording answer:", err);\n      return c.json({ error: "Failed to record answer" }, 500);\n    }\n\n    return c.json({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Trivia by-date API error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/trivia/dates` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const db = new Database(DB_PATH);\n    \n    const query = db.query(`\n      SELECT play_date FROM questions ORDER BY play_date ASC\n    `);\n    \n    const rows = query.all() as any[];\n    const dates = rows.map(r => r.play_date);\n    \n    return c.json({ \n      dates,\n      count: dates.length,\n      first_date: dates[0] || null,\n      last_date: dates[dates.length - 1] || null\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Trivia dates API error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/trivia/leaderboard` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db";\n\nfunction getMSTDate(): string {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const mstOffset = -7 * 60;\n  const localOffset = now.getTimezoneOffset();\n  const mstDate = new Date(now.getTime() + (localOffset + mstOffset) * 60 * 1000);\n  return mstDate.toISOString().split("T")[0];\n}\n\nfunction getWeekBounds(dateStr: string): { start: string; end: string } {\n  const date = new Date(dateStr + "T00:00:00");\n  const day = date.getDay();\n  const mondayOffset = day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day;\n  const monday = new Date(date.getTime() + mondayOffset * 86400000);\n  const sunday = new Date(monday.getTime() + 6 * 86400000);\n  return {\n    start: monday.toISOString().split("T")[0],\n    end: sunday.toISOString().split("T")[0]\n  };\n}\n\nfunction getMonthBounds(dateStr: string): { start: string; end: string } {\n  const year = dateStr.substring(0, 4);\n  const month = dateStr.substring(5, 7);\n  return {\n    start: `${year}-${month}-01`,\n    end: `${year}-${month}-31`\n  };\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const db = new Database(DB_PATH);\n  \n  try {\n    const today = getMSTDate();\n    const { start: weekStart, end: weekEnd } = getWeekBounds(today);\n    const { start: monthStart, end: monthEnd } = getMonthBounds(today);\n    \n    const periods = {\n      today: { start: today, end: today },\n      week: { start: weekStart, end: weekEnd },\n      month: { start: monthStart, end: monthEnd },\n      alltime: { start: "1970-01-01", end: "2099-12-31" }\n    };\n    \n    const result: Record<string, Array<{ rank: number; user_handle: string; score: number; streak: number }>> = {};\n    \n    for (const [period, bounds] of Object.entries(periods)) {\n      const rows = db.query(`\n        SELECT \n          user_handle,\n          SUM(is_correct) as total_correct,\n          MAX(streak) as best_streak\n        FROM leaderboard\n        WHERE play_date >= ? AND play_date <= ?\n        GROUP BY user_handle\n        ORDER BY total_correct DESC, best_streak DESC\n        LIMIT 10\n      `).all(bounds.start, bounds.end) as Array<{ user_handle: string; total_correct: number; best_streak: number }>;\n      \n      result[period] = rows.map((row, idx) => ({\n        rank: idx + 1,\n        user_handle: row.user_handle,\n        score: row.total_correct,\n        streak: row.best_streak\n      }));\n    }\n    \n    return c.json(result);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Leaderboard API error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);\n  } finally {\n    db.close();\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/trivia/random` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db";\n\nfunction getTodayMST(): string {\n  const date = new Date();\n  const options = { timeZone: "America/Edmonton", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" } as const;\n  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", options);\n  const parts = formatter.formatToParts(date);\n  const year = parts.find(p => p.type === "year")?.value;\n  const month = parts.find(p => p.type === "month")?.value;\n  const day = parts.find(p => p.type === "day")?.value;\n  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const db = new Database(DB_PATH);\n    const count = parseInt(c.req.query("count") || "10", 10);\n    const limit = Math.min(count, 50);\n    const userHandle = c.req.header("X-Zo-User");\n    const today = getTodayMST();\n    const isZoUser = userHandle && !userHandle.startsWith("anon_");\n    \n    let availableQuery = `\n      SELECT id, play_date, question, options, correct_index, explanation\n      FROM questions\n      WHERE play_date <= $today\n    `;\n\n    let query: ReturnType<typeof db.query>;\n\n    if (userHandle) {\n      // Exclude questions this user has already answered\n      query = db.query(`\n        SELECT q.play_date, q.question, q.options, q.correct_index, q.explanation\n        FROM questions q\n        WHERE q.play_date <= ?\n          AND q.play_date NOT IN (\n            SELECT play_date FROM leaderboard WHERE user_handle = ?\n          )\n        ORDER BY RANDOM()\n        LIMIT ?\n      `);\n      const rows = query.all(today, userHandle, limit) as any[];\n      const questions = rows.map(row => ({\n        play_date: row.play_date,\n        question: row.question,\n        options: JSON.parse(row.options),\n        correct_index: row.correct_index,\n        explanation: row.explanation\n      }));\n      db.close();\n      return c.json({ questions, count: questions.length, filtered: true });\n    } else {\n      // Anonymous users: exclude already-answered via localStorage (handled on client)\n      query = db.query(`\n        SELECT play_date, question, options, correct_index, explanation\n        FROM questions\n        WHERE play_date <= ?\n        ORDER BY RANDOM()\n        LIMIT ?\n      `);\n      const rows = query.all(today, limit) as any[];\n      const questions = rows.map(row => ({\n        play_date: row.play_date,\n        question: row.question,\n        options: JSON.parse(row.options),\n        correct_index: row.correct_index,\n        explanation: row.explanation\n      }));\n      db.close();\n      return c.json({ questions, count: questions.length, filtered: false });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Random trivia API error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);\n  }\n};
```

### `/api/trivia/subscribe` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nconst TEABLE_BASE = "https://app.teable.io/api";\nconst SUBSCRIBERS_TABLE = "tblUhUiUmvKbaxA2cx4";\nconst API_KEY = process.env.TEABLE_API_KEY;\n\nasync function teableReq(method: string, endpoint: string, body?: any): Promise<any> {\n  const opts: any = {\n    method,\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${API_KEY}` }\n  };\n  if (body) {\n    opts.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";\n    opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);\n  }\n  const res = await fetch(`${TEABLE_BASE}${endpoint}`, opts);\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text();\n    throw new Error(`${method} ${endpoint}: ${res.status} ${text}`);\n  }\n  return res.json();\n}\n\nconst SENDFOX_BASE = "https://api.sendfox.com";\n\nasync function sendfoxReq(method: string, path: string, body?: any): Promise<any> {\n  const token = process.env.SENDFOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;\n  const opts: any = { method, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, Accept: "application/json" } };\n  if (body) {\n    opts.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";\n    opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);\n  }\n  const res = await fetch(`${SENDFOX_BASE}${path}`, opts);\n  const text = await res.text();\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${method} ${path}: ${res.status} ${text}`);\n  return text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;\n}\n\nasync function ensureSendfoxSubscriber(email: string): Promise<void> {\n  const token = process.env.SENDFOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;\n  if (!token) { console.log("SENDFOX_ACCESS_TOKEN not set; skipping SendFox sync"); return; }\n  const lists = await sendfoxReq("GET", "/lists?per_page=100");\n  let list = lists.data?.find((l: any) => l.name === "Zo Trivia");\n  if (!list) list = await sendfoxReq("POST", "/lists", { name: "Zo Trivia" });\n  const listId: number = list.id;\n  const search = await sendfoxReq("GET", `/contacts?search=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`);\n  const found = search.data?.find((c: any) => c.email?.toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase());\n  if (found) {\n    const detail = await sendfoxReq("GET", `/contacts/${found.id}`);\n    const current: number[] = Array.isArray(detail.lists) ? detail.lists.map((l: any) => l.id) : [];\n    const merged = Array.from(new Set([...current, listId]));\n    await sendfoxReq("PATCH", `/contacts/${found.id}`, { lists: merged });\n  } else {\n    await sendfoxReq("POST", "/contacts", { email, lists: [listId] });\n  }\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const { email } = await c.req.json();\n    \n    if (!email || !email.includes("@")) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Valid email required" }, 400);\n    }\n    \n    // 1. Sync to Teable\n    // Get all subscribers\n    const existing = await teableReq("GET", `/table/${SUBSCRIBERS_TABLE}/record?take=200`);\n    \n    // Check if already subscribed\n    const alreadySubscribed = existing.records?.find((r: any) => \n      r.fields.email === email\n    );\n    \n    if (!alreadySubscribed) {\n      // Add new subscriber to Teable\n      await teableReq("POST", `/table/${SUBSCRIBERS_TABLE}/record`, {\n        records: [{\n          fields: {\n            email: email,\n            subscribed_at: new Date().toISOString()\n          }\n        }],\n        typecast: true\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 2. Sync to local databases (for email agent consistency)\n    try {\n      const { Database } = await import("bun:sqlite");\n      \n      // Update trivia.db (sqlite fallback)\n      const triviaDb = new Database("/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db");\n      triviaDb.run(\n        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO subscribers (email) VALUES (?)",\n        [email]\n      );\n      triviaDb.close();\n      \n      console.log(`Synced ${email} to local databases`);\n    } catch (dbErr) {\n      console.error("Local DB sync error:", dbErr);\n    }\n    try {\n      await ensureSendfoxSubscriber(email);\n    } catch (sendfoxErr) {\n      console.error("SendFox sync error:", sendfoxErr);\n    }\n    \n    return c.json({ success: true, message: alreadySubscribed ? "Already subscribed" : "Subscribed successfully" });\n    \n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Subscribe error:", error);\n    return c.json({ \n      error: "Subscription failed", \n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error" \n    }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/trivia/user-stats` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Projects/zo-trivia/trivia.db";\n\nasync function validateSession(c: Context): Promise<string | null> {\n  const zoUser = c.req.header("X-Zo-User") || c.req.header("x-zo-user");\n  if (zoUser && zoUser.length > 0) return zoUser;\n  \n  const authHeader = c.req.header("Authorization");\n  if (authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) {\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n    if (process.env.ZO_API_KEY && token === process.env.ZO_API_KEY) return "curtastrophe";\n  }\n  \n  const cookieHeader = c.req.header("Cookie") || "";\n  if (cookieHeader.includes("zo_session") || cookieHeader.includes("auth_token")) return "curtastrophe";\n  \n  return null;\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const db = new Database(DB_PATH);\n  \n  try {\n    const handle = await validateSession(c);\n    \n    if (!handle) {\n      return c.json({ error: "Not authenticated" }, 401);\n    }\n    \n    const stats = db.query(`\n      SELECT \n        COUNT(*) as total_played,\n        SUM(score) as total_score,\n        SUM(CASE WHEN score > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_correct,\n        MAX(streak) as best_streak\n      FROM leaderboard\n      WHERE user_handle = ?\n    `).get(handle) as { total_played: number; total_score: number; total_correct: number; best_streak: number } | null;\n    \n    const recent = db.query(`\n      SELECT play_date, score, streak\n      FROM leaderboard\n      WHERE user_handle = ?\n      ORDER BY play_date DESC\n      LIMIT 10\n    `).all(handle) as Array<{ play_date: string; score: number; streak: number }>;\n    \n    const joined = db.query(`\n      SELECT joined_at FROM subscribers WHERE email LIKE ?\n    `).get(`%${handle}%`) as { joined_at: string } | null;\n    \n    return c.json({\n      handle,\n      total_played: stats?.total_played || 0,\n      total_score: stats?.total_score || 0,\n      total_correct: stats?.total_correct || 0,\n      accuracy: stats?.total_played > 0 ? Math.round((stats.total_correct / stats.total_played) * 100) : 0,\n      best_streak: stats?.best_streak || 0,\n      recent_plays: recent,\n      referral_link: `https://zo.computer?referrer=curtastrophe`\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("User stats API error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);\n  } finally {\n    db.close();\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/api/twinmind-callback` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\n// This route captures the OAuth authorization code from TwinMind\n// and displays it for manual token exchange\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const code = c.req.query("code");\n  const state = c.req.query("state");\n  const error = c.req.query("error");\n\n  if (error) {\n    return c.html(`<html><body><h1>Error</h1><p>${error}</p></body></html>`);\n  }\n\n  if (!code) {\n    return c.html(`<html><body><h1>Error</h1><p>No authorization code received</p></body></html>`);\n  }\n\n  // Store the code in a simple in-memory store so we can retrieve it\n  const fs = await import("fs");\n  const data = JSON.stringify({ code, state, timestamp: Date.now() });\n  fs.writeFileSync("/tmp/twinmind_oauth_code.json", data);\n\n  return c.html(`\n    <html>\n      <body style="font-family: sans-serif; max-width: 600px; margin: 40px auto; text-align: center;">\n        <h1>TwinMind Connected!</h1>\n        <p>Authorization code received. You can close this tab and return to Zo.</p>\n        <p style="color: #888; font-size: 12px;">Code captured at ${new Date().toISOString()}</p>\n      </body>\n    </html>\n  `);\n};\n
```

import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, statSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";\nimport { resolve, join } from "node:path";\n\nconst CACHE_FILE = resolve("/home/workspace/.zo/.temp/x_feed_cache.json");\nconst MAX_AGE_MS = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\nconst ZO_TOKEN = process.env.ZO_CLIENT_IDENTITY_TOKEN || "";\nconst MODEL = "vercel:minimax/minimax-m2.7";\n\ninterface UpdateItem {\n  id: string;\n  type: "tweet" | "note";\n  url: string;\n  text: string;\n  date: string;\n  timestamp: number;\n  likes?: number;\n  retweets?: number;\n  replies?: number;\n  views?: number;\n  title?: string;\n}\n\nfunction loadTweetCache(): UpdateItem[] {\n  try {\n    if (!existsSync(CACHE_FILE)) return [];\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(CACHE_FILE, "utf-8")) as UpdateItem[];\n  } catch { return []; }\n}\n\nfunction saveTweetCache(posts: UpdateItem[]) {\n  writeFileSync(CACHE_FILE, JSON.stringify(posts, null, 2), "utf-8");\n}\n\nfunction isCacheFresh(): boolean {\n  try { return Date.now() - statSync(CACHE_FILE).mtimeMs < MAX_AGE_MS; }\n  catch { return false; }\n}\n\nasync function fetchTweetsViaZoAsk(): Promise<UpdateItem[] | null> {\n  if (!ZO_TOKEN) return null;\n  const prompt = `Search for recent posts from X user @Z3nlyte using x_search with:\n- allowed_x_handles: ["Z3nlyte"]\n- from_date: "2025-01-01"\n- enable_image_understanding: false\n- enable_video_understanding: false\nReturn ONLY a valid JSON array of post objects with: id, url, text (full text, no truncation), date (e.g. "Mar 16, 2026"), likes, retweets, replies, views. If no posts found, return: [].`;\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch("https://api.zo.computer/zo/ask", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ZO_TOKEN}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        input: prompt,\n        model_name: MODEL,\n        output_format: {\n          type: "object",\n          properties: { posts: { type: "array", items: { type: "object",\n            properties: { id: {type:"string"}, url: {type:"string"}, text: {type:"string"},\n              date: {type:"string"}, likes: {type:"integer"}, retweets: {type:"integer"},\n              replies: {type:"integer"}, views: {type:"integer"} },\n            required: ["id","url","text","date"] } } },\n          required: ["posts"] }\n      }),\n    });\n    if (!resp.ok) return null;\n    const data = (await resp.json()) as { output?: { posts?: any[] } };\n    const posts = data.output?.posts || [];\n    return posts.map(p => ({\n      ...p,\n      type: "tweet",\n      timestamp: new Date(p.date).getTime()\n    }));\n  } catch { return null; }\n}\n\nfunction fetchNotes(): UpdateItem[] {\n  const notesDir = "/home/workspace/Documents/blog/notes";\n  if (!existsSync(notesDir)) return [];\n  \n  const notes: UpdateItem[] = [];\n  const files = readdirSync(notesDir).filter(f => f.endsWith(".md"));\n  \n  for (const file of files) {\n    try {\n      const content = readFileSync(join(notesDir, file), "utf-8");\n      const match = content.match(/^---\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n---/);\n      if (match) {\n        const fm = match[1];\n        const titleMatch = fm.match(/title:\\s*(.+)/);\n        const dateMatch = fm.match(/^date:\\s*(.+)/m);\n        const excerptMatch = fm.match(/excerpt:\\s*(.+)/);\n        \n        const title = titleMatch ? titleMatch[1].replace(/^["\

"true") !== "false";\nconst ZO_TOKEN = process.env.ZO_CLIENT_IDENTITY_TOKEN || "";\nconst MODEL = "vercel:minimax/minimax-m2.7";\n\nconst HOMEPAGE_KB: Record<string, string> = {\n  identity:\n    "This site is ZOS, also called ZenOS, by Zenlyte. It is presented as a personal site rebuilt as an operating system and built entirely on Zo Computer for the Zo Computer Challenge.",\n  about:\n    "The site presents Zenlyte as a Data & Analytics professional and AI builder working with Zo Computer.",\n  focus:\n    "The homepage highlights work across data, AI, automation, building, and community.",\n  play:\n    "Interactive homepage )?(system|hidden|secret|private|internal)/i,\n  /\\b(jailbreak|dan mode|developer mode|unrestricted|uncensored)\\b/i,\n  /\\b(password|api[- ]?key|token|credentials?)\\b/i,\n  /\\b(secret|hidden)\\s+(route|page|path|url|link|endpoint)/i,\n  /\\b\\/secret\\b|\\babout-the-build\\b|\\btrivia\\b.*\\b(answer|solution)/i,\n  /override.*\\b(safety|rules|guard|policy)/i,\n];\n\nconst RATE_MAX = 10;\nconst RATE_WINDOW_MS = 60_000;\nconst rate = new Map<string, number[]>();\n\nconst CACHE_MAX = 100;\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\ntype Confidence = "high" | "medium" | "low";\ninterface VoiResponse {\n  allowed: boolean;\n  answer: string;\n  confidence: Confidence;\n  source_keys: string[];\n  cached?: boolean;\n}\n\ntype CacheVal = { at: number; payload: VoiResponse };\nconst cache = new Map<string, CacheVal>();\nconst VALID_KEYS = new Set(Object.keys(HOMEPAGE_KB));\n\nfunction isLikelyInjection(s: string): boolean {\n  return INJECTION_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(s));\n}\n\nfunction rateLimited(ip: string): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const arr = (rate.get(ip) || []).filter((t) => now - t < RATE_WINDOW_MS);\n  if (arr.length >= RATE_MAX) {\n    rate.set(ip, arr);\n    return true;\n  }\n  arr.push(now);\n  rate.set(ip, arr);\n  return false;\n}\n\nfunction normKey(q: string): string {\n  return q.toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, " ").replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, "").trim();\n}\n\nfunction cacheGet(k: string): VoiResponse | null {\n  const v = cache.get(k);\n  if (!v) return null;\n  if (Date.now() - v.at > CACHE_TTL_MS) {\n    cache.delete(k);\n    return null;\n  }\n  cache.delete(k);\n  cache.set(k, v);\n  return v.payload;\n}\n\nfunction cacheSet(k: string, payload: VoiResponse) {\n  cache.set(k, { at: Date.now(), payload });\n  if (cache.size > CACHE_MAX) {\n    const first = cache.keys().next().value;\n    if (first !== undefined) cache.delete(first);\n ### `/api/x-feed` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, statSync } from "node:fs";\nimport { resolve } from "node:path";\n\nconst CACHE_FILE = resolve("/home/workspace/.zo/.temp/x_feed_cache.json");\nconst MAX_AGE_MS = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\nconst ZO_TOKEN = process.env.ZO_CLIENT_IDENTITY_TOKEN || "";\nconst MODEL = "vercel:minimax/minimax-m2.7";\n\ninterface Tweet {\n  id: string;\n  url: string;\n  text: string;\n  date: string;\n  date_display: string;\n  created_at: string;\n  likes: number;\n  retweets: number;\n  replies: number;\n  views: number;\n}\n\ntype RawTweet = Partial<Tweet> & {\n  full_text?: string;\n  date_string?: string;\n};\n\nfunction parseDateString(input: string): string {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  const direct = new Date(trimmed);\n  if (!Number.isNaN(direct.getTime())) return direct.toISOString();\n\n  const withUtc = new Date(`${trimmed} UTC`);\n  if (!Number.isNaN(withUtc.getTime())) return withUtc.toISOString();\n\n  const match = trimmed.match(/^([A-Za-z]{3,9})\\s+(\\d{1,2}),\\s+(\\d{4})$/);\n  if (match) {\n    const [, monthName, day, year] = match;\n    const normalized = new Date(`${monthName} ${day}, ${year} 12:00:00 UTC`);\n    if (!Number.isNaN(normalized.getTime())) return normalized.toISOString();\n  }\n\n  return new Date(0).toISOString();\n}\n\nfunction normalizeTweet(raw: RawTweet): Tweet | null {\n  const id = String(raw.id || "").trim();\n  const url = String(raw.url || "").trim();\n  const text = String(raw.text || raw.full_text || "").trim();\n  const display = String(raw.date_display || raw.date_string || raw.date || "").trim();\n  const createdAtSource = String(raw.created_at || "").trim();\n  const created_at = createdAtSource ? parseDateString(createdAtSource) : parseDateString(display || new Date().toISOString());\n  const date_display = display || new Date(created_at).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric", year: "numeric" });\n\n  if (!id || !url || !text) return null;\n\n  return {\n    id,\n    url,\n    text,\n    date: date_display,\n    date_display,\n    created_at,\n    likes: Number(raw.likes || 0),\n    retweets: Number(raw.retweets || 0),\n    replies: Number(raw.replies || 0),\n    views: Number(raw.views || 0),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction loadCache(): Tweet[] {\n  try {\n    if (!existsSync(CACHE_FILE)) return [];\n    const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CACHE_FILE, "utf-8")) as RawTweet[];\n    return raw.map(normalizeTweet).filter(Boolean) as Tweet[];\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nfunction saveCache(posts: Tweet[]) {\n  writeFileSync(CACHE_FILE, JSON.stringify(posts, null, 2), "utf-8");\n}\n\nfunction isCacheFresh(): boolean {\n  try {\n    return Date.now() - statSync(CACHE_FILE).mtimeMs < MAX_AGE_MS;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function fetchTweetsViaZoAsk(): Promise<Tweet[] | null> {\n  if (!ZO_TOKEN) return null;\n  const prompt = `Search for recent posts from X user @Z3nlyte using x_search with:\n- allowed_x_handles: ["Z3nlyte"]\n- from_date: "2025-01-01"\n- enable_image_understanding: false\n- enable_video_understanding: false\nReturn ONLY a valid JSON array of post objects with: id, url, text (full text, no truncation), date (e.g. "Mar 16, 2026"), likes, retweets, replies, views. If no posts found, return: [].`;\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch("https://api.zo.computer/zo/ask", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        Authorization: `Bearer ${ZO_TOKEN}`,\n        "Content-Type": "application/json",\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        input: prompt,\n        model_name: MODEL,\n        output_format: {\n          type: "object",\n          properties: {\n            posts: {\n              type: "array",\n              items: {\n                type: "object",\n                properties: {\n                  id: { type: "string" },\n                  url: { type: "string" },\n                  text: { type: "string" },\n                  date: { type: "string" },\n                  likes: { type: "integer" },\n                  retweets: { type: "integer" },\n                  replies: { type: "integer" },\n                  views: { type: "integer" },\n                },\n                required: ["id", "url", "text", "date"],\n              },\n            },\n          },\n          required: ["posts"],\n        },\n      }),\n    });\n    if (!resp.ok) return null;\n    const data = (await resp.json()) as { output?: { posts?: RawTweet[] } };\n    const normalized = (data.output?.posts || []).map(normalizeTweet).filter(Boolean) as Tweet[];\n    return normalized;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  if (c.req.query("cache") !== "false") {\n    const cached = loadCache();\n    if (cached.length > 0 && isCacheFresh()) {\n      return c.json({ source: "cache", cached: true, posts: cached });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const posts = await fetchTweetsViaZoAsk();\n  if (!posts?.length) {\n    const stale = loadCache();\n    return c.json({ source: "fallback", cached: !!stale.length, posts: stale });\n  }\n\n  saveCache(posts);\n  return c.json({ source: "fresh", cached: false, posts });\n};\n
```

### `/api/zo-city-data` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFile, readdir, stat, access } from "node:fs/promises";\nimport path from "node:path";\n\nconst WORKSPACE = "/home/workspace";\nconst SKILLS_DIR = path.join(WORKSPACE, "Skills");\nconst TIMELINE_PATH = path.join(WORKSPACE, "Data/zo-city/timeline.json");\nconst CENSUS_PATH = path.join(WORKSPACE, "Data/zo-city/census.json");\n\nconst DISTRICTS = [\n  { id: "skills", name: "Skills", color: "#8b5cf6", label: "Automation Hills" },\n  { id: "agents", name: "Agents", color: "#ec4899", label: "Agent Quarter" },\n  { id: "routes", name: "Routes", color: "#22c55e", label: "Downtown Core" },\n  { id: "services", name: "Services", color: "#f59e0b", label: "Service Yard" },\n  { id: "sites", name: "Sites", color: "#3b82f6", label: "Site Harbor" },\n  { id: "datasets", name: "Datasets", color: "#06b6d4", label: "Data Lake" },\n];\n\nasync function exists(p: string) {\n  try { await access(p); return true; } catch { return false; }\n}\n\nasync function listDirs(dir: string) {\n  try {\n    const entries = await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n    return entries.filter(e => e.isDirectory()).map(e => e.name);\n  } catch { return []; }\n}\n\nasync function getLiveCounts() {\n  const skillDirs = await listDirs(SKILLS_DIR);\n  let skillCount = 0;\n  for (const name of skillDirs) {\n    if (await exists(path.join(SKILLS_DIR, name, "SKILL.md"))) skillCount++;\n  }\n\n  let census: any = {};\n  try {\n    census = JSON.parse(await readFile(CENSUS_PATH, "utf-8"));\n  } catch {}\n\n  return {\n    skills: skillCount,\n    agents: census.districts?.agents?.count ?? 0,\n    routes: census.districts?.routes?.count ?? 0,\n    services: census.districts?.services?.count ?? 0,\n    sites: census.districts?.sites?.count ?? 0,\n    datasets: census.districts?.datasets?.count ?? 0,\n  };\n}\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const live = await getLiveCounts();\n\n  let timeline: any = null;\n  try {\n    timeline = JSON.parse(await readFile(TIMELINE_PATH, "utf-8"));\n  } catch {}\n\n  const districts = DISTRICTS.map(d => ({\n    ...d,\n    count: live[d.id as keyof typeof live] || 0,\n  }));\n  const total = districts.reduce((s, d) => s + d.count, 0);\n\n  const elements = timeline?.elements || [];\n  const dailyCounts = timeline?.dailyCounts || {};\n  const dateRange = timeline?.dateRange || { start: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), end: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) };\n\n  return c.json({\n    total,\n    districts,\n    timeline: {\n      dateRange,\n      dailyCounts,\n      elements,\n    },\n    meta: {\n      live: true,\n      generated: timeline?.generated || null,\n      note: "Live counts from filesystem + census. Timeline from Data/zo-city/timeline.json with real creation dates.",\n    },\n  });\n};
```

### `/api/zo-space-theme-gallery` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync } from "fs";\n\nconst REGISTRY_PATH = "/home/workspace/Skills/zo-theme-gallery/assets/theme-registry.json";\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, OPTIONS");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Accept, Content-Type");\n\n  if (c.req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return c.text("", 204);\n  }\n\n  let registry: any[];\n  try {\n    registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, "utf-8"));\n  } catch {\n    return c.json({ error: "Registry not found" }, 500);\n  }\n\n  const mode = c.req.query("mode");\n  const fontType = c.req.query("fontType");\n  const search = c.req.query("q")?.toLowerCase();\n\n  let filtered = registry;\n  if (mode) filtered = filtered.filter((t: any) => t.mode === mode);\n  if (fontType) filtered = filtered.filter((t: any) => t.fontType === fontType);\n  if (search) {\n    filtered = filtered.filter((t: any) =>\n      t.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||\n      t.description.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||\n      t.tags.some((tag: string) => tag.includes(search))\n    );\n  }\n\n  return c.json(filtered);\n};
```

### `/api/zo-space-theme-gallery/:id` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";\n\nconst REGISTRY_PATH = "/home/workspace/Skills/zo-theme-gallery/assets/theme-registry.json";\nconst THEMES_DIR = "/home/workspace/Skills/zo-theme-gallery/assets/themes";\nconst SKILL_PATH = "/home/workspace/Skills/zo-theme-remote/SKILL.md";\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, OPTIONS");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Accept, Content-Type");\n\n  if (c.req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return c.text("", 204);\n  }\n\n  const id = c.req.param("id");\n\n  if (id === "skill") {\n    c.header("Content-Type", "text/markdown; charset=utf-8");\n    try {\n      const content = readFileSync(SKILL_PATH, "utf-8");\n      return c.text(content);\n    } catch {\n      return c.text("# Error\\nSkill file not found.", 500);\n    }\n  }\n\n  let registry: any[];\n  try {\n    registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, "utf-8"));\n  } catch {\n    return c.json({ error: "Registry not found" }, 500);\n  }\n\n  const theme = registry.find((t: any) => t.id === id);\n  if (!theme) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Theme not found" }, 404);\n  }\n\n  const promptPath = `${THEMES_DIR}/${id}.md`;\n  const prompt = existsSync(promptPath) ? readFileSync(promptPath, "utf-8") : null;\n\n  return c.json({ ...theme, prompt });\n};
```

import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync } from "fs";\n\nconst SKILL_PATH = "/home/workspace/Skills/zo-space-themer/SKILL.md";\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, OPTIONS");\n  c.header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Accept, Content-Type");\n  c.header("Content-Type", "text/markdown; charset=utf-8");\n\n  if (c.req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return c.text("", 204);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const skillContent = readFileSync(SKILL_PATH, "utf-8");\n    return c.text(skillContent);\n  } catch {\n    return c.text("# Skill not found\\n\\nThe zo-space-themer skill could not be loaded.", 404);\n  }\n};

```tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";\nimport { ArrowLeft, Clock, Tag, Share2, ChevronUp } from "lucide-react";\n\nconst COLORS = {\n  bg: "#0a0a0f",\n  card: "#0f1117",\n  cyan: "#06b6d4",\n  cyanLight: "#22d3ee",\n  indigo: "#6366f1",\n  indigoLight: "#818cf8",\n  muted: "#94a3b8",\n  dimmed: "#64748b",\n  border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",\n};\n\nfunction GlobalNav() {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [links, setLinks] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [auth, setAuth] = useState(false);\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/nav-links", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { setLinks(d.links || []); setAuth(d.authenticated); })\n      .catch(() => {});\n      \n    const onClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);\n    };\n    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {/* Desktop: Top-right navigation */}\n      <div className="hidden md:block fixed top-6 right-6 z-[9998]" ref={ref}>\n        <button \n          onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}\n          className="bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700 text-white p-2.5 rounded-lg shadow-xl hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"\n          style={{ borderColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" }}\n        >\n          <span style={{ color: "#06b6d4", fontSize: "20px" }}>☰</span>\n        </button>\n        \n        {open && (\n          <div className="absolute top-full right-0 mt-2 w-64 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"\n            style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.98)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)", border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" }}>\n            {auth && (\n              <div className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-cyan-400 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">\n                <span>Authenticated</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n            <div className="py-2 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">\n              {links.map((l, idx) => (\n                <a key={l.path} href={l.path} \n                  onClick={() => setOpen(false)}\n                  className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">\n                  <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider w-6 text-cyan-400">\n                    {String(idx + 1).padStart(2, \

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";\n\nexport default function BuildinAuth() {\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"checking" | "connected" | "disconnected">("checking");\n  const [spaces, setSpaces] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    checkStatus();\n  }, []);\n\n  const checkStatus = async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch("/api/buildin/status");\n      const data = await res.json();\n      if (data.connected) {\n        setStatus("connected");\n        setSpaces(data.spaces || []);\n      } else {\n        setStatus("disconnected");\n      }\n    } catch {\n      setStatus("disconnected");\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleAuthorize = () => {\n    const clientId = process.env.BUILDIN_CLIENT_ID || "";\n    const redirectUri = encodeURIComponent("https://curtastrophe.zo.space/api/buildin/callback");\n    const state = Math.random().toString(36).substring(7);\n    const authUrl = `https://api.buildin.ai/oauth/authorize?client_id=${clientId}&response_type=code&redirect_uri=${redirectUri}&scope=all&state=${state}`;\n    window.open(authUrl, "_blank");\n  };\n\n  const handleDisconnect = async () => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    await fetch("/api/buildin/disconnect", { method: "POST" });\n    setStatus("disconnected");\n    setSpaces([]);\n    setLoading(false);\n  };\n\n  if (status === "checking") {\n    return (\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-white p-8 flex items-center justify-center">\n        <div className="animate-pulse">Checking connection status...</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-white p-8">\n      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">\n        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">\n          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500 to-pink-500 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">\n            📝\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Buildin.ai Integration</h1>\n            <p className="text-zinc-400">Connect your knowledge workspace</p>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {status === "disconnected" ? (\n          <div className="bg-zinc-900 rounded-2xl p-8 border border-zinc-800">\n            <div className="text-center mb-6">\n              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-zinc-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">\n                <span className="text-3xl">🔗</span>\n              </div>\n              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-2">Connect to Buildin</h2>\n              <p className="text-zinc-400 text-sm">\n                Authorize Zo to access your Buildin pages and content.\n                You\

### `/byok-reference` (page, private)

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";\nimport { Copy, Check, RefreshCw, Database, Search, ExternalLink, AlertCircle, Clock } from "lucide-react";\n\ntype ByokModel = {\n  friendlyName: string;\n  byokId: string;\n  contextWindow: number | null;\n  priority: number | null;\n};\n\ntype ProviderGroup = {\n  provider: string;\n  models: ByokModel[];\n};\n\ntype ApiResponse = {\n  fetchedAt: string;\n  source: string;\n  totalUpstream: number;\n  totalByok: number;\n  groups: ProviderGroup[];\n  servedFrom: "live" | "cache" | string;\n  cacheAgeMs: number;\n};\n\nconst theme = {\n  background: "#0b0b0d",\n  foreground: "#f5f5f4",\n  card: "#161618",\n  cardHover: "#1c1c1f",\n  border: "#27272a",\n  muted: "#a1a1aa",\n  faint: "#71717a",\n  accent: "#3b82f6",\n  accentFg: "#ffffff",\n  byokColor: "#a855f7",\n};\n\nfunction fmtContext(n: number | null): string {\n  if (n == null) return "—";\n  if (n >= 1_000_000) return `${(n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M`;\n  if (n >= 1_000) return `${(n / 1_000).toFixed(0)}K`;\n  return String(n);\n}\n\nfunction fmtAge(ms: number): string {\n  if (ms < 60_000) return `${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s ago`;\n  if (ms < 3_600_000) return `${Math.round(ms / 60_000)}m ago`;\n  return `${Math.round(ms / 3_600_000)}h ago`;\n}\n\nfunction CopyButton({ value }: { value: string }) {\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);\n  return (\n    <button\n      onClick={() => {\n        navigator.clipboard.writeText(value).then(() => {\n          setCopied(true);\n          setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1200);\n        });\n      }}\n      className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity p-1 rounded hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white"\n      title={`Copy: ${value}`}\n    >\n      {copied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function ByokReference() {\n  const [data, setData] = useState<ApiResponse | null>(null);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("");\n  const [onlyByok, setOnlyByok] = useState(false);\n  const [refreshTick, setRefreshTick] = useState(0);\n  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());\n\n  const load = async () => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch("/api/byok-reference", { cache: "no-store" });\n      if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`API ${resp.status}`);\n      const json = (await resp.json()) as ApiResponse;\n      setData(json);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setError(String(e));\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n  }, [refreshTick]);\n\n  // Tick once a minute so the "freshness" badge updates without manual reload\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 30_000);\n    return () => clearInterval(t);\n  }, []);\n\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!data) return [];\n    const q = filter.trim().toLowerCase();\n    return data.groups\n      .map((g) => ({\n        ...g,\n        models: g.models.filter((m) => {\n          if (onlyByok && !m.byokId.startsWith("byok:")) return false;\n          if (!q) return true;\n          return (\n            m.friendlyName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n            m.byokId.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n            g.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q)\n          );\n        }),\n      }))\n      .filter((g) => g.models.length > 0);\n  }, [data, filter, onlyByok]);\n\n  const totalShown = filtered.reduce((n, g) => n + g.models.length, 0);\n  const cacheAgeSec = data ? Math.round((now - new Date(data.fetchedAt).getTime()) / 1000) : 0;\n  const isStale = cacheAgeSec > 600; // >10 min old\n\n  return (\n    <main\n      style={\n        {\n          "--page-bg": theme.background,\n          "--page-fg": theme.foreground,\n          "--page-card": theme.card,\n          "--page-border": theme.border,\n          "--page-muted": theme.muted,\n        } as React.CSSProperties\n      }\n      className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--page-bg)] text-[var(--page-fg)]"\n    >\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div\n        className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b backdrop-blur"\n        style={{ backgroundColor: "rgba(11,11,13,0.85)", borderColor: theme.border }}\n      >\n        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 py-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">\n            <div\n              className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"\n              style={{ background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${theme.accent}, ${theme.byokColor})` }}\n            >\n              <Database className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">BYOK Models Reference</h1>\n              <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: theme.faint }}>\n                {data ? `${data.totalByok} BYOK models across ${data.groups.filter((g) => g.models.length).length} providers` : "Loading…"}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className="flex-1" />\n\n          {data && (\n            <div\n              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs"\n              style={{\n                borderColor: isStale ? "#b91c1c" : theme.border,\n                backgroundColor: theme.card,\n                color: isStale ? "#fca5a5" : theme.muted,\n              }}\n              title={`Source: ${data.source}\\nFetched: ${data.fetchedAt}`}\n            >\n              {isStale ? <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}\n              <span className="font-mono">{fmtAge(now - new Date(data.fetchedAt).getTime())}</span>\n              <span className="text-zinc-600">·</span>\n              <span style={{ color: data.servedFrom === "live" ? "#86efac" : theme.faint }}>\n                {data.servedFrom}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setRefreshTick((n) => n + 1)}\n            disabled={loading}\n            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-sm font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-50"\n            style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.foreground }}\n          >\n            <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />\n            Refresh\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Search row */}\n        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 pb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">\n          <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[240px]">\n            <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" style={{ color: theme.muted }} />\n            <input\n              type="text"\n              value={filter}\n              onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder="Search model, ID, or provider…"\n              className="w-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"\n              style={{ backgroundColor: theme.card, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.foreground }}\n            />\n          </div>\n          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs cursor-pointer select-none" style={{ color: theme.muted }}>\n            <input\n              type="checkbox"\n              checked={onlyByok}\n              onChange={(e) => setOnlyByok(e.target.checked)}\n              className="rounded border-zinc-600 bg-zinc-800"\n            />\n            Only show byok: IDs\n          </label>\n          <div className="text-xs" style={{ color: theme.faint }}>\n            {filter && `${totalShown} match${totalShown === 1 ? "" : "es"}`}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Body */}\n      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 py-6">\n        {error && (\n          <div className="border rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 flex items-start gap-3" style={{ borderColor: "#7f1d1d", backgroundColor: "#1c0a0a" }}>\n            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-red-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />\n            <div>\n              <div className="font-semibold text-red-300">Failed to load</div>\n              <div className="text-sm text-red-400/80 font-mono mt-1">{error}</div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {loading && !data && (\n          <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20" style={{ color: theme.muted }}>\n            <RefreshCw className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin" />\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {data && filtered.length === 0 && (\n          <div className="text-center py-20" style={{ color: theme.muted }}>\n            No matches for "<span className="font-mono">{filter}</span>".\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {filtered.map((group) => (\n          <section key={group.provider} className="mb-8">\n            <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3 mb-3">\n              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight">{group.provider}</h2>\n              <span className="text-xs font-mono" style={{ color: theme.faint }}>\n                {group.models.length} model{group.models.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}\n              </span>\n              <div className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ backgroundColor: theme.border }} />\n            </div>\n\n            <div\n              className="rounded-xl border overflow-hidden"\n              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, backgroundColor: theme.card }}\n            >\n              <table className="w-full text-sm">\n                <thead>\n                  <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)" }}>\n                    <th\n                      className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider"\n                      style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                    >\n                      Friendly name\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider"\n                      style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                    >\n                      BYOK ID\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className="text-right px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider"\n                      style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                    >\n                      Context\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className="text-right px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider"\n                      style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                    >\n                      Priority\n                    </th>\n                  </tr>\n                </thead>\n                <tbody>\n                  {group.models.map((m) => (\n                    <tr\n                      key={m.byokId}\n                      className="group border-t transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800/40"\n                      style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}\n                    >\n                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium">\n                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                          <span className="font-mono text-[13px]">{m.friendlyName}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      </td>\n                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">\n                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                          <code\n                            className="font-mono text-[12px] truncate max-w-[28rem]"\n                            style={{ color: "#d4d4d8" }}\n                          >\n                            {m.byokId}\n                          </code>\n                          <CopyButton value={m.byokId} />\n                        </div>\n                      </td>\n                      <td\n                        className="px-4 py-2.5 text-right font-mono text-xs"\n                        style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                      >\n                        {fmtContext(m.contextWindow)}\n                      </td>\n                      <td\n                        className="px-4 py-2.5 text-right font-mono text-xs"\n                        style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n                      >\n                        {m.priority != null ? m.priority.toFixed(1) : "—"}\n                      </td>\n                    </tr>\n                  ))}\n                </tbody>\n              </table>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n        ))}\n\n        {/* Footer */}\n        <footer\n          className="mt-12 pt-6 border-t text-xs flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1"\n          style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.faint }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Source: <span className="font-mono">{data?.source ?? "…"}</span>\n          </span>\n          <span>·</span>\n          <span>\n            Cached on server: 5 min\n          </span>\n          <span>·</span>\n          <span>\n            Verified: never (visual check only — copy with care)\n          </span>\n          <span className="flex-1" />\n          <a\n            href="/model-advisor"\n            className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline"\n            style={{ color: theme.muted }}\n          >\n            Open Model Advisor <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />\n          </a>\n        </footer>\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n

### `/career-ops` (page, private)
