```tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";\nimport {\n  Briefcase, FileText, TrendingUp, BarChart3, Plus,\n  Search, RefreshCw, ExternalLink, ChevronDown, ChevronUp,\n  Filter, Download, Eye, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock,\n  ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownRight, Minus, Loader2, Send,\n  Target, Zap, Brain, Shield, Radar, Link2, PlayCircle\n} from "lucide-react";\n\nconst API_BASE = "/api/career-ops";\n\nconst toNumber = (value: unknown): number => {\n  if (typeof value === "number") return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;\n  if (typeof value === "string") {\n    const parsed = parseFloat(value);\n    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;\n  }\n  return 0;\n};\n\ntype PipelineEntry = {\n  url: string;\n  company: string;\n  title: string;\n  source: string;\n  added_at: string;\n};\n\ntype Application = {\n  id: number | string;\n  date: string;\n  company: string;\n  role: string;\n  score: number | string;\n  status: string;\n  pdf: boolean;\n  report: string;\n  url?: string;\n  notes?: string;\n};\n\ntype Overview = {\n  total: number;\n  applied: number;\n  interview: number;\n  offer: number;\n  avgScore: number;\n  pdfRate: number;\n  scoreDistribution: { green: number; yellow: number; red: number };\n  statusBreakdown: Record<string, number>;\n  pipelineFunnel?: Array<{ stage: string; count: number }>;\n  pendingUrls?: number;\n  lastUpdated?: string;\n};\n\ntype ScanHistory = { entries: any[]; last_scan?: string | null };\n\ntype BatchState = { running: boolean; lastRun?: string | null; log?: string };\n\nfunction ScorePill({ score }: { score: number | string }) {\n  const numericScore = toNumber(score);\n  const color = numericScore >= 4 ? "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30" : numericScore >= 3 ? "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30" : "bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/30";\n  return <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${color}`}>{numericScore.toFixed(1)}/5</span>;\n}\n\nexport default function CareerOpsDashboard() {\n  const [overview, setOverview] = useState<Overview | null>(null);\n  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<Application[]>([]);\n  const [pipeline, setPipeline] = useState<PipelineEntry[]>([]);\n  const [scanHistory, setScanHistory] = useState<ScanHistory>({ entries: [] });\n  const [batchState, setBatchState] = useState<BatchState>({ running: false });\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [busyAction, setBusyAction] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [expandedApp, setExpandedApp] = useState<string | number | null>(null);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>("all");\n  const [showPipeline, setShowPipeline] = useState(true);\n\n  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const [overviewRes, appsRes, pipelineRes, historyRes] = await Promise.all([\n        fetch(API_BASE, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }),\n        fetch(`${API_BASE}/applications`, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }),\n        fetch(`${API_BASE}/pipeline`, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }),\n        fetch(`${API_BASE}/scan-history`, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }),\n      ]);\n\n      if (!overviewRes.ok || !appsRes.ok || !pipelineRes.ok || !historyRes.ok) throw new Error("Failed to load dashboard data");\n\n      const overviewData = await overviewRes.json();\n      const appsData = await appsRes.json();\n      const pipelineData = await pipelineRes.json();\n      const historyData = await historyRes.json();\n\n      const normalizedApps = (appsData.applications || []).map((app: Application) => ({ ...app, score: toNumber(app.score) }));\n      setOverview({ ...overviewData, avgScore: toNumber(overviewData.avgScore) });\n      setApplications(normalizedApps);\n      setPipeline(pipelineData.pending || []);\n      setScanHistory(historyData || { entries: [] });\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load data");\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => { loadAll(); }, [loadAll]);\n\n  const runScan = async () => {\n    setBusyAction("scan");\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/scan`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ mode: "apply" }) });\n      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Scan failed");\n      await loadAll();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Scan failed");\n    } finally {\n      setBusyAction(null);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const runBatch = async () => {\n    setBusyAction("batch");\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/batch`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ limit: 5 }) });\n      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Batch failed");\n      const data = await res.json();\n      setBatchState({ running: true, lastRun: new Date().toISOString(), log: data.log });\n      setTimeout(() => loadAll(), 3000);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Batch failed");\n    } finally {\n      setBusyAction(null);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const filteredApplications = applications.filter((app) => filter === "all" ? true : app.status.toLowerCase() === filter.toLowerCase());\n\n  if (loading) {\n    return <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-100 flex items-center justify-center"><div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-zinc-400"><Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" /> Loading Career-Ops…</div></div>;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-100">\n      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/95 backdrop-blur sticky top-0 z-20">\n        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">\n          <div>\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">\n              <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-cyan-950/40"><Briefcase className="h-5 w-5" /></div>\n              <div>\n                <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Career-Ops</h1>\n                <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">BI, Data Analytics, Workflow Automation, AI Productivity</p>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n            <button onClick={loadAll} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-900"><RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" /> Refresh</button>\n            <button onClick={runScan} disabled={busyAction === "scan"} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-950 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-60">{busyAction === "scan" ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Radar className="h-4 w-4" />} Scan</button>\n            <button onClick={runBatch} disabled={busyAction === "batch"} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500/90 text-zinc-950 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-cyan-400 disabled:opacity-60">{busyAction === "batch" ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <PlayCircle className="h-4 w-4" />} Batch</button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-6 space-y-6">\n        {error && <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-800 bg-rose-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-200">{error}</div>}\n\n        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4">\n          {[\n            { label: "Evaluations", value: overview?.total ?? 0, icon: FileText, color: "text-blue-400" },\n            { label: "Applied", value: overview?.applied ?? 0, icon: Send, color: "text-emerald-400" },\n            { label: "Interviews", value: overview?.interview ?? 0, icon: Target, color: "text-violet-400" },\n            { label: "Offers", value: overview?.offer ?? 0, icon: CheckCircle2, color: "text-amber-400" },\n            { label: "Pipeline Queue", value: overview?.pendingUrls ?? pipeline.length ?? 0, icon: Link2, color: "text-cyan-400" },\n          ].map((card) => (\n            <div key={card.label} className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-4">\n              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">\n                <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">{card.label}</p>\n                <card.icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${card.color}`} />\n              </div>\n              <p className="mt-3 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">{card.value}</p>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </section>\n\n        {/* Quick Actions */}\n        <div className="mb-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/80 p-4">\n          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">\n            <div>\n              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-zinc-400">Quick Actions</h2>\n              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">Manual controls for scan, batch, refresh, and reports.</p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">\n            <button\n              onClick={() => { loadAll(); }}\n              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100 transition hover:border-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-700"\n            >\n              <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />\n              Refresh Dashboard\n            </button>\n            <button\n              onClick={runScan}\n              disabled={busyAction === "scan"}\n              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-blue-800 bg-blue-950/50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-blue-100 transition hover:border-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-900/50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"\n            >\n              <Search className="h-4 w-4" />\n              {busyAction === "scan" ? "Running Scan..." : "Run Scan"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              onClick={runBatch}\n              disabled={busyAction === "batch"}\n              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-800 bg-emerald-950/50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-100 transition hover:border-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-900/50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"\n            >\n              <Briefcase className="h-4 w-4" />\n              {busyAction === "batch" ? "Running Batch..." : "Run Batch"}\n            </button>\n            <a\n              href={applications[0]?.report ? `/file/${applications[0].report}` : "#"}\n              className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition ${applications[0]?.report ? "border-violet-800 bg-violet-950/50 text-violet-100 hover:border-violet-700 hover:bg-violet-900/50" : "pointer-events-none border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-500"}`}\n            >\n              <FileText className="h-4 w-4" />\n              Open Latest Report\n            </a>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-6">\n          <div className="xl:col-span-2 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">\n            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">\n              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Applications</h2>\n              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                <select value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)} className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-200">\n                  <option value="all">All</option>\n                  <option value="evaluated">Evaluated</option>\n                  <option value="applied">Applied</option>\n                  <option value="interview">Interview</option>\n                  <option value="offer">Offer</option>\n                  <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>\n                </select>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div className="space-y-3">\n              {filteredApplications.length === 0 ? <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">No applications yet.</div> : filteredApplications.map((app) => (\n                <div key={String(app.id)} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/70 overflow-hidden">\n                  <button onClick={() => setExpandedApp(expandedApp === app.id ? null : app.id)} className="w-full px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left hover:bg-zinc-900/60">\n                    <div className="min-w-0">\n                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">\n                        <h3 className="font-medium text-zinc-100">{app.company}</h3>\n                        <ScorePill score={app.score} />\n                        <span className="text-xs rounded-full border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-0.5 text-zinc-300">{app.status}</span>\n                      </div>\n                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400 truncate">{app.role}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    {expandedApp === app.id ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500" /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500" />}\n                  </button>\n                  {expandedApp === app.id && (\n                    <div className="border-t border-zinc-800 px-4 py-4 text-sm text-zinc-300 space-y-3">\n                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">\n                        <div><p className="text-zinc-500">Date</p><p>{app.date}</p></div>\n                        <div><p className="text-zinc-500">PDF</p><p>{app.pdf ? "Generated" : "Not generated"}</p></div>\n                        <div><p className="text-zinc-500">Report</p><a className="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline" href={`${API_BASE}/report?path=${encodeURIComponent(app.report)}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open report</a></div>\n                      </div>\n                      {app.notes && <p className="text-zinc-400">{app.notes}</p>}\n                      {app.url && <a className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300" href={app.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener"><ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4" /> Open job posting</a>}\n                    </div>\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">\n            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">\n              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Pipeline Queue</h2>\n              <button onClick={() => setShowPipeline((v) => !v)} className="text-sm text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200">{showPipeline ? "Hide" : "Show"}</button>\n            </div>\n            {showPipeline && (\n              <div className="space-y-3 max-h-[720px] overflow-auto pr-1">\n                {pipeline.length === 0 ? <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">No jobs in pipeline.</div> : pipeline.slice(0, 100).map((item, idx) => (\n                  <div key={`${item.url}-${idx}`} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/70 p-3">\n                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">\n                      <div className="min-w-0">\n                        <p className="font-medium text-sm text-zinc-100 line-clamp-2">{item.title}</p>\n                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-400">{item.company}</p>\n                        <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500">{item.source}</p>\n                      </div>\n                      <a href={item.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="text-zinc-500 hover:text-cyan-400"><ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4" /></a>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                ))}\n                {pipeline.length > 100 && <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 pt-2">Showing first 100 of {pipeline.length} queued jobs.</p>}\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </section>\n\n        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-6">\n          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">\n            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-4">Score Distribution</h2>\n            <div className="space-y-4">\n              {[\n                { label: "Strong Match (4.0+)", value: overview?.scoreDistribution?.green ?? 0, color: "bg-emerald-500" },\n                { label: "Medium Fit (3.0–3.9)", value: overview?.scoreDistribution?.yellow ?? 0, color: "bg-amber-500" },\n                { label: "Low Fit (<3.0)", value: overview?.scoreDistribution?.red ?? 0, color: "bg-rose-500" },\n              ].map((row) => {\n                const total = (overview?.total ?? 0) || 1;\n                const width = `${Math.max(6, (row.value / total) * 100)}%`;\n                return (\n                  <div key={row.label}>\n                    <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-zinc-400"><span>{row.label}</span><span>{row.value}</span></div>\n                    <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 overflow-hidden"><div className={`h-full ${row.color}`} style={{ width }} /></div>\n                  </div>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">\n            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-4">Recent Scan Activity</h2>\n            <div className="space-y-3 max-h-[360px] overflow-auto pr-1">\n              {(scanHistory.entries || []).slice(-20).reverse().map((entry, idx) => (\n                <div key={idx} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/70 p-3">\n                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">\n                    <div className="min-w-0">\n                      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-200 truncate">{entry.company || "Unknown"}</p>\n                      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 truncate">{entry.title || entry.url}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">{entry.status || "seen"}</span>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </section>\n\n        <div className="pt-2 text-center">\n          <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-600">Career-Ops v1.1 (Zo Edition) · Adapted from <a href="https://github.com/santifer/career-ops" target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-300 underline">santifer/career-ops</a></p>\n        </div>\n      </main>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n
```

```tsx
import { useEffect } from "react";\n\nexport default function DataExplorerRedirect() {\n  useEffect(() => { window.location.replace("/datasets"); }, []);\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#0a0a0f] text-white">\n      <p className="text-gray-400">Redirecting to Data Explorer...</p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}
```

### `/data/zo-trivia/` (page, private)

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";\nimport { \n  Bar, \n  BarChart, \n  CartesianGrid, \n  XAxis, \n  YAxis, \n  Pie, \n  PieChart, \n  Cell, \n  ResponsiveContainer,\n  Line,\n  LineChart,\n  Tooltip as RechartsTooltip\n} from "recharts";\nimport { \n  Card, \n  CardContent, \n  CardDescription, \n  CardHeader, \n  CardTitle,\n  CardFooter\n} from "@/components/ui/card";\nimport { \n  ChartConfig, \n  ChartContainer, \n  ChartTooltip, \n  ChartTooltipContent,\n  ChartLegend, \n  ChartLegendContent\n} from "@/components/ui/chart";\nimport { \n  Trophy, \n  Users, \n  QuestionMark, \n  Target, \n  TrendingUp, \n  Calendar,\n  Activity\n} from "lucide-react";\n\nconst chartConfig = {\n  plays: {\n    label: "Total Plays",\n    color: "var(--chart-1)",\n  },\n  correct: {\n    label: "Correct Answers",\n    color: "var(--chart-2)",\n  },\n  count: {\n    label: "Questions",\n    color: "var(--chart-3)",\n  },\n  status: {\n    label: "Status",\n  },\n  is_correct: {\n    label: "Correct",\n    color: "var(--chart-2)",\n  },\n  is_incorrect: {\n    label: "Incorrect",\n    color: "var(--chart-5)",\n  }\n} satisfies ChartConfig;\n\nexport default function ZoTriviaDashboard() {\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [playsData, setPlaysData] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [correctnessData, setCorrectnessData] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [leaderboard, setLeaderboard] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    async function fetchData() {\n      try {\n        const [s, p, c, l] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch("/data/zo-trivia/api/query?q=stats").then(r => r.json()),\n          fetch("/data/zo-trivia/api/query?q=plays-by-date").then(r => r.json()),\n          fetch("/data/zo-trivia/api/query?q=correctness-dist").then(r => r.json()),\n          fetch("/data/zo-trivia/api/query?q=leaderboard").then(r => r.json())\n        ]);\n        setStats(s);\n        setPlaysData(p);\n        \n        // Format correctness for pie chart\n        setCorrectnessData(c.map((item: any) => ({\n          status: item.status === 1 ? "Correct" : "Incorrect",\n          count: item.count,\n          fill: item.status === 1 ? "var(--chart-2)" : "var(--chart-5)"\n        })));\n        \n        setLeaderboard(l);\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error("Failed to fetch dashboard data:", err);\n      } finally {\n        setLoading(false);\n      }\n    }\n    fetchData();\n  }, []);\n\n  if (loading) {\n    return (\n      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-400">\n        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">\n          <Activity className="w-12 h-12 animate-pulse text-blue-500" />\n          <p className="text-lg font-medium tracking-tight">Loading Zo Trivia Insights...</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-100 p-6 lg:p-10 space-y-8 font-sans selection:bg-blue-500/30">\n      <header className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-4">\n        <div>\n          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white flex items-center gap-2">\n            <Trophy className="text-yellow-500 w-8 h-8" />\n            Zo Trivia Insights\n          </h1>\n          <p className="text-zinc-400 mt-1 max-w-lg">\n            Analyzing {stats?.totalQuestions} questions from {stats?.dateRange?.start} to {stats?.dateRange?.end}.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className="flex gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 bg-zinc-900/50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-zinc-800">\n          <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />\n          Last Updated: {new Date().toLocaleDateString()}\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* Summary Cards */}\n      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">\n        <StatCard title="Total Plays" value={stats?.totalPlays} icon={<Activity className="w-4 h-4" />} color="text-blue-500" />\n        <StatCard title="Avg Accuracy" value={`${stats?.accuracy}%`} icon={<Target className="w-4 h-4" />} color="text-green-500" />\n        <StatCard title="Unique Players" value={stats?.uniquePlayers} icon={<Users className="w-4 h-4" />} color="text-purple-500" />\n        <StatCard title="Max Streak" value={stats?.maxStreak} icon={<TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4" />} color="text-orange-500" />\n      </div>\n\n      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">\n        {/* Plays Over Time */}\n        <Card className="lg:col-span-2 bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">\n          <CardHeader className="border-b border-zinc-800">\n            <CardTitle className="text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">\n              <TrendingUp className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-500" />\n              Game Activity\n            </CardTitle>\n            <CardDescription className="text-zinc-500">Total plays and correctness by date</CardDescription>\n          </CardHeader>\n          <CardContent className="pt-6">\n            <ChartContainer config={chartConfig} className="min-h-[300px] w-full">\n              <BarChart accessibilityLayer data={playsData}>\n                <CartesianGrid vertical={false} strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#27272a" />\n                <XAxis\n                  dataKey="date"\n                  tickLine={false}\n                  tickMargin={10}\n                  axisLine={false}\n                  tickFormatter={(value) => value.split("-").slice(1).join("/")}\n                  stroke="#71717a"\n                />\n                <ChartTooltip content={<ChartTooltipContent />} />\n                <ChartLegend content={<ChartLegendContent />} />\n                <Bar \n                  dataKey="plays" \n                  fill="var(--color-plays)" \n                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} \n                  name="Total Plays"\n                />\n                <Bar \n                  dataKey="correct" \n                  fill="var(--color-correct)" \n                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} \n                  name="Correct Answers"\n                />\n              </BarChart>\n            </ChartContainer>\n          </CardContent>\n        </Card>\n\n        {/* Accuracy Distribution */}\n        <Card className="bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">\n          <CardHeader className="border-b border-zinc-800">\n            <CardTitle className="text-zinc-100">Accuracy Split</CardTitle>\n            <CardDescription className="text-zinc-500">Global correct vs incorrect answers</CardDescription>\n          </CardHeader>\n          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[300px]">\n            <ChartContainer config={chartConfig} className="mx-auto aspect-square w-full max-w-[250px]">\n              <PieChart>\n                <ChartTooltip content={<ChartTooltipContent hideLabel nameKey="status" />} />\n                <Pie\n                  data={correctnessData}\n                  dataKey="count"\n                  nameKey="status"\n                  innerRadius={60}\n                  strokeWidth={5}\n                  stroke="transparent"\n                />\n              </PieChart>\n            </ChartContainer>\n            <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 mt-4 text-sm">\n              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-[var(--chart-2)]" />\n                <span className="text-zinc-400">Correct</span>\n              </div>\n              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-[var(--chart-5)]" />\n                <span className="text-zinc-400">Incorrect</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </CardContent>\n          <CardFooter className="flex-col border-t border-zinc-800 p-4">\n            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-center w-full">{stats?.accuracy}%</div>\n            <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500 text-center w-full uppercase tracking-tighter">Global Accuracy Rating</div>\n          </CardFooter>\n        </Card>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Leaderboard Table */}\n      <Card className="bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">\n        <CardHeader className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/50">\n          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">\n            <div>\n              <CardTitle className="text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">\n                <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-500" />\n                Top Players\n              </CardTitle>\n              <CardDescription className="text-zinc-500">Ranked by total score and streak</CardDescription>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </CardHeader>\n        <CardContent className="p-0">\n          <div className="overflow-x-auto">\n            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">\n              <thead>\n                <tr className="border-b border-zinc-800 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">\n                  <th className="px-6 py-4 font-medium">Player</th>\n                  <th className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-right">Max Score</th>\n                  <th className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-right">Best Streak</th>\n                  <th className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-right">Accuracy</th>\n                  <th className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-right">Games</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-800/50">\n                {leaderboard.map((player, i) => (\n                  <tr key={player.user_handle} className="hover:bg-zinc-800/30 transition-colors group">\n                    <td className="px-6 py-4">\n                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">\n                        <span className={`flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${\n                          i === 0 ? "bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-500" : \n                          i === 1 ? "bg-zinc-400/20 text-zinc-400" : \n                          i === 2 ? "bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-500" : "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-500"\n                        }`}>\n                          {i + 1}\n                        </span>\n                        <span className="font-semibold text-zinc-200 group-hover:text-white transition-colors">\n                          {player.user_handle}\n                        </span>\n                      </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right font-mono text-blue-400">{player.max_score}</td>\n                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">\n                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-orange-400">\n                        {player.max_streak}\n                        <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" />\n                      </span>\n                    </td>\n                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">\n                      <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">\n                        <span className="text-zinc-200">\n                          {Math.round((player.correct_answers / player.games_played) * 100)}%\n                        </span>\n                        <div className="w-16 h-1 bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">\n                          <div \n                            className="h-full bg-green-500" \n                            style={{ width: `${(player.correct_answers / player.games_played) * 100}%` }}\n                          />\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right text-zinc-400">{player.games_played}</td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n      \n      <footer className="pt-8 text-center text-zinc-600 text-xs">\n        <p>Zo Computer Trivia Dataset Visualization &bull; Zenlyte</p>\n      </footer>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction StatCard({ title, value, icon, color }: { title: string, value: any, icon: React.ReactNode, color: string }) {\n  return (\n    <Card className="bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 shadow-lg group hover:border-zinc-700 transition-all">\n      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">\n        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-tight">{title}</CardTitle>\n        <div className={`${color} bg-zinc-800/50 p-2 rounded-lg`}>{icon}</div>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">{value}</div>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n
```

### `/data/zo-trivia/api/query` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { Database } from "bun:sqlite";\n\nconst DB_PATH = "/home/workspace/Data/zo-trivia/source/trivia.db";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const query = c.req.query("q");\n  \n  try {\n    const db = new Database(DB_PATH, { readonly: true });\n    \n    switch (query) {\n      case "stats": {\n        const totalQuestions = db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM questions").get() as { count: number };\n        const totalPlays = db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM leaderboard").get() as { count: number };\n        const uniquePlayers = db.query("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_handle) as count FROM leaderboard").get() as { count: number };\n        const accuracy = db.query("SELECT ROUND(100.0 * SUM(is_correct) / COUNT(*), 1) as pct FROM leaderboard").get() as { pct: number };\n        const maxStreak = db.query("SELECT MAX(streak) as max FROM leaderboard").get() as { max: number };\n        const dateRange = db.query("SELECT MIN(play_date) as start, MAX(play_date) as end FROM questions").get() as { start: string; end: string };\n        \n        db.close();\n        return c.json({\n          totalQuestions: totalQuestions.count,\n          totalPlays: totalPlays.count,\n          uniquePlayers: uniquePlayers.count,\n          accuracy: accuracy.pct || 0,\n          maxStreak: maxStreak.max || 0,\n          dateRange\n        });\n      }\n      \n      case "plays-by-date": {\n        const rows = db.query(`\n          SELECT strftime("%Y-%m-%d", played_at) as date, COUNT(*) as plays, SUM(is_correct) as correct\n          FROM leaderboard\n          GROUP BY strftime("%Y-%m-%d", played_at)\n          ORDER BY date\n        `).all() as Array<{ date: string; plays: number; correct: number }>;\n        db.close();\n        return c.json(rows);\n      }\n      \n      case "questions-by-date": {\n        const rows = db.query(`\n          SELECT play_date as date, COUNT(*) as count\n          FROM questions\n          GROUP BY play_date\n          ORDER BY date\n        `).all() as Array<{ date: string; count: number }>;\n        db.close();\n        return c.json(rows);\n      }\n      \n      case "correctness-dist": {\n        const rows = db.query(`\n          SELECT is_correct as status, COUNT(*) as count\n          FROM leaderboard\n          GROUP BY is_correct\n        `).all() as Array<{ status: number; count: number }>;\n        db.close();\n        return c.json(rows);\n      }\n      \n      case "leaderboard": {\n        const rows = db.query(`\n          SELECT user_handle, MAX(score) as max_score, MAX(streak) as max_streak,\n                 COUNT(*) as games_played, SUM(is_correct) as correct_answers\n          FROM leaderboard\n          GROUP BY user_handle\n          ORDER BY max_score DESC\n        `).all() as Array<{ user_handle: string; max_score: number; max_streak: number; games_played: number; correct_answers: number }>;\n        db.close();\n        return c.json(rows);\n      }\n      \n      default:\n        db.close();\n        return c.json({ error: "Invalid query. Use: stats, plays-by-date, questions-by-date, correctness-dist, leaderboard" }, 400);\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Query error:", error);\n    return c.json({ error: "Database error" }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/kg-browse` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\nimport { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "fs";\nimport { join } from "path";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  try {\n    const vaultPath = "/home/workspace/Projects/mengram-self-hosted/vault";\n    const files = readdirSync(vaultPath).filter(f => f.endsWith(".md"));\n    \n    const entities: Record<string, any[]> = {};\n    \n    for (const file of files) {\n      try {\n        const content = readFileSync(join(vaultPath, file), "utf-8");\n        const frontmatterMatch = content.match(/^---\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n---/);\n        \n        if (frontmatterMatch) {\n          const fm: Record<string, string> = {};\n          for (const line of frontmatterMatch[1].split("\\n")) {\n            const [key, ...vals] = line.split(":");\n            if (key && vals.length) {\n              fm[key.trim()] = vals.join(":").trim();\n            }\n          }\n          \n          const type = fm.type || "unknown";\n          if (!entities[type]) entities[type] = [];\n          \n          // Extract title from filename or first H1\n          const title = file.replace(/\\.md$/, "");\n          const h1Match = content.match(/^#\\s+(.+)$/m);\n          \n          entities[type].push({\n            name: h1Match ? h1Match[1] : title,\n            file: title,\n            tags: fm.tags?.split(",").map((t: string) => t.trim()) || [],\n            updated: fm.updated,\n            created: fm.created\n          });\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        // Skip malformed files\n      }\n    }\n    \n    // Sort each type alphabetically\n    for (const type in entities) {\n      entities[type].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));\n    }\n    \n    return c.json({ total: files.length, entities });\n  } catch (e) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Failed to read vault" }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/kg-by-type` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const secret = process.env.MENGRAM_API_KEY;\n  const targetType = c.req.query("type");\n  \n  if (!targetType) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Missing type parameter" }, 400);\n  }\n  \n  try {\n    // Search for entities of this type\n    const searchRes = await fetch(`http://localhost:8420/api/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(targetType)}&top_k=50`, {\n      headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${secret}` }\n    });\n    const searchData = await searchRes.json();\n    \n    // Filter results to only entities matching the type\n    const results = (searchData.results || [])\n      .filter((r: any) => r.entity_type?.toLowerCase() === targetType.toLowerCase())\n      .slice(0, 30);\n    \n    return c.json({ type: targetType, count: results.length, entities: results });\n  } catch (e) {\n    return c.json({ error: "Failed to fetch" }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/kg-entity` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const secret = process.env.MENGRAM_API_KEY || "";\n  if (!secret) return c.json({ error: "Server misconfigured" }, 500);\n\n  const name = c.req.query("name");\n  if (!name) return c.json({ error: "Name is required" }, 400);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8420/api/entity/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, {\n      headers: {\n        "Authorization": `Bearer ${secret}`,\n        "Content-Type": "application/json",\n      },\n    });\n    \n    if (!response.ok) {\n      return c.json({ error: `Mengram API error: ${response.status}` }, response.status);\n    }\n    \n    const data = await response.json();\n    return c.json(data);\n  } catch (error: any) {\n    return c.json({ error: `Connection failed: ${error.message}` }, 500);\n  }\n};\n
```

### `/kg-recall` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default async (c: Context) => {\n  const secret = process.env.MENGRAM_API_KEY || "";\n  if (!secret) return c.json({ error: "Server misconfigured" }, 500);\n  const { query, top_k = 5 } = await c.req.json();\n  try {\n    const res = await fetch("http://localhost:8420/api/recall/gated", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        "Authorization": `Bearer ${secret}`,\n        "Content-Type": "application/json"\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ query, top_k })\n    });\n    const data = await res.json();\n    return c.json(data);\n  } catch {\n    return c.json({ error: "Mengram unreachable" }, 502);\n  }\n};
```

```tsx
import { useState, useMemo, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";\nimport { Brain, Zap, DollarSign, Download, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Sparkles, ArrowUpDown, Check, Info, Calculator, Trophy, Star, Target, MessageSquare, Code, BookOpen, Scale, Cpu, Sun, Moon, Bot, X, Filter, Search } from "lucide-react";\n\ninterface ModelEntry { id: string; name: string; provider: string; origin: string; context: number; inputCost: number; outputCost: number; intelligence: number; coding: number; speed: number; throughput: number; isYourModel: boolean; }\n\nconst TASKS = [\n  { id: "coding", label: "Coding", icon: Code },\n  { id: "reasoning", label: "Reasoning", icon: Brain },\n  { id: "general", label: "General", icon: Scale },\n  { id: "longdocs", label: "Long Docs", icon: BookOpen },\n  { id: "lowcost", label: "Budget", icon: DollarSign },\n];\n\nconst BUDGETS = [\n  { id: "tight", label: "Tight", desc: "< $1/M" }, { id: "moderate", label: "Moderate", desc: "$1-5/M" },\n  { id: "flexible", label: "Flexible", desc: "$5-15/M" }, { id: "unlimited", label: "Unlimited", desc: "Best" },\n];\n\nconst SPEEDS = [\n  { id: "any", label: "Any" }, { id: "fast", label: "Fast" }, { id: "medium", label: "Medium+" },\n];\n\nconst CONTEXTS = [\n  { id: "any", label: "Any" }, { id: "small", label: "<200K" }, { id: "medium", label: "200K-500K" }, { id: "large", label: ">500K" },\n];\n\n// Percentile-based scoring using actual AA benchmark data\n// Computes relative ranking across all models, then applies task-weighted preferences\nfunction scoreModels(models: ModelEntry[], task: string, budget: string, speed: string, context: string): (ModelEntry & { score: number })[] {\n  if (models.length === 0) return [];\n\n  // Collect non-zero values for percentile computation\n  const vals = { intelligence: [] as number[], coding: [] as number[], speed: [] as number[], throughput: [] as number[], cost: [] as number[], context: [] as number[] };\n  for (const m of models) {\n    if (m.intelligence > 0) vals.intelligence.push(m.intelligence);\n    if (m.coding > 0) vals.coding.push(m.coding);\n    if (m.speed > 0) vals.speed.push(m.speed);\n    if (m.throughput > 0) vals.throughput.push(m.throughput);\n    const blended = (m.inputCost + m.outputCost) / 2;\n    if (blended > 0) vals.cost.push(blended);\n    if (m.context > 0) vals.context.push(m.context);\n  }\n  // Sort each dimension\n  for (const k of Object.keys(vals) as (keyof typeof vals)[]) vals[k].sort((a, b) => a - b);\n\n  // Percentile rank: 0-100, where 100 = best in that dimension\n  function pctRank(sorted: number[], value: number, higherIsBetter: boolean): number {\n    if (sorted.length === 0 || value <= 0) return 50; // neutral for missing data\n    let below = 0;\n    for (const v of sorted) { if (v < value) below++; else break; }\n    const raw = (below / sorted.length) * 100;\n    return higherIsBetter ? raw : 100 - raw;\n  }\n\n  // Task-based weight profiles: [intelligence, coding, speed, throughput, costEfficiency]\n  const weights: Record<string, [number, number, number, number, number]> = {\n    coding:    [0.10, 0.45, 0.15, 0.10, 0.20],\n    reasoning: [0.70, 0.00, 0.05, 0.05, 0.20],\n    general:   [0.40, 0.05, 0.15, 0.15, 0.25],\n    longdocs:  [0.50, 0.00, 0.10, 0.15, 0.25],\n    lowcost:   [0.20, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.50],\n  };\n  let [wIntel, wCoding, wSpeed, wTput, wCost] = weights[task] || weights.general;\n\n  // Reduce attribute weight based on user "Any/Unlimited" preferences\n  if (speed === "any") {\n    // If coding matters, shift speed weight there, else to intel\n    if (wCoding > 0) wCoding += wSpeed;\n    else wIntel += wSpeed;\n    wSpeed = 0;\n    \n    // Throughput matters less if speed doesn\
```

"border-cyan-400 text-cyan-400"\n          : "border-transparent text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:border-zinc-600"\n      }`}\n    >\n      <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />\n      {label}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {\n  const c = STATUS_COLORS[status] || STATUS_COLORS.planned;\n  return (\n    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-mono tracking-wider uppercase"\n      style={{ background: c.bg, color: c.text, border: `1px solid ${c.text}30` }}>\n      {c.label}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ProjectCard({ project, onVote }: { project: typeof PROJECTS[0]; onVote: () => void }) {\n  const statusColor = STATUS_COLORS[project.status] || STATUS_COLORS.planned;\n  return (\n    <div className="p-6 rounded-xl transition-all group" style={{ background: COLORS.card, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}>\n      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">\n        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">\n            <StatusBadge status={project.status} />\n          </div>\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n            <h3 className="text-white font-semibold text-base">{project.name}</h3>\n            {project.link && (\n              <a href={project.link} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"\n                className="transition-colors" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }}>\n                <ArrowUpRight className="w-4 h-4" />\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          <p className="text-sm mt-1.5 leading-relaxed" style={{ color: COLORS.muted }}>{project.desc}</p>\n          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-3">\n            {project.tags.map((t) => (\n              <span key={t} className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono"\n                style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}15`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>{t}</span>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <button\n          onClick={onVote}\n          className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border transition-all shrink-0"\n          style={{ borderColor: `${COLORS.border}`, background: "transparent" }}\n          onMouseEnter={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = `${COLORS.cyan}60`; (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = `${COLORS.cyan}10`; }}\n          onMouseLeave={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = COLORS.border; (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = "transparent"; }}\n        >\n          <ChevronUp className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} />\n          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">{project.votes}</span>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function Profile() {\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("updates");\n  const [projects, setProjects] = useState(PROJECTS);\n  const [updates, setUpdates] = useState<UpdateItem[]>([]);\n  const [updateLoading, setUpdateLoading] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (activeTab === "updates" && updates.length === 0) {\n      setUpdateLoading(true);\n      fetch("/api/updates?cache=false")\n        .then(r => r.json())\n        .then(d => { setUpdates(d.updates || d.posts || []); setUpdateLoading(false); })\n        .catch(() => setUpdateLoading(false));\n    }\n  }, [activeTab]);\n\n  const handleVote = (idx: number) => {\n    setProjects((prev) => prev.map((p, i) => i === idx ? { ...p, votes: (p as any).votes + 1 } : p));\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: COLORS.bg, color: "white" }}>\n      {/* Cover */}\n      <div className="relative w-full h-48 sm:h-64 md:h-72">\n        <img src="/images/cover-profile.png" alt="Cover" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />\n        <div className="absolute inset-0" style={{\n          background: `linear-gradient(to top, ${COLORS.bg} 0%, ${COLORS.bg}50 30%, transparent 70%)`,\n        }} />\n      </div>\n\n      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">\n        {/* Avatar + Info */}\n        <div className="relative -mt-16 sm:-mt-20 mb-6">\n          <div className="w-28 h-28 sm:w-32 sm:h-32 rounded-full border-4 overflow-hidden"\n            style={{ borderColor: COLORS.bg, background: COLORS.card }}>\n            <img src={PROFILE.avatar} alt={PROFILE.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />\n          </div>\n\n          <div className="mt-4">\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">\n              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-heading font-bold">{PROFILE.name}</h1>\n              <span className="text-sm font-mono" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{PROFILE.handle}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <p className="mt-2 text-base leading-relaxed" style={{ color: COLORS.muted }}>{PROFILE.bio}</p>\n\n            <div className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 mt-3 text-sm">\n              <a href={PROFILE.website} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"\n                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors hover:opacity-80"\n                style={{ color: COLORS.cyan HTMLAnchorElement).style.color = "white"}\n                      onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget as HTMLAnchorElement).style.color = COLORS.dimmed}>\n                      {typeof Icon === "function" ? <Icon /> : <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />}\n                    </a>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-4">\n              {PROFILE.tags.map((t) => (\n                <span key={t} className="px-3 py-1 text-xs font-mono rounded-full"\n                  style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}15`, color: COLORS.indigoLight, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.indigo}30` }}>\n                  {t}\n                </span>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Tabs */}\n        <div className="border-b overflow-x-auto" style={{ borderColor: COLORS.border }}>\n          <div className="flex gap-1">\n            <TabButton label="Updates" icon={Rss} active={activeTab === "updates"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("updates")} />\n            <TabButton label="Blog" icon={BookOpen} active={activeTab === "blog"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("blog")} />\n            <TabButton label="Projects" icon={FolderKanban} active={activeTab === "projects"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("projects")} />\n            <TabButton label="Zo Stack" icon={LayoutGrid} active={activeTab === "stack"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("stack")} />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Tab Content */}\n        <div className="py-6 pb-20">\n          {/* Updates */}\n          {activeTab === "updates" && (\n            <div className="space-y-4">\n              {updateLoading && (\n                <div className="text-sm" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>Loading posts...</div>\n              )}\n              {!updateLoading && updates.length === 0 && (\n                <div className="text-sm" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>\n                  No posts found. Make sure your X/Twitter posts are public.\n                </div>\n              )}\n              {!updateLoading && updates.map((u) => {\n                const isNote = u.type === "note";\n                return (\n                  <div key={u.id} className="rounded-xl p-5 transition-colors"\n                    style={{ background: COLORS.card, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}>\n                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">\n                      <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: COLORS.cardHover }}>\n                        <img src={PROFILE.avatar} alt={PROFILE.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />\n                      </div>\n                      <div>\n                        <span className="text-white text-sm font-medium">Zenlyte</span>\n                        <span className="text-sm ml-2" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{PROFILE.handle}</span>\n                        <span className="text-sm ml-2" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{u.date}</span>\n                        {isNote && (\n                          <span className="ml-2 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono" style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}20`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>\n                            Blog Note\n                          </span>\n                        )}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                    {u.title && (\n                      <h3 className="text-white font-semibold text-sm mb-1">{u.title}</h3>\n                    )}\n                    <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed" style={{ color: COLORS.muted }}>{u.text}</p>\n                    {isNote ? (\n                      <a href={u.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"\n                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-3 text-xs transition-colors hover:text-cyan-400"\n                        style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }}>\n                        Read Note <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />\n                      </a>\n                    ) : (\n                      <div className="flex items-center gap-5 mt-3 text-xs" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>\n                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5                        <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {u.views ?? 0} views\n                        </a>\n                      </div>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-mono" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>\n                      <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />\n                      {new Date(post.date +             <p className="text-sm mt-1.5 leading-relaxed" style={{ color: COLORS.muted }}>{post.excerpt}</p>\n                </a>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          {/*        {/* Zo Stack */}\n          {activeTab === "stack" && (\n            <div className="space-y-8">\n              {ZO_STACK.map((section) => {\n                const Icon = section.icon;\n                return (\n                  <div key={section.category}>\n                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">\n                      <Icon className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} />\n                      <h3 className="text-white font-semibold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider font-mono">{section.category}</h3>\n                    </div>\n                    <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">\n                      {section.items.map((item) => (\n                        <div key={item.name} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 transition-colors"\n                          style={{ background: COLORS.card, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}>\n                          <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ background: COLORS.cyan }} />\n                          <div>\n                            <p className="text-white text-sm font-medium">{item.name}</p>\n                            <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{item.desc}</p>\n                          </div>\n                        </div>\n                      ))}\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    \n      <GlobalNav />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n\n\nfunction GlobalNav({ links = [] }: { links?: any[] }) {\n  const [navOpen, setNavOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [navAuth, setNavAuth] = useState(false);\n  const [navLinks, setNavLinks] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const navRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/nav-links", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" }, credentials: "include" })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { \n        setNavLinks(d.links || []); \n        setNavAuth(d.authenticated); \n      })\n      .catch(() => {});\n      \n    const interval = setInterval(() => {\n      fetch("/api/auth-status", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" }, credentials: "include" })\n        .then(r => r.json())\n        .then(d => setNavAuth(d.authenticated))\n        .catch(() => {});\n    }, 30000);\n    \n    const handleClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (navRef.current && !navRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {\n        setNavOpen(false);\n      }\n    };\n    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);\n    \n    return () => {\n      clearInterval(interval);\n      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const ICON_MAP: Record<string, any> = {\n    "pencil": PenLine,\n    "palette": Palette,\n    "settings": Settings,\n    "layout-dashboard": LayoutDashboard,\n    "sparkles": Sparkles,\n    "share-2": Share2,\n    "clock": Clock,\n    "briefcase": Briefcase,\n  };\n\n  const filteredNavLinks = navLinks.filter(l => l.path !== window.location.pathname);\n\n  const COLORS = {\n    cyan: "#06b6d4",\n    cyanLight: "#22d3ee",\n    indigo: "#6366f1",\n    indigoLight: "#818cf8",\n    muted: "#94a3b8",\n    dimmed: "#64748b",\n    border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)"\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <div className="hidden md:block fixed top-6 right-6 z-[9999]" ref={navRef}>\n        <div className="relative">\n          <button \n            onClick={() => setNavOpen(!navOpen)}\n            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900/50 hover:bg-zinc-800/80 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors border"\n            style={{ borderColor: COLORS.border }}\n          >\n            {navOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} />}\n          </button>\n          \n          {navOpen && (\n            <div className="absolute top-full right-0 mt-3 min-w-[250px] w-max max-w-md rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"\n              style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.95)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)", border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}>\n              {navAuth && (\n                <div className="px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono" style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}`, color: COLORS.cyan }}>\n                  <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />\n                  <span>Authenticated view</span>\n                </div>\n              )}\n              <div className="py-2 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto">\n                {filteredNavLinks.map((link: any) => {\n                  const IconComp = ICON_MAP[link.icon] || ExternalLink;\n                  return (\n                    <a key={link.path} href={link.path}\n                      onClick={() => setNavOpen(false)}\n                      className="flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 group">\n                      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5"\n                        style={{\n                          background: link.category === "private" ? `${COLORS.indigo}15` : `${COLORS.cyan}15`,\n                          border: `1px solid ${link.category === "private" ? `${COLORS.indigo}30` : `${COLORS.cyan}30`}`,\n                        }}>\n                        <IconComp className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: link.category === "private" ? COLORS.indigoLight : COLORS.cyan }} />\n                      </div>\n                      <div className="min-w-0">\n                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white group-hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">{link.name}</span>\n                          {link.category === "private" && (\n                            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider"\n                              style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}20`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>\n                              Private\n                            </span>\n                          )}\n                        </div>\n                        <p className="text-xs mt-0.5 truncate" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{link.description}</p>\n                      </div>\n                    </a>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[9999]">\n        {navOpen && (\n          <div\n            className="absolute bottom-16 right-0 mb-2 min-w-[200px] w-max max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden"\n            style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.98)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)", border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}\n          >\n            {navAuth && (\n              <div className="px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono" style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}`, color: COLORS.cyan }}>\n                <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />\n                <span>Authenticated</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n            <div className="py-2 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">\n              <div className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-mono tracking-widest uppercase" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>\n                Pages\n              </div>\n              {filteredNavLinks.map((link: any) => {\n                const IconComp = ICON_MAP[link.icon] || ExternalLink;\n                return (\n                  <a key={link.path} href={link.path}\n                    onClick={() => setNavOpen(false)}\n                    className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">\n                    <div className="w-6 flex justify-center shrink-0">\n                      <IconComp className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: link.category === "private" ? COLORS.indigoLight : COLORS.cyan }} />\n                    </div>\n                    <span className="truncate">{link.name}</span>\n                    {link.category === "private" && (\n                      <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono"\n                        style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}20`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>\n                        Private\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </a>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n        \n        <button\n          onClick={() => setNavOpen(!navOpen)}\n          className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg transition-transform hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"\n          style={{ background: COLORS.cyan, color: "white", boxShadow: `0 4px 20px -5px ${COLORS.cyan}60` }}\n        >\n          {navOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n

if (mimeType.startsWith("video/")) return <span className="text-3xl">🎬</span>;\n  if (mimeType.startsWith("audio/")) return <span className="text-3xl">🎵</span>;\n  if (mimeType.includes("pdf")) return <span className="text-3xl">📄</span>;\n  if (mimeType.includes("word") || ext === "doc" || ext === "docx") return <span className="text-3xl">📝</span>;\n  if (mimeType.includes("sheet") || ext === "xls" || ext === "xlsx" || ext === "csv") return <span className="text-3xl">📊</span>;\n  if (mimeType.includes("presentation") || ext === "ppt" || ext === "pptx") return <span className="text-3xl">📽️</span>;\n  if (mimeType.includes("zip") || mimeType.includes("tar") || ext === "gz") return <span className="text-3xl">📦</span>;\n  return <span className="text-3xl">📁</span>;\n}\n\nfunction getFileAccent(mimeType: string, fileName: string): string {\n  if (mimeType.startsWith("image/")) return "#818cf8";\n  if (mimeType.includes("pdf")) return "#EF4444";\n  if (mimeType.includes("word") || fileName.endsWith(".doc") || fileName.endsWith(".docx")) return "#3B82F6";\n  if (mimeType.includes("sheet") || fileName.endsWith(".csv")) return "#10B981";\n  if (mimeType.includes("presentation") || fileName.includes("ppt")) return "#F97316";\n  if (mimeType.startsWith("video/")) return "#EC4899";\n  if (mimeType.startsWith("audio/")) return "#06B6D4";\n  if (mimeType.includes("zip") || mimeType.includes("tar")) return "#EAB308";\n  return "#06b6d4";\n}\n\nexport default function SharedFile() {\n  const [share, setShare] = setLoading(false);\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        setNotFound(true);\n        setLoading(false);\n      });\n  }, [shareId]);\n\n  const handleDownload = async () => {\n    if (!share) document.body.removeChild(a);\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n      setDownloaded(true);\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message);\n    }\n    setDownloading(false);\n  };\n\n  if "tar", "gz"].includes(ext)) return <FileIcon className={iconClass + " text-yellow-400"} />;\n  \n  return <FileIcon className={iconClass + " text-zinc-400"} />;\n}\n\nconst COLORS = {\n  bg: credentials: "include" })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { \n        setNavLinks(d.links || []); \n        setNavAuth(d.authenticated); \n      })\n      .catch(() => {});\n      \n ### `/speech-game-manifest.json` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  const manifest = {\n    name: "S-Blend Speech Game",\n    short_name: "S-Blends",\n    description: "Speech therapy practice game for s-blend words",\n    start_url: "/speech-game",\n    scope: "/speech-game",\n    display: "standalone",\n    orientation: "portrait",\n    theme_color: "#7c3aed",\n    background_color: "#eff6ff",\n    icons: [\n      { src: "/icons/speech-game-192.png", sizes: "192x192", type: "image/png", purpose: "any maskable" },\n      { src: "/icons/speech-game-512.png", sizes: "512x512", type: "image/png", purpose: "any maskable" },\n    ],\n    categories: ["education", "kids"],\n  };\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(manifest), {\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": "application/manifest+json",\n      "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=3600",\n    },\n  });\n};\n
```

### `/speech-game-sw.js` (api, private)

```ts
import type { Context } from "hono";\n\nconst SW_CODE = `\nconst CACHE_NAME = "sblend-v1";\nconst PRECACHE = [\n  "/speech-game",\n  "/speech-game/stickers",\n  "/icons/speech-game-192.png",\n  "/icons/speech-game-512.png",\n];\n\nself.addEventListener("install", (e) => {\n  e.waitUntil(\n    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => cache.addAll(PRECACHE)).then(() => self.skipWaiting())\n  );\n});\n\nself.addEventListener("activate", (e) => {\n  e.waitUntil(\n    caches.keys().then((keys) =>\n      Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE_NAME).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))\n    ).then(() => self.clients.claim())\n  );\n});\n\nself.addEventListener("fetch", (e) => {\n  const url = new URL(e.request.url);\n  if (url.pathname.startsWith("/api/")) return;\n  if (e.request.method !== "GET") return;\n  e.respondWith(\n    fetch(e.request)\n      .then((res) => {\n        if (res.ok) {\n          const clone = res.clone();\n          caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => cache.put(e.request, clone));\n        }\n        return res;\n      })\n      .catch(() => caches.match(e.request))\n  );\n});\n`;\n\nexport default (c: Context) => {\n  return new Response(SW_CODE.trim(), {\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": "application/javascript",\n      "Service-Worker-Allowed": "/",\n      "Cache-Control": "no-cache",\n    },\n  });\n};\n
```

### `/speech-game/stats` (page, private)
