```tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";\nimport { Lock, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";\n\nconst AUTH_KEY = "sblend-auth";\n\ninterface Attempt {\n  id: string; word: string; phrase: string; position: "first" | "second";\n  round: number; tries: number; date: string; time: string; timestamp: number;\n}\n\ninterface StickerInfo { sticker: string; appliedAt: string }\ninterface ServerData { attempts: Attempt[]; stickers: { [date: string]: StickerInfo } }\n\nconst WORD_EMOJIS: { [k: string]: string } = {\n  smell: "🌸", sneeze: "🤧", swing: "🎠", small: "🐜", stop: "🛑", spoon: "🥄",\n  swim: "🏊", star: "⭐", smile: "😊", snack: "🍪", snow: "❄️", space: "🚀",\n};\n\nconst COLORS = [\n  "#8b5cf6","#3b82f6","#10b981","#f59e0b","#ef4444","#ec4899",\n  "#06b6d4","#84cc16","#f97316","#6366f1","#14b8a6","#e11d48",\n];\n\nfunction BarChart({ data, maxVal, title, color }: {\n  data: { label: string; value: number; sublabel?: string }[];\n  maxVal: number; title: string; color: string;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-purple-100">\n      <h3 className="font-black text-gray-700 text-sm sm:text-base mb-3">{title}</h3>\n      <div className="space-y-2">\n        {data.map((d, i) => (\n          <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">\n            <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-600 w-16 sm:w-20 truncate text-right">{d.label}</span>\n            <div className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-5 sm:h-6 overflow-hidden">\n              <div className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 flex items-center justify-end pr-2"\n                style={{ width: `${maxVal > 0 ? Math.max((d.value / maxVal) * 100, 8) : 8}%`, backgroundColor: color || COLORS[i % COLORS.length] }}>\n                <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-white drop-shadow">{d.value}</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            {d.sublabel && <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 w-10">{d.sublabel}</span>}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction DonutChart({ segments, centerText, centerSub, size }: {\n  segments: { value: number; color: string; label: string }[];\n  centerText: string; centerSub: string; size?: number;\n}) {\n  const s = size || 120;\n  const total = segments.reduce((a, b) => a + b.value, 0);\n  let cumulative = 0;\n  const strokes = segments.map(seg => {\n    const pct = total > 0 ? (seg.value / total) * 100 : 0;\n    const dash = `${pct} ${100 - pct}`;\n    const rotate = cumulative * 3.6;\n    cumulative += pct;\n    return { ...seg, dash, rotate, pct };\n  });\n  const r = 40;\n  const circ = 2 * Math.PI * r;\n\n  return (\n    <div className="flex flex-col items-center">\n      <div className="relative" style={{ width: s, height: s }}>\n        <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className="w-full h-full -rotate-90">\n          <circle cx="50" cy="50" r={r} fill="none" stroke="#f3f4f6" strokeWidth="14" />\n          {strokes.map((seg, i) => (\n            seg.value > 0 && <circle key={i} cx="50" cy="50" r={r} fill="none"\n              stroke={seg.color} strokeWidth="14"\n              strokeDasharray={`${(seg.value / total) * circ} ${circ}`}\n              strokeDashoffset={`${-strokes.slice(0, i).reduce((a, b) => a + (b.value / total) * circ, 0)}`}\n              className="transition-all duration-700" />\n          ))}\n        </svg>\n        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">\n          <span className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-gray-800">{centerText}</span>\n          <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">{centerSub}</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 mt-2 justify-center">\n        {segments.map((seg, i) => (\n          <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1">\n            <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: seg.color }} />\n            <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-600">{seg.label} ({seg.value})</span>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function StatsPage() {\n  const [isAuth, setIsAuth] = useState(false);\n  const [passcode, setPasscode] = useState("");\n  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState("");\n  const [data, setData] = useState<ServerData | null>(null);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [dateRange, setDateRange] = useState<"all" | "7d" | "30d">("all");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTH_KEY);\n    if (saved) {\n      fetch("/api/speech-game-auth", { headers: { "X-Passcode": saved } })\n        .then(r => { if (r.ok) { setIsAuth(true); setPasscode(saved); } else localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); setAuthLoading(false); })\n        .catch(() => setAuthLoading(false));\n    } else setAuthLoading(false);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!isAuth) return;\n    const pc = localStorage.getItem(AUTH_KEY) || "";\n    fetch("/api/speech-game-data", { headers: { "X-Passcode": pc } })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { setData(d); setLoading(false); })\n      .catch(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [isAuth]);\n\n  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault(); if (!passcode) return; setAuthLoading(true); setAuthError("");\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch("/api/speech-game-auth", { headers: { "X-Passcode": passcode } });\n      if (res.ok) { localStorage.setItem(AUTH_KEY, passcode); setIsAuth(true); } else setAuthError("Wrong passcode");\n    } catch { setAuthError("Connection error"); }\n    setAuthLoading(false);\n  };\n\n  if (authLoading) {\n    return (<div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-green-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 flex items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-green-500" /></div>);\n  }\n  if (!isAuth) {\n    return (\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-green-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">\n        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-xl w-full max-w-sm space-y-6">\n          <div className="flex justify-center"><div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-green-600" /></div></div>\n          <div className="text-center"><h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700">Speech Stats</h2><p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Enter the passcode to view</p></div>\n          {authError && <p className="text-sm text-center text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg">{authError}</p>}\n          <input type="password" value={passcode} onChange={e => setPasscode(e.target.value)}\n            className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-green-200 text-center text-lg tracking-widest focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none" placeholder="••••••" autoFocus />\n          <button type="submit" disabled={authLoading || !passcode}\n            className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-teal-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shadow disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center">\n            {authLoading ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : "Unlock 🔓"}\n          </button>\n        </form>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (loading || !data) {\n    return (<div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-green-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 flex items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-green-500" /></div>);\n  }\n\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const cutoff = dateRange === "7d" ? now - 7 * 86400000 : dateRange === "30d" ? now - 30 * 86400000 : 0;\n  const attempts = data.attempts.filter(a => (a.timestamp || 0) >= cutoff);\n\n  const totalAttempts = attempts.length;\n  const totalTries = attempts.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.tries || 1), 0);\n  const totalRetries = totalTries - totalAttempts;\n  const overallAccuracy = totalTries > 0 ? Math.round((totalAttempts / totalTries) * 100) : 0;\n  const uniqueDays = new Set(attempts.map(a => a.date)).size;\n  const totalStickers = Object.keys(data.stickers).length;\n\n  const stickerDays = Object.keys(data.stickers).sort();\n  const todayStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-CA");\n\n  // Position stats\n  const firstCount = attempts.filter(a => a.position === "first").length;\n  const secondCount = attempts.filter(a => a.position === "second").length;\n  const firstTries = attempts.filter(a => a.position === "first").reduce((s, a) => s + (a.tries || 1), 0);\n  const secondTries = attempts.filter(a => a.position === "second").reduce((s, a) => s + (a.tries || 1), 0);\n  const firstAccuracy = firstTries > 0 ? Math.round((firstCount / firstTries) * 100) : 0;\n  const secondAccuracy = secondTries > 0 ? Math.round((secondCount / secondTries) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // Per-word stats\n  const wordStats: { [w: string]: { attempts: number; tries: number; firstTries: number; secondTries: number; firstCount: number; secondCount: number } } = {};\n  const allWords = Object.keys(WORD_EMOJIS);\n  allWords.forEach(w => { wordStats[w] = { attempts: 0, tries: 0, firstTries: 0, secondTries: 0, firstCount: 0, secondCount: 0 }; });\n  attempts.forEach(a => {\n    if (!wordStats[a.word]) wordStats[a.word] = { attempts: 0, tries: 0, firstTries: 0, secondTries: 0, firstCount: 0, secondCount: 0 };\n    wordStats[a.word].attempts++;\n    wordStats[a.word].tries += a.tries || 1;\n    if (a.position === "first") { wordStats[a.word].firstTries += a.tries || 1; wordStats[a.word].firstCount++; }\n    else { wordStats[a.word].secondTries += a.tries || 1; wordStats[a.word].secondCount++; }\n  });\n\n  const wordsByRetries = [...allWords].sort((a, b) => (wordStats[b]?.tries || 0) - (wordStats[a]?.tries || 0));\n  const wordsByAccuracy = [...allWords].sort((a, b) => {\n    const accA = wordStats[a]?.tries > 0 ? (wordStats[a].attempts / wordStats[a].tries) : 1;\n    const accB = wordStats[b]?.tries > 0 ? (wordStats[b].attempts / wordStats[b].tries) : 1;\n    return accA - accB;\n  });\n\n  const maxWordTries = Math.max(...allWords.map(w => wordStats[w]?.tries || 0), 1);\n\n  // Sticker streaks\n  let currentStickerStreak = 0;\n  let bestStickerStreak = 0;\n  let rollingStickerStreak = 0;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < stickerDays.length; i++) {\n    if (i === 0) {\n      rollingStickerStreak = 1;\n    } else {\n      const prev = new Date(stickerDays[i - 1] + "T12:00:00");\n      const curr = new Date(stickerDays[i] + "T12:00:00");\n      const diff = Math.round((curr.getTime() - prev.getTime()) / 86400000);\n      rollingStickerStreak = diff === 1 ? rollingStickerStreak + 1 : 1;\n    }\n    bestStickerStreak = Math.max(bestStickerStreak, rollingStickerStreak);\n  }\n\n  if (stickerDays.length > 0) {\n    const lastSticker = new Date(stickerDays[stickerDays.length - 1] + "T12:00:00");\n    const today = new Date(todayStr + "T12:00:00");\n    const daysSinceLastSticker = Math.round((today.getTime() - lastSticker.getTime()) / 86400000);\n\n    if (daysSinceLastSticker <= 1) {\n      currentStickerStreak = rollingStickerStreak;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Recent activity (last 7 days) counts total tries, not completed answers\n  const last7 = Array.from({ length: 7 }).map((_, i) => {\n    const d = new Date(now - (6 - i) * 86400000);\n    const ds = d.toLocaleDateString("en-CA");\n    const dayAttempts = data.attempts.filter(a => a.date === ds);\n    const dayLabel = d.toLocaleDateString("en-CA", { weekday: "short" });\n    const tries = dayAttempts.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.tries || 1), 0);\n    return { label: dayLabel, value: tries };\n  });\n  const maxDaily = Math.max(...last7.map(d => d.value), 1);\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-green-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 p-2 sm:p-4 pb-24">\n      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">\n        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">\n          <a href="/speech-game" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm hover:underline">← Game</a>\n          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-green-700">Speech Stats 📊</h1>\n          <a href="/speech-game/stickers" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm hover:underline">Stickers ⭐</a>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Date range filter */}\n        <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mb-4">\n          {(["all", "7d", "30d"] as const).map(r => (\n            <button key={r} onClick={() => setDateRange(r)}\n              className={`px-3 sm:px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-all\n                ${dateRange === r ? "bg-green-500 text-white shadow" : "bg-white text-gray-600 border border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-50"}`}>\n              {r === "all" ? "All Time" : r === "7d" ? "7 Days" : "30 Days"}\n            </button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n\n        {totalAttempts === 0 ? (\n          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-sm text-center">\n            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">🎯</div>\n            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-700">No data yet!</h2>\n            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Play the game to see your stats here.</p>\n            <a href="/speech-game" className="inline-block mt-4 bg-green-500 text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-green-600 transition">\n              Play Now 🎮\n            </a>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            {/* Summary cards */}\n            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-4">\n              {[\n                { emoji: "🎯", label: "Words Done", value: totalAttempts, color: "from-purple-400 to-indigo-400" },\n                { emoji: "🔄", label: "Retries", value: totalRetries, color: "from-orange-400 to-red-400" },\n                { emoji: "📐", label: "Accuracy", value: `${overallAccuracy}%`, color: "from-green-400 to-emerald-400" },\n                { emoji: "📅", label: "Days Played", value: uniqueDays, color: "from-blue-400 to-cyan-400" },\n              ].map((c, i) => (\n                <div key={i} className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${c.color} rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 text-white text-center shadow`}>\n                  <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl">{c.emoji}</div>\n                  <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black mt-1">{c.value}</div>\n                  <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs opacity-80 font-semibold">{c.label}</div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Streak + stickers */}\n            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-4">\n              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-green-100 text-center">\n                <div className="text-3xl">🔥</div>\n                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-orange-600">{currentStickerStreak}</div>\n                <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">Current Sticker Streak</div>\n              </div>\n              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-green-100 text-center">\n                <div className="text-3xl">🏆</div>\n                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-600">{bestStickerStreak}</div>\n                <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">Best Sticker Streak</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 mb-4">\n              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-green-100 text-center">\n                <div className="text-3xl">⭐</div>\n                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-yellow-600">{totalStickers}</div>\n                <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">Stickers Earned</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Position comparison donut */}\n            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-purple-100 mb-4">\n              <h3 className="font-black text-gray-700 text-sm sm:text-base mb-3 text-center">Word Position: First vs Second</h3>\n              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-around gap-4">\n                <DonutChart\n                  segments={[\n                    { value: firstCount, color: "#8b5cf6", label: "1st in phrase" },\n                    { value: secondCount, color: "#3b82f6", label: "2nd in phrase" },\n                  ]}\n                  centerText={`${totalAttempts}`}\n                  centerSub="total picks"\n                />\n                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">\n                  <div className="bg-purple-50 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">\n                    <div className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-600">Word FIRST</div>\n                    <div className="text-lg font-black text-purple-800">{firstCount} picks</div>\n                    <div className="text-xs text-purple-500">{firstAccuracy}% accuracy</div>\n                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{firstTries} total tries</div>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className="bg-blue-50 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">\n                    <div className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600">Word SECOND</div>\n                    <div className="text-lg font-black text-blue-800">{secondCount} picks</div>\n                    <div className="text-xs text-blue-500">{secondAccuracy}% accuracy</div>\n                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{secondTries} total tries</div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Accuracy by position per word */}\n            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-purple-100 mb-4">\n              <h3 className="font-black text-gray-700 text-sm sm:text-base mb-3">Position Accuracy by Word</h3>\n              <div className="space-y-2">\n                {allWords.map((w, i) => {\n                  const ws = wordStats[w];\n                  const fAcc = ws.firstTries > 0 ? Math.round((ws.firstCount / ws.firstTries) * 100) : -1;\n                  const sAcc = ws.secondTries > 0 ? Math.round((ws.secondCount / ws.secondTries) * 100) : -1;\n                  return (\n                    <div key={w} className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                      <span className="text-base">{WORD_EMOJIS[w]}</span>\n                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 w-12 sm:w-14 truncate">{w}</span>\n                      <div className="flex-1 flex gap-1">\n                        <div className="flex-1 bg-purple-50 rounded-full h-4 overflow-hidden">\n                          <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-purple-400 transition-all duration-500"\n                            style={{ width: fAcc >= 0 ? `${fAcc}%` : "0%", opacity: fAcc >= 0 ? 1 : 0.2 }} />\n                        </div>\n                        <div className="flex-1 bg-blue-50 rounded-full h-4 overflow-hidden">\n                          <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-400 transition-all duration-500"\n                            style={{ width: sAcc >= 0 ? `${sAcc}%` : "0%", opacity: sAcc >= 0 ? 1 : 0.2 }} />\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div className="flex gap-2 text-[10px] w-20 text-right">\n                        <span className="text-purple-600 font-bold">{fAcc >= 0 ? `${fAcc}%` : "-"}</span>\n                        <span className="text-blue-600 font-bold">{sAcc >= 0 ? `${sAcc}%` : "-"}</span>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n              <div className="flex justify-center gap-4 mt-2">\n                <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-purple-400" /><span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">1st pos</span></div>\n                <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-blue-400" /><span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">2nd pos</span></div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Most retries */}\n            <BarChart\n              title="Most Retries by Word 🔄"\n              data={wordsByRetries.slice(0, 6).map(w => ({\n                label: `${WORD_EMOJIS[w]} ${w}`,\n                value: (wordStats[w]?.tries || 0) - (wordStats[w]?.attempts || 0),\n                sublabel: `${wordStats[w]?.attempts || 0} done`,\n              }))}\n              maxVal={Math.max(...wordsByRetries.slice(0, 6).map(w => (wordStats[w]?.tries || 0) - (wordStats[w]?.attempts || 0)), 1)}\n              color="#f97316"\n            />\n\n            {/* Best words */}\n            <div className="mt-4">\n              <BarChart\n                title="Best Words (Highest Accuracy) 🌟"\n                data={wordsByAccuracy.reverse().slice(0, 6).map(w => {\n                  const ws = wordStats[w];\n                  const acc = ws.tries > 0 ? Math.round((ws.attempts / ws.tries) * 100) : 0;\n                  return { label: `${WORD_EMOJIS[w]} ${w}`, value: acc, sublabel: `${ws.attempts} done` };\n                })}\n                maxVal={100}\n                color="#10b981"\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Worst words */}\n            <div className="mt-4">\n              <BarChart\n                title="Words to Practice More 📝"\n                data={wordsByAccuracy.reverse().slice(0, 6).map(w => {\n                  const ws = wordStats[w];\n                  const acc = ws.tries > 0 ? Math.round((ws.attempts / ws.tries) * 100) : 0;\n                  return { label: `${WORD_EMOJIS[w]} ${w}`, value: acc, sublabel: `${ws.attempts} done` };\n                })}\n                maxVal={100}\n                color="#ef4444"\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Per-word detail grid */}\n            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-purple-100 mt-4">\n              <h3 className="font-black text-gray-700 text-sm sm:text-base mb-3">Word Detail Grid</h3>\n              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 landscape:grid-cols-4 gap-2">\n                {allWords.map((w, i) => {\n                  const ws = wordStats[w];\n                  const acc = ws.tries > 0 ? Math.round((ws.attempts / ws.tries) * 100) : 0;\n                  const retries = ws.tries - ws.attempts;\n                  return (\n                    <div key={w} className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 sm:p-3 border-2 ${acc >= 80 ? "border-green-300 bg-green-50" : acc >= 50 ? "border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50" : acc > 0 ? "border-red-300 bg-red-50" : "border-gray-200 bg-gray-50"}`}>\n                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">\n                        <span className="text-lg">{WORD_EMOJIS[w]}</span>\n                        <span className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800">{w}</span>\n                      </div>\n                      <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 space-y-0.5">\n                        <div>Done: <span className="font-bold text-gray-700">{ws.attempts}</span></div>\n                        <div>Accuracy: <span className={`font-bold ${acc >= 80 ? "text-green-600" : acc >= 50 ? "text-yellow-600" : "text-red-600"}`}>{acc}%</span></div>\n                        <div>Retries: <span className="font-bold text-orange-600">{retries}</span></div>\n                        <div className="flex gap-1 text-[9px]">\n                          <span className="text-purple-500">1st:{ws.firstCount}</span>\n                          <span className="text-blue-500">2nd:{ws.secondCount}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Activity chart - last 7 days */}\n            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-4 shadow-sm border border-purple-100 mt-4">\n              <h3 className="font-black text-gray-700 text-sm sm:text-base mb-3">Last 7 Days Total Tries</h3>\n              <div className="flex items-end gap-1 sm:gap-2 h-28 sm:h-32">\n                {last7.map((d, i) => (\n                  <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-end h-full">\n                    <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">{d.value > 0 ? d.value : ""}</span>\n                    <div className="w-full rounded-t-lg transition-all duration-500 bg-gradient-to-t from-blue-400 to-purple-400"\n                      style={{ height: `${maxDaily > 0 ? (d.value / maxDaily) * 80 : 0}%`, minHeight: d.value > 0 ? 8 : 2, opacity: d.value > 0 ? 1 : 0.2 }} />\n                    <span className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">{d.label}</span>\n                  </div>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <div className="mt-6 text-center flex justify-center gap-4">\n          <a href="/speech-game" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm hover:underline">← Game</a>\n          <a href="/speech-game/stickers" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm hover:underline">Stickers ⭐</a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n
```

### `/speech-game/stickers` (page, private)

```tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";\nimport { Lock, Loader2, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";\n\nconst STICKER_KEY = "sblend-sticker-chart";\nconst AUTH_KEY = "sblend-auth";\nconst WORDS = ["smell","sneeze","swing","small","stop","spoon","swim","star","smile","snack","snow","space"];\nconst STICKERS = formatDateKey(d: Date) {\n  const y = d.getFullYear();\n  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");\n  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");\n  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;\n}\nfunction formatDisplay(dStr: string) {\n  const [y, m, day] = dStr.split("-").map(Number);\n  return new Date(y, m - 1, day).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { weekday: "short", month: "short", day: "numeric" });\n}\nfunction today() { return formatDateKey(new Date()); }\n\nexport default function Stickers() {\n  const [authed, setAuthed] = useState(false);\n  const [passcode, setPasscode] = useState("");\n  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState("");\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [activeStickers, setActiveStickers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n  const [completedDates, setCompletedDates] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});\n  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [selectedSticker, setSelectedSticker] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [viewYear, setViewYear] = useState(new Date().getFullYear());\n  const [viewMonth, setViewMonth] = useState(new Date().getMonth());\n\n  const verify = async (code: string) => {\n    const res = await fetch("/api/speech-game-auth", {\n      method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Passcode": code },\n    });\n    return res.ok;\n  };\n\n  const loadServerData = async (code: string) => {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch("/api/speech-game-data", {\n        headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${code}`, "X-Passcode": code, "Accept": "application/json" },\n      });\n      if (!res.ok) return;\n      const data = await res.json();\n      setActiveStickers(data.stickers || {});\n\n      const byDate: Record<string, boolean> = {};\n      const attempts: any[] = data.attempts || [];\n      const dates = [...new Set(attempts.map((a: any) => a.date))];\n      for (const dt of dates) {\n        const dayAttempts = attempts.filter((a: any) => a.date === dt);\n        const wordRounds: Record<string, Set<number>> = {};\n        for (const a of dayAttempts) {\n          if (!wordRounds[a.word]) wordRounds[a.word] = new Set();\n          wordRounds[a.word].add(a.round);\n        }\n        const allComplete = WORDS.every(w => {\n          const rounds = wordRounds[w];\n          return rounds && rounds.has(1) && rounds.has(2);\n        });\n        if (allComplete) byDate[dt] = true;\n      }\n      setCompletedDates(byDate);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  const login = async () => {\n    if (!passcode) return;\n    setLoading(true); \n    setAuthError("");\n    \n    const ok = await verify(passcode);\n    if (ok) {\n      localStorage.setItem(AUTH_KEY, passcode);\n      setAuthed(true);\n      await loadServerData(passcode);\n    } else {\n      setAuthError("Wrong passcode");\n    }\n    setLoading(false);\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const saved = localStorage.getItem("sblend-auth");\n    if (saved) {\n      setPasscode(saved);\n      setAuthed(true);\n      loadServerData(saved);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const applySticker = async (date: string) => {\n    if (!selectedSticker) return;\n    setSaving(true);\n    const updated = { ...activeStickers, [date]: selectedSticker };\n    setActiveStickers(updated);\n    localStorage.setItem(STICKER_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));\n    await fetch("/api/speech-game-data", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Passcode": passcode },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "saveStickers", stickers: updated }),\n    });\n    setSelectedDate(null); setSelectedSticker(null);\n    setSaving(false);\n  };\n\n  const removeSticker = async (date: string) => {\n    const updated = { ...activeStickers };\n    delete updated[date];\n    setActiveStickers(updated);\n    localStorage.setItem(STICKER_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));\n    await fetch("/api/speech-game-data", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Passcode": passcode },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "saveStickers", stickers: updated }),\n    });\n    setSelectedDate(null);\n  };\n\n  const firstDay = new Date(viewYear, viewMonth, 1);\n  const lastDay = new Date(viewYear, viewMonth + 1, 0);\n  const daysInMonth = lastDay.getDate();\n  const startDow = firstDay.getDay();\n  const monthDays: string[] = [];\n  for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {\n    monthDays.push(formatDateKey(new Date(viewYear, viewMonth, d)));\n  }\n\n  const prevMonth = () => {\n    if (viewMonth === 0) { setViewMonth(11); setViewYear(viewYear - 1); }\n    else setViewMonth(viewMonth - 1);\n  };\n  const nextMonth = () => {\n    if (viewMonth === 11) { setViewMonth(0); setViewYear(viewYear + 1); }\n    else setViewMonth(viewMonth + 1);\n  };\n  const monthName = new Date(viewYear, viewMonth).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });\n  const todayStr = today();\n\n  if (!authed) {\n    return (\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-pink-50 to-purple-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4">\n        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-8 max-w-sm w-full text-center">\n          <div className="text-5xl mb-4">🏆</div>\n          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-purple-700 mb-2">Reward Chart</h1>\n          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-6">Enter your passcode to access the reward chart</p>\n          <input type="password" value={passcode} onChange={(e: any) => setPasscode(e.target.value)}\n            onKeyDown={(e: any) => e.key === "Enter" && login()}\n            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-purple-200 rounded-xl text-center text-lg tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"\n            placeholder="••••" maxLength={8} />\n          {authError && <p className="mt-3 text-red-500 text-sm font-bold">{authError}</p>}\n          <button onClick={login} disabled={loading}\n            className="mt-4 w-full bg-purple-600 text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-purple-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50">\n            {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : "Enter"}\n          </button>\n          <div className="mt-6 text-center">\n            <a href="/speech-game" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-sm hover:underline">← Back to Game</a>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-pink-50 to-purple-100 p-4 sm:p-6">\n      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">\n        <div className="text-center mb-4">\n          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-purple-700">🏆 Reward Chart</h1>\n          <p className="text-gray-500 text-xs sm:text-sm mt-1">Tap any past day or today to add a sticker. Future dates are blocked.</p>\n          <div className="flex justify-center gap-3 mt-3 text-xs sm:text-sm">\n            <a href="/speech-game" className="text-purple-600 font-bold hover:underline">← Game</a>\n            <a href="/speech-game/stats" className="text-purple-600 font-bold hover:underline">Stats →</a>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Legend */}\n        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 justify-center mb-3 text-xs">\n          <span className="px-2 py-1 bg-green-100 text-green-700 rounded-full font-bold">✅ Completed</span>\n          <span className="px-2 py-1 bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 rounded-full font-bold">⭐ Sticker</span>\n          <span className="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-50 text-blue-500 rounded-full font-bold">📅 Today</span>\n          <span className="px-2 py-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 rounded-full font-bold">🚫 Future blocked</span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Month navigation */}\n        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1">\n          <button onClick={prevMonth} className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white shadow hover:bg-purple-50 transition-colors">\n            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4 sm:w-5 sm:h-5 text-purple-600" />\n          </button>\n          <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-purple-700">{monthName}</h2>\n          <button onClick={nextMonth} className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white shadow hover:bg-purple-50 transition-colors">\n            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 sm:w-5 sm:h-5 text-purple-600" />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Calendar grid - MOBILE RESPONSIVE */}\n        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-lg p-2 sm:p-4">\n          <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1">\n            {["S","M","T","W","T","F","S"].map((d, i) => (\n              <div key={i} className="text-center text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 py-1">{d}</div>\n            ))}\n            {Array.from({ length: startDow }).map((_, i) => (\n              <div key={`off-${i}`} />\n            ))}\n            {monthDays.map(dateStr => {\n              const isComplete = !!completedDates[dateStr];\n              const hasSticker = !!activeStickers[dateStr];\n              const isToday = dateStr === todayStr;\n              const isSelected = selectedDate === dateStr;\n              const dayNum = parseInt(dateStr.split("-")[2]);\n              const isFuture = dateStr > todayStr;\n              const canSelect = !isFuture;\n              \n              let cls = "aspect-square flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ";\n              if (hasSticker) cls += "bg-purple-100 border-2 border-purple-400 cursor-pointer ";\n              else if (isComplete) cls += "bg-green-100 border-2 border-green-400 cursor-pointer ";\n              else if (isToday) cls += "bg-blue-50 border-2 border-blue-300 cursor-pointer ";\n              else if (isFuture) cls += "bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed ";\n              else cls += "bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 cursor-pointer hover:border-purple-300 ";\n              if (isSelected) cls += "ring-2 ring-yellow-400 scale-105 ";\n              \n              return (\n                <div key={dateStr} className={cls}\n                  onClick={() => {\n                    if (canSelect) {\n                      setSelectedDate(dateStr);\n                      setSelectedSticker(activeStickers[dateStr] || null);\n                    }\n                  }}\n                  title={formatDisplay(dateStr)}>\n                  <span className="text-base sm:text-lg">{activeStickers[dateStr] || (isComplete ? "✅" : isToday ? "📅" : isFuture ? "🚫" : "⭐")}</span>\n                  <span className="text-gray-400 text-[10px] sm:text-xs">{dayNum}</span>\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Summary */}\n        <div className="mt-3 text-center text-xs text-gray-400">\n          {Object.keys(completedDates).length} completed · {Object.keys(activeStickers).length} stickers\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Sticker picker modal */}\n        {selectedDate && (\n          <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center z-50" \n               onClick={() => { setSelectedDate(null); setSelectedSticker(null); }}>\n            <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full sm:max-w-md max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col" \n                 onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>\n              <div className="p-4 pb-2 border-b border-gray-100 shrink-0">\n                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-center text-purple-700">Pick a Sticker!</h2>\n                <p className="text-center text-gray-400 text-xs">{formatDisplay(selectedDate)}</p>\n                {!completedDates[selectedDate] && (\n                  <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-orange-500 mt-1 font-semibold">Manual completion mode</p>\n                )}\n              </div>\n              \n              <div className="overflow-y-auto flex-1 p-3">\n                <div className="grid grid-cols-5 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5">\n                  {STICKERS.map(s => (\n                    <button key={s} onClick={() => setSelectedSticker(s)}\n                      className={`aspect-square text-2xl sm:text-3xl rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center ${\n                        selectedSticker === s ? "bg-purple-100 ring-2 ring-purple-500 scale-110" : "bg-gray-50 hover:bg-purple-50"\n                      }`}>\n                      {s}\n                    </button>\n                  ))}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              \n              {activeStickers[selectedDate] && (\n                <div className="text-center py-2 border-t border-gray-100 shrink-0">\n                  <span className="text-2xl">{activeStickers[selectedDate]}</span>\n                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Current sticker</p>\n                </div>\n              )}\n              \n              <div className="flex gap-2 p-4 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 shrink-0">\n                <button onClick={() => { setSelectedDate(null); setSelectedSticker(null); }}\n                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-200 font-bold text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-300 text-sm">Cancel</button>\n                <button onClick={() => applySticker(selectedDate)} disabled={!selectedSticker || saving}\n                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm">\n                  {saving ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : "✓ Apply"}\n                </button>\n              </div>\n              \n              {activeStickers[selectedDate] && (\n                <div className="px-4 pb-4 shrink-0">\n                  <button onClick={() => removeSticker(selectedDate)}\n                    className="w-full py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-100 text-red-600 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-red-200">\n                    🗑️ Remove sticker\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        <div className="mt-4 text-center">\n          <a href="/speech-game" className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm hover:underline">← Back to Game</a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n
```

& 0xff) * f))},${Math.min(255, Math.round(((n >> 8) & 0xff) * f))},${Math.min(255, Math.round((n & 0xff) * f))})`;\n}\n\ninterface District {\n  id: string; name: string; count: number; color: "routes", name: "Routes", count: 97, color: "#a855f7", label: "Space District", sub: "60 API, 37 Pages", cols: 4, rows: 4, qx: 6, qy: 1 },\n  { id: "agents", name: "Agents", count: 39, color: "#f59e0b", label: "Automation Alley", sub: "26 Active", cols: 4, rows: 3, qx: 1, qy: 6 },\n  { id: "services", name: "Services", count: 5, color: "#22c55e", label: "Services Row", cols: 5, rows: 1, qx: 6, qy: 6 },\n  { id: "sites", name: "Sites", count: 3, color: "#ec4899", label: "Sites Square", cols: 2, rows: 2, qx: -3, qy: 1 },\n  { id: "datasets", name: "Datasets", count: 3, color: "#3b82f6", label: "Data Warehouse", cols: 3, rows: 1, qx: 1, qy: 10 },\n];\n\nconst FALLBACK: District[] = FALLBACK_RAW.map(d => ({\n  ...d,\n  ox: CX + (d.qx - d.qy) * GW,\n  oy: CY + (d.qx + d.qy) * false],\n  // Secondary E-W 1 (top)\n  [-6, -1, 12, -1, 10, false],\n  // Secondary E-W 2 (bottom/mid)\n  [-2, 9, 8, 9, 10, false],\n  // Secondary E-W 3 (below sites)\n  [-6, 3, -1, 3, 10, const ct = Math.floor(len / 55);\n    for (let i = 0; i < ct; i++) {\n      const t = (i + 0.5) / ct;\n      const side = r() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1;\n      const off = (w/2 + 8 + r() * 14) * side;\n      ts.push({ x: x1+dx*t+nx*off, y: y1+dy*t+ny*off, s: 0.5+r()*0.5, tp: r()>0.55 ? 1 : 0 });\n    }\n  }\n  const GRID_PARKS: [number, number][] = [\n    [-2, -2], [2, -2], [8, -2],\n    [-2, 6], [-2, 8], fill="#1c1408" />\n      <path d={`M${x},${y-15*s} L${x+3.5*s},${y-5*s} L${x-3.5*s},${y-5*s}Z`} fill="#0e3518" />\n      <path d={`M${x},${y-18*s} L${x+2.8*s},${y-9*s} L${x-2.8*s},${y-9*s}Z`} elemsByType[e.type].push(e);\n  }\n\n  let globalIdx = 0;\n  districts.forEach((d, di) => {\n    const rand = rng(42 + di * 137);\n    const maxH = Math.min(85, 20 + d.count * 0.4);\n    const midX = parallel to left-bottom edge\n              const ax = topX + (rightX - topX) * t, ay = topY + (rightY - topY) * t;\n              const bx = leftX + (bottomX - leftX) * t, by = leftY + (bottomY - leftY) * t;\n              // Lines parallel to right-bottom edge\n              const cx2 = topX + (leftX - topX) * t, cy2 = topY + (leftY - topY) * t;\n              const dx = rightX + (bottomX - rightX) * t, dy = rightY + (bottomY - rightY) * t;\n              return (\n                <g key={`gg${i}`}>\n                  <line x1={ax} y1={ay} x2={bx} y2={by} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.015)" strokeWidth={0.4} />\n                  <line x1={cx2} y1={cy2} x2={dx} y2={dy} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.015)" strokeWidth={0.4} />\n                </g>\n              );\n            })}\n\n            {/* ===== GRASS PATCHES ===== */}\n            {TREES.map((t, i) => (\n              <ellipse key={`gp${i}`} cx={t.x} cy={t.y + 5 * t.s} rx={7 * t.s} ry={3.5 * t.s} fill="#071207" opacity={0.5} />\n            ))}\n\n            {/* ===== INTERNAL DISTRICT ROADS ===== */}\n            {districts.map(d => {\n              const districtVisible = timelineIdx < 0 || buildings.some(b => b.district === d.id && b.elemDate && b.elemDate <= currentDate);\n              if (!districtVisible && timelineIdx >= 0) return null;\n              \n              const els = [];\n              for (let gy = 0; gy <= d.rows; gy++) {\n                const y_line = gy - 0.5;\n                const x1 = d.ox + (-0.5 - y_line) * GW;\n                const y1 = d.oy + (-0.5 + y_line) * GH;\n                const x2 = d.ox + (d.cols - 0.5 - y_line) * GW;\n                const y2 = d.oy + (d.cols - 0.5 + y_line) * GH;\n                els.push(<line key={`r_gy_${d.id}_${gy}`} x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke="#0d1115" strokeWidth={3.5} strokeLinecap="round" />);\n              }\n              for (let gx = 0; gx <= d.cols; gx++) {\n                const x_line = gx - 0.5;\n                const x1 = d.ox + (x_line - (-0.5)) * GW;\n                const y1 = d.oy + (x_line + (-0.5)) * GH;\n                const x2 = d.ox + (x_line - (d.rows - 0.5)) * GW;\n                const y2 = d.oy + (x_line + (d.rows - 0.5)) * GH;\n                els.push(<line key={`r_gx_${d.id}_${gx}`} x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke="#0d1115" strokeWidth={3.5} strokeLinecap="round" />);\n              }\n              return <g key={`int_roads_${d.id}`} opacity={0.8}>{els}</g>;\n            })}\n\n            {/* ===== ROADS ===== */}\n            {ROADS.map(([x1, y1, x2, y2, w, main], i) => (\n              <g key={`rd${i}`}>\n                {/* Sidewalk / road edge */}\n                <line x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke="#0a0a16" strokeWidth={w + 6} strokeLinecap="round" />\n                {/* Road surface */}\n                <line x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke={main ? "#131320" : "#101018"} strokeWidth={w} strokeLinecap="round" />\n                {/* Center line marking */}\n                <line x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke={main ? "rgba(245,158,11,0.1)" : "rgba(245,158,11,0.06)"} strokeWidth={main ? 1.2 : 0.8} strokeDasharray={main ? "10 7" : "6 5"} />\n                {/* Edge lines (white lane markers on main roads) */}\n                {main && (() => {\n                  const dx = x2-x1, dy = y2-y1, len = Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy);\n                  const nx = -dy/len * (w/2 - 1), ny = dx/len * (w/2 - 1);\n                  return (\n                    <>\n                      <line x1={x1+nx} y1={y1+ny} x2={x2+nx} y2={y2+ny} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" strokeWidth={0.5} />\n                      <line x1={x1-nx} y1={y1-ny} x2={x2-nx} y2={y2-ny} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" strokeWidth={0.5} />\n                    </>\n                  );\n                })()}\n              </g>\n            ))}\n\n            {/* Road intersection glow at center (qx=5, qy=5) */}\n            <circle cx={CX + (5 - 5) * GW} cy={CY + (5 + 5) * GH} r={20} fill="rgba(245,158,11,0.03)" />\n\n            {/* ===== TREES (behind buildings, y < split) ===== */}\n            {TREES.filter(t => t.y < treeSplitY).map((t, i) => <Tree key={`tb${i}`} t={t} />)}\n\n            {/* ===== BUILDINGS ===== */}\n            {buildings.map((b, i) => {\n              const scale = buildingScales[i];\n              if (scale <= 0) return null;\n              const active = !hovered || hovered === b.district;\n              const bright = hovered === b.district;\n              return (\n                <g key={i} opacity={active ? 1 : 0.12} style={{ transition: "opacity 0.3s" }}\n                  onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(b.district)} onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(null)}>\n                  <Bldg b={b} bright={bright} scale={scale} />\n                </g>\n              );\n            })}\n\n            {/* District labels */}\n            {districts.map(d => {\n              const cx = d.ox + ((d.cols - 1 - d.rows + 1) / 2) * TW;\n              const districtVisible = timelineIdx < 0 || buildings.some(b => b.district === d.id && b.elemDate && b.elemDate <= currentDate);\n              if (!districtVisible && timelineIdx >= 0) return null;\n              return (\n                <text key={d.id} x={cx} y={d.oy - 18} fill={d.color} fontSize="8" fontWeight="700"\n                  textAnchor="middle" opacity={!hovered || hovered === d.id ? 0.6 : 0.08}\n                  style={{ transition: "opacity 0.3s", letterSpacing: "0.15em" }}>\n                  {d.name.toUpperCase()}\n                </text>\n              );\n            })}\n\n            {/* ===== TREES (in front of buildings, y >= split) ===== */}\n            {TREES.filter(t => t.y >= treeSplitY).map((t, i) => <Tree key={`tf${i}`} t={t} />)}\n          </svg>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stats Panel */}\n        <div className="w-full lg:w-72 xl:w-80 bg-[#0a0a14] border-t lg:border-t-0 lg:border-l border-white/[0.04] p-5 flex flex-col gap-6">\n          <div>\n            <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-600 tracking-[0.2em] font-medium">TOTAL ELEMENTS</p>\n            <p className="text-4xl font-black mt-1 tabular-nums" style={{ textShadow: "0 0 20px rgba(6,182,212,0.25)" }}>{displayTotal}</p>\n          </div>\n          <div className="flex-1">\n            <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-600 tracking-[0.2em] font-medium mb-3">DISTRICTS</p>\n            <div className="space-y-1">\n              {districts.map(d => {\n                const count = currentCounts ? (currentCounts[d.id] ?? 0) : d.count;\n                return (\n                  <div key={d.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-2 py-2 rounded-md cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/[0.03] transition-all"\n                    style={{ opacity: hovered && hovered !== d.id ? 0.3 : 1 }}\n                    onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(d.id)} onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(null)}>\n                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: d.color, boxShadow: `0 0 6px ${d.color}60` }} />\n                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">\n                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold leading-tight">{d.name}</p>\n                      <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 leading-tight">{d.label}{d.sub ? ` · ${d.sub}` : ""}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <p className="text-sm font-mono font-bold shrink-0" style={{ color: d.color }}>{count}</p>\n                  </div>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div className="border-t border-white/[0.04] pt-4">\n            <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-600 tracking-[0.2em] font-medium mb-2">VIEW MODE</p>\n            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">\n              {["Isometric", "Bird\

### `/zo-city-three-test` (page, private)

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";\n\nexport default function Page() {\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading three...");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let dead = false;\n    let raf = 0;\n    let renderer: any;\n    const load = (src: string) => new Promise<void>((ok, bad) => {\n      const found = document.querySelector(`script[src="${src}"]`) as HTMLScriptElement | null;\n      if (found) return ok();\n      const s = document.createElement("script");\n      s.src = src;\n      s.async = true;\n      s.onload = () => ok();\n      s.onerror = () => bad(new Error("script load failed"));\n      document.head.appendChild(s);\n    });\n    (async () => {\n      try {\n        await load("https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.160.0/build/three.min.js");\n        const THREE = (window as any).THREE;\n        if (!THREE) throw new Error("THREE missing");\n        const el = ref.current;\n        if (!el) throw new Error("mount missing");\n        const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n        scene.background = new THREE.Color("#020617");\n        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, 800 / 420, 0.1, 100);\n        camera.position.set(4, 4, 6);\n        camera.lookAt(0, 0.6, 0);\n        renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });\n        renderer.setSize(800, 420);\n        el.innerHTML = "";\n        el.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n        scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.9));\n        const dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x88ccff, 1.4);\n        dl.position.set(4, 8, 3);\n        scene.add(dl);\n        const ground = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(12, 12), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x111827 }));\n        ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n        scene.add(ground);\n        const road = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(7, 0.05, 1.6), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1f2937 }));\n        road.position.y = 0.03;\n        scene.add(road);\n        const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x22d3ee }));\n        box.position.y = 0.7;\n        scene.add(box);\n        const loop = () => {\n          if (dead) return;\n          raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n          box.rotation.y += 0.01;\n          renderer.render(scene, camera);\n        };\n        loop();\n        setStatus("ready");\n      } catch (e: any) {\n        setStatus(`failed: ${e?.message || "unknown"}`);\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => {\n      dead = true;\n      if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);\n      renderer?.dispose?.();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white p-6"><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">ZoCity Three.js Test</h1><p className="text-cyan-300 mb-4">{status}</p><div ref={ref} className="w-[800px] max-w-full min-h-[420px] border border-cyan-500/20 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-black/40" /></div>;\n}\n
```

LayoutDashboard, Settings, Share2, Clock, Briefcase, Sparkles, PenLine\n} from "lucide-react";\nimport { marked } from "marked";\n\ninterface Theme {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  mode: "light" | "dark";\n  accent: string;\n  fontType: string;\n  description: string;\n  tags: string[];\n  keywords: string[];\n}\n\ninterface ThemeDetail extends Theme {\n  prompt: string | null;\n}\n\nconst MODE_OPTIONS = ["all", "light", "dark"] as const;\nconst FONT_OPTIONS = ["all", "sans-serif", "serif", "mono"] as const;\n\nfunction GlobalNav() {\n  const [navOpen, setNavOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [navAuth, setNavAuth] = useState(false);\n  const [navLinks, setNavLinks] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const navRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/nav-links", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" }, credentials: "include" })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { \n        setNavLinks(d.links || []); \n => {\n      clearInterval(interval);\n      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const ICON_MAP: Record<string, any> = {\n    "pencil": PenLine,\n const filteredNavLinks = navLinks.filter(l => l.path !== window.location.pathname);\n\n  const COLORS = {\n    cyan: "#06b6d4",\n    cyanLight: "#22d3ee",\n    indigo: "#6366f1",\n    indigoLight: ref={navRef}>\n        <div className="relative">\n          <button \n            onClick={() => setNavOpen(!navOpen)}\n            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900/50 hover:bg-zinc-800/80 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors border"\n            style={{ borderColor: COLORS.border }}\n          >\n            {navOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: COLORS.cyan }} />}\n          </button>\n          \n          {navOpen && (\n            <div className="absolute top-full right-0 mt-3 min-w-[250px] w-max max-w-md rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"\n              style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.95)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)", border: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}` }}>\n              {navAuth && (\n                <div className="px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono" style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.border}`, color: COLORS.cyan }}>\n                  <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />\n                  <span>Authenticated view</span>\n                </div>\n              )}\n              <div className="py-2 max-h-[70vh] key={link.path} href={link.path}\n                      onClick={() => setNavOpen(false)}\n                      className="flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 group">\n                      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5"\n                        style={{\n                          background: link.category === "private" ? `${COLORS.indigo}15` : `${COLORS.cyan}15`,\n                          border: `1px solid ${link.category === "private" ? `${COLORS.indigo}30` : `${COLORS.cyan}30`}`,\n                        }}>\n                        <IconComp className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: link.category === "private" ? COLORS.indigoLight : COLORS.cyan }} />\n                      </div>\n                      <div className="min-w-0">\n                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white group-hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">{link.name}</span>\n                          {link.category === "private" && (\n                            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider"\n                              style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}20`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>\n                              Private\n                            </span>\n                          )}\n                        </div>\n                        <p className="text-xs mt-0.5 truncate" style={{ color: COLORS.dimmed }}>{link.description}</p>\n                      </div>\n                    </a>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[9999]">\n        {navOpen && (\n          <div\n            className="absolute bottom-16 right-0 mb-2 min-w-[200px] w-max max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden"\n            style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.98)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)",               <div className="w-6 flex justify-center shrink-0">\n                      <IconComp className="w-4 h-4" style={{ color: link.category === "private" ? COLORS.indigoLight : COLORS.cyan }} />\n                    </div>\n                    <span className="truncate">{link.name}</span>\n                    {link.category === "private" && (\n                      <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono"\n                        style={{ background: `${COLORS.indigo}20`, color: COLORS.indigoLight }}>\n                        Private\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </a>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n        \n        <button\n          onClick={() => setNavOpen(!navOpen)}\n          className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg transition-transform hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"\n          style={{ background: COLORS.cyan, color: "white", boxShadow: `0 4px 20px -5px ${COLORS.cyan}60` }}\n        >\n          {navOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ThemeCard({ theme, onSelect }: { theme: Theme; onSelect: (id: string) => void }) {\n  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";\n  const cardBg = isDark ? "bg-zinc-900" : "bg-white";\n  const cardBorder = isDark ? "border-zinc-700" : "border-zinc-200";\n  const textPrimary = isDark ? "text-zinc-100" : "text-zinc-900";\n  const textSecondary = isDark ? "text-zinc-400" : "text-zinc-500";\n  const fontLabel = theme.fontType === "mono" ? "Mono" : theme.fontType === "serif" ? "Serif" : "Sans";\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type="button"\n      onClick={() => onSelect(theme.id)}\n      className={`group block w-full text-left rounded-xl border ${cardBorder} ${cardBg} overflow-hidden transition-all hover:shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-0.5`}\n    >\n      <div className="relative h-28 overflow-hidden" style={{ background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${theme.accent}22, ${theme.accent}66, ${theme.accent}22)` }}>\n        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">\n          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 opacity-70">\n            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: theme.accent }} />\n            <span className={`text-xs font-mono ${isDark ? "text-zinc-300" : "text-zinc-600"}`}>{theme.accent}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 flex gap-1.5">\n          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-medium ${isDark ? "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300" : "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600"}`}>\n            {isDark ? <Moon className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> : <SunMedium className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />}\n            {theme.mode}\n          </span>\n          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-medium ${isDark ? "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300" : "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600"}`}>\n            {fontLabel}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div className="p-4">\n        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">\n          <h3 className={`font-semibold ${textPrimary}`}>{theme.name}</h3>\n          <ChevronRight className={`w-4 h-4 ${textSecondary} opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity`} />\n        </div>\n        <p className={`text-sm ${textSecondary} line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed`}>{theme.description}</p>\n      </div>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ThemeDetailModal({\n  themeId,\n  onClose,\n}: {\n  themeId: string;\n  onClose: () => void;\n}) {\n  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeDetail | null>(null);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);\n  const [hasPreview, setHasPreview] = useState(false);\n  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);\n\n  const previewSrc = `/images/themes/${themeId}-preview.jpg`;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const img = new Image();\n    img.onload = () => setHasPreview(true);\n    img.onerror = () => setHasPreview(false);\n    img.src = previewSrc;\n  }, [previewSrc]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n    setTheme(null);\n    setCopied(false);\n\n    fetch(`/api/zo-space-theme-gallery/${encodeURIComponent(themeId)}`, {\n      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n    })\n      .then(async (r) => {\n        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`Request failed (${r.status})`);\n        return r.json();\n      })\n      .then((data) => {\n        setTheme(data);\n        setLoading(false);\n      })\n      .catch((e) => {\n        setError(e?.message || "Failed to load theme");\n        setLoading(false);\n      });\n  }, [themeId]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {\n      if (e.key === "Escape") {\n        if (previewOpen) setPreviewOpen(false);\n        else onClose();\n      }\n    }\n    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);\n  }, [onClose, previewOpen]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";\n    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ""; };\n  }, []);\n\n  async function copyPrompt() {\n    if (!theme?.prompt) return;\n    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(theme.prompt);\n    setCopied(true);\n    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);\n  }\n\n  const html = useMemo(\n    () => (theme?.prompt ? marked.parse(theme.prompt) : "<p>No prompt available.</p>"),\n    [theme?.prompt]\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <div\n        className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm"\n        onClick={onClose}\n      />\n      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, useRef } from "react";\nimport { ArrowLeft, Copy, Palette, Check, SunMedium, Moon, Eye, X } from "lucide-react";\nimport { marked } from "marked";\n\ninterface Theme {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  mode: "light" | "dark";\n  accent: string;\n  fontType: "sans-serif" | "serif" | "mono";\n  description: string;\n  tags: string[];\n  keywords: string[];\n  prompt: string | null;\n}\n\nfunction GlobalNav() {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [links, setLinks] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [auth, setAuth] = useState(false);\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/nav-links", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } })\n      .then(r => r.json())\n      .then(d => { setLinks(d.links || []); setAuth(d.authenticated); })\n      .catch(() => {});\n      \n    const onClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);\n    };\n    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {/* Desktop: Top-right navigation */}\n      <div className="hidden md:block absolute top-6 right-6 z-50" ref={ref}>\n        <button \n          onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}\n          className="bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700 text-white p-2.5 rounded-lg shadow-xl hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"\n          style={{ borderColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" }}\n        >\n          <span style={{ color: "#06b6d4", fontSize: "20px" }}>☰</span>\n        </button>\n        \n        {open && (\n          <div className="absolute top-full right-0 mt-2 w-64 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"\n            style={{ background: "rgba(15,17,23,0.98)", backdropFilter: "blur(20px)", border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" }}>\n            {auth && (\n              <div className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-cyan-400 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">\n                <span>Authenticated</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n            <div className="py-2 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">\n              {links.map((l, idx) => (\n                <a key={l.path} href={l.path} \n                  onClick={() => setOpen(false)}\n                  className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">\n                  <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider w-6 text-cyan-400">\n                    {String(idx + 1).padStart(2, \

string;\n};\n\ntype GeminiBucket = {\n  modelId: string;\n  remainingFraction: number;\n  resetTime: string;\n  tokenType?: string;\n};\n\ntype GeminiUsage = {\n  used: number;\n  limit: number;\n  percentage: number;\n  status: "ok" | "exhausted" | "warning" | "error" | "no_data";\n  resetTime: string | null;\n  recentRequests?: number;\n  details?: string;\n};\n\nexport default function [bootTime, setBootTime] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [diagnosing, setDiagnosing] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});\n  const [diagnoseSuccess, setDiagnoseSuccess] = const date = new Date(json.bootTime.replace(" ", "T") + "Z");\n            setBootTime(date.toLocaleString());\n          } catch (e) {\n            setBootTime(json.bootTime);\n          }\n  console.error(e);\n    }\n    setLoading(false);\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchData();\n    const interval = setInterval(fetchData, 60000);\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, import { useState, useEffect } from "react";\nimport { Layout, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";\n\nconst theme = {\n  background: "#0f0f0e",\n  foreground: "#f5f1e8",\n  card: "rgba(28, 26, 22, 0.82)",\n  muted: "#7a746b",\n  accent: "#d4a04a",\n  border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",\n};\n\nexport default function ZoBoard() {\n  const [boards, setBoards] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/zoboard/boards", {\n      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n    })\n      .then((r) => {\n        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);\n        return r.json();\n      })\n      .then((data) => {\n        setBoards(data.boards ?? []);\n        setLoading(false);\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        setError(err.message);\n        setLoading(false);\n      });\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <main\n      style={\n        {\n          "--page-bg": theme.background,\n          "--page-fg": theme.foreground,\n          "--page-card": theme.card,\n          "--page-muted": theme.muted,\n          "--page-accent": theme.accent,\n          "--page-border": theme.border,\n        } as React.CSSProperties\n      }\n      className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--page-bg)] text-[var(--page-fg)]"\n    >\n      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">\n        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-8">\n          <Layout className="w-8 h-8 text-[var(--page-accent)]" />\n          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">ZoBoard</h1>\n          <span className="text-[var(--page-muted)] text-sm ml-2">\n            Visual Project Canvas\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {loading && (\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n            <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />\n            Loading boards...\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {error && (\n          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-red-400 bg-red-950/30 border border-red-800/30 rounded-lg p-4">\n            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5" />\n            <span>Failed to load boards: {error}</span>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {!loading && !error && (\n          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">\n            {boards.map((slug) => (\n              <a\n                key={slug}\n                href={`/zoboard/${slug}`}\n                className="block p-6 rounded-xl border border-[var(--page-border)] bg-[var(--page-card)] hover:border-[var(--page-accent)]/40 transition-colors"\n              >\n                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold capitalize">{slug}</h2>\n                <p className="text-[var(--page-muted)] text-sm mt-1">\n                  Project board\n                </p>\n              </a>\n            ))}\n            {boards.length === 0 && (\n              <div className="col-span-full text-center py-16 text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n                <Layout className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40" />\n                <p>No boards yet.</p>\n                <p className="text-sm mt-1">Create one via the API or seed script.</p>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n
