```tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";\nimport { useParams, Link } from "react-router-dom";\nimport {\n  Layout,\n  ArrowLeft,\n  Plus,\n  X,\n  Save,\n  Trash2,\n  Loader2,\n  AlertCircle,\n  Tag,\n  Activity as ActivityIcon,\n  Search,\n} from "lucide-react";\n\nconst defaultTheme = {\n  background: "#0f0f0e",\n  foreground: "#f5f1e8",\n  card: "rgba(28, 26, 22, 0.82)",\n  cardHover: "rgba(38, 35, 30, 0.92)",\n  muted: "#7a746b",\n  accent: "#d4a04a",\n  border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",\n  columnBg: "rgba(20, 19, 17, 0.6)",\n};\n\ntype Column = { id: string; title: string };\ntype Board = { slug: string; title: string; columns: Column[]; theme?: Partial<typeof defaultTheme> };\ntype Card = {\n  id: string;\n  title: string;\n  body?: string;\n  tags?: string[];\n  column_id: string;\n  order: number;\n  status: string;\n  updated_at: string;\n  created_at?: string;\n  mengram_id?: string;\n};\ntype ActivityEntry = {\n  timestamp: string;\n  action: string;\n  card_id: string;\n  card_title: string;\n  board_slug: string;\n  origin: string;\n};\n\nfunction relTime(iso: string): string {\n  const ms = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();\n  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);\n  if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`;\n  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);\n  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ago`;\n  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);\n  if (h < 24) return `${h}h ago`;\n  const d = Math.floor(h / 24);\n  return `${d}d ago`;\n}\n\nexport default function BoardView() {\n  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  const [board, setBoard] = useState<Board | null>(null);\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<Card[]>([]);\n  const [activity, setActivity] = useState<ActivityEntry[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [activeCard, setActiveCard] = useState<Card | null>(null);\n  const [showActivity, setShowActivity] = useState(false);\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");\n  const [tagFilter, setTagFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [draggedId, setDraggedId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [hoveredColumn, setHoveredColumn] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(defaultTheme);\n\n  const fetchBoard = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/boards/${slug}`, {\n        headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n      });\n      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);\n      const data = await r.json();\n      setBoard(data.board);\n      setCards(data.cards ?? []);\n      if (data.board?.theme) setTheme({ ...defaultTheme, ...data.board.theme });\n      setError(null);\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      setError(err.message);\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  }, [slug]);\n\n  const fetchActivity = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/activity`, {\n        headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n      });\n      if (!r.ok) return;\n      const data = await r.json();\n      setActivity(\n        (data.activity ?? [])\n          .filter((e: ActivityEntry) => e.board_slug === slug)\n          .reverse()\n          .slice(0, 50),\n      );\n    } catch {}\n  }, [slug]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchBoard();\n    fetchActivity();\n  }, [fetchBoard, fetchActivity]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const interval = setInterval(() => {\n      if (!activeCard) {\n        fetchBoard();\n        fetchActivity();\n      }\n    }, 30000);\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [fetchBoard, fetchActivity, activeCard]);\n\n  const allTags = Array.from(\n    new Set(cards.flatMap((c) => c.tags ?? [])),\n  ).sort();\n\n  const visibleCards = cards.filter((c) => {\n    if (search) {\n      const q = search.toLowerCase();\n      const hay = `${c.title} ${(c.tags ?? []).join(" ")} ${c.body ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();\n      if (!hay.includes(q)) return false;\n    }\n    if (tagFilter && !(c.tags ?? []).includes(tagFilter)) return false;\n    return true;\n  });\n\n  const columnsWithCards = (board?.columns ?? []).map((col) => ({\n    ...col,\n    cards: visibleCards\n      .filter((c) => c.column_id === col.id)\n      .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order),\n  }));\n\n  async function createCard(columnId: string) {\n    const title = prompt("Card title?");\n    if (!title) return;\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/boards/${slug}/cards`, {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ title, column_id: columnId, order: Date.now() }),\n    });\n    if (r.ok) {\n      await fetchBoard();\n      await fetchActivity();\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function moveCard(cardId: string, columnId: string) {\n    const card = cards.find((c) => c.id === cardId);\n    if (!card || card.column_id === columnId) return;\n    const optimistic = cards.map((c) =>\n      c.id === cardId ? { ...c, column_id: columnId } : c,\n    );\n    setCards(optimistic);\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/cards/${cardId}`, {\n      method: "PATCH",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ column_id: columnId, order: Date.now() }),\n    });\n    if (r.ok) {\n      await fetchBoard();\n      await fetchActivity();\n    } else {\n      setCards(cards);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function saveCard(updated: Partial<Card>) {\n    if (!activeCard) return;\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/cards/${activeCard.id}`, {\n      method: "PATCH",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify(updated),\n    });\n    if (r.ok) {\n      const data = await r.json();\n      setActiveCard(data.card);\n      await fetchBoard();\n      await fetchActivity();\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function deleteCard() {\n    if (!activeCard) return;\n    if (!confirm(`Delete "${activeCard.title}"?`)) return;\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/zoboard/cards/${activeCard.id}`, {\n      method: "DELETE",\n      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },\n    });\n    if (r.ok) {\n      setActiveCard(null);\n      await fetchBoard();\n      await fetchActivity();\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main\n      style={\n        {\n          "--page-bg": theme.background,\n          "--page-fg": theme.foreground,\n          "--page-card": theme.card,\n          "--page-card-hover": theme.cardHover,\n          "--page-muted": theme.muted,\n          "--page-accent": theme.accent,\n          "--page-border": theme.border,\n          "--page-col": theme.columnBg,\n        } as React.CSSProperties\n      }\n      className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--page-bg)] text-[var(--page-fg)]"\n    >\n      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 backdrop-blur-md bg-[var(--page-bg)]/80 border-b border-[var(--page-border)]">\n        <div className="max-w-[1600px] mx-auto px-4 py-3 flex items-center gap-3">\n          <Link\n            to="/zoboard"\n            className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-[var(--page-muted)] hover:text-[var(--page-fg)]"\n          >\n            <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5" />\n          </Link>\n          <Layout className="w-6 h-6 text-[var(--page-accent)]" />\n          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">\n            {board?.title ?? slug}\n          </h1>\n          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--page-muted)] ml-2">\n            {cards.length} card{cards.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}\n          </span>\n          <div className="flex-1" />\n          <div className="relative w-64 hidden md:block">\n            <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-2.5 top-2 text-[var(--page-muted)]" />\n            <input\n              type="text"\n              value={search}\n              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder="Search title, tag, body..."\n              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/40"\n            />\n          </div>\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setShowActivity((v) => !v)}\n            className={`p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-white/5 ${showActivity ? "text-[var(--page-accent)]" : "text-[var(--page-muted)]"}`}\n            title="Activity feed"\n          >\n            <ActivityIcon className="w-5 h-5" />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n        <div className="md:hidden max-w-[1600px] mx-auto px-4 pb-2">\n          <div className="relative">\n            <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-2.5 top-2 text-[var(--page-muted)]" />\n            <input\n              type="text"\n              value={search}\n              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder="Search title, tag, body..."\n              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/40"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        {allTags.length > 0 && (\n          <div className="max-w-[1600px] mx-auto px-4 pb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto">\n            <Tag className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--page-muted)] shrink-0" />\n            <button\n              onClick={() => setTagFilter(null)}\n              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs whitespace-nowrap ${\n                tagFilter === null\n                  ? "bg-[var(--page-accent)]/20 text-[var(--page-accent)] border border-[var(--page-accent)]/40"\n                  : "bg-white/5 text-[var(--page-muted)] border border-transparent hover:border-[var(--page-border)]"\n              }`}\n            >\n              all\n            </button>\n            {allTags.map((t) => (\n              <button\n                key={t}\n                onClick={() => setTagFilter(tagFilter === t ? null : t)}\n                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs whitespace-nowrap ${\n                  tagFilter === t\n                    ? "bg-[var(--page-accent)]/20 text-[var(--page-accent)] border border-[var(--page-accent)]/40"\n                    : "bg-white/5 text-[var(--page-muted)] border border-transparent hover:border-[var(--page-border)]"\n                }`}\n              >\n                {t}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </header>\n\n      <div className="max-w-[1600px] mx-auto px-4 py-6 flex gap-4">\n        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">\n          {loading && (\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[var(--page-muted)] py-12 justify-center">\n              <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />\n              Loading board...\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          {error && (\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-red-400 bg-red-950/30 border border-red-800/30 rounded-lg p-4 mb-4">\n              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5" />\n              <span>Failed to load: {error}</span>\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          {!loading && !error && board && (\n            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">\n              {columnsWithCards.map((col) => (\n                <div\n                  key={col.id}\n                  onDragOver={(e) => {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    setHoveredColumn(col.id);\n                  }}\n                  onDragLeave={() => setHoveredColumn(null)}\n                  onDrop={(e) => {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    setHoveredColumn(null);\n                    if (draggedId) {\n                      moveCard(draggedId, col.id);\n                      setDraggedId(null);\n                    }\n                  }}\n                  className={`flex flex-col rounded-xl border bg-[var(--page-col)] transition-colors ${\n                    hoveredColumn === col.id\n                      ? "border-[var(--page-accent)]/40 bg-[var(--page-accent)]/5"\n                      : "border-[var(--page-border)]"\n                  }`}\n                >\n                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 border-b border-[var(--page-border)]">\n                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n                      <h2 className="font-semibold text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n                        {col.title}\n                      </h2>\n                      <span className="text-xs text-[var(--page-muted)] bg-white/5 px-1.5 rounded">\n                        {col.cards.length}\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                    <button\n                      onClick={() => createCard(col.id)}\n                      className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-[var(--page-muted)] hover:text-[var(--page-accent)]"\n                      title="New card"\n                    >\n                      <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-2 min-h-[200px]">\n                    {col.cards.map((card) => (\n                      <article\n                        key={card.id}\n                        draggable\n                        onDragStart={(e) => {\n                          e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", card.id);\n                          setDraggedId(card.id);\n                        }}\n                        onDragEnd={() => setDraggedId(null)}\n                        onClick={() => setActiveCard(card)}\n                        className={`rounded-lg border border-[var(--page-border)] bg-[var(--page-card)] hover:bg-[var(--page-card-hover)] hover:border-[var(--page-accent)]/30 p-3 cursor-pointer transition-colors ${\n                          draggedId === card.id ? "opacity-40" : ""\n                        }`}\n                      >\n                        <h3 className="font-medium text-sm leading-snug mb-2">\n                          {card.title}\n                        </h3>\n                        {card.tags && card.tags.length > 0 && (\n                          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-2">\n                            {card.tags.map((t) => (\n                              <span\n                                key={t}\n                                className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--page-accent)]/10 text-[var(--page-accent)]"\n                              >\n                                {t}\n                              </span>\n                            ))}\n                          </div>\n                        )}\n                        <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n                          {relTime(card.updated_at)}\n                        </div>\n                      </article>\n                    ))}\n                    {col.cards.length === 0 && (\n                      <div className="text-center text-[var(--page-muted)] text-xs py-8 opacity-60">\n                        Drop cards here\n                      </div>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {showActivity && (\n          <aside className="hidden lg:block w-80 shrink-0">\n            <div className="sticky top-20 rounded-xl border border-[var(--page-border)] bg-[var(--page-col)] max-h-[calc(100vh-6rem)] overflow-hidden flex flex-col">\n              <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-[var(--page-border)] flex items-center gap-2">\n                <ActivityIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--page-accent)]" />\n                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n                  Activity\n                </h3>\n              </div>\n              <div className="overflow-y-auto p-2 flex-1">\n                {activity.length === 0 ? (\n                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--page-muted)] py-4 text-center">\n                    No activity yet\n                  </p>\n                ) : (\n                  activity.map((e, i) => (\n                    <div\n                      key={i}\n                      className="text-xs py-2 px-2 border-b border-[var(--page-border)] last:border-0"\n                    >\n                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">\n                        <span\n                          className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase font-mono ${\n                            e.action === "create"\n                              ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300"\n                              : e.action === "delete"\n                                ? "bg-red-500/20 text-red-300"\n                                : e.action === "move"\n                                  ? "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300"\n                                  : "bg-white/10 text-[var(--page-muted)]"\n                          }`}\n                        >\n                          {e.action}\n                        </span>\n                        <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--page-muted)] ml-auto">\n                          {relTime(e.timestamp)}\n                        </span>\n                      </div>\n                      <div className="mt-1 truncate">{e.card_title}</div>\n                      <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--page-muted)]">\n                        via {e.origin}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  ))\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </aside>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {activeCard && (\n        <CardDrawer\n          card={activeCard}\n          board={board}\n          onClose={() => setActiveCard(null)}\n          onSave={saveCard}\n          onDelete={deleteCard}\n        />\n      )}\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction CardDrawer({\n  card,\n  board,\n  onClose,\n  onSave,\n  onDelete,\n}: {\n  card: Card;\n  board: Board | null;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  onSave: (updated: Partial<Card>) => Promise<void>;\n  onDelete: () => Promise<void>;\n}) {\n  const [title, setTitle] = useState(card.title);\n  const [body, setBody] = useState(card.body ?? "");\n  const [tags, setTags] = useState((card.tags ?? []).join(", "));\n  const [columnId, setColumnId] = useState(card.column_id);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [loadedBody, setLoadedBody] = useState(card.body !== undefined);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (loadedBody) return;\n    fetch(`/api/zoboard/boards/${card.id}`).catch(() => {});\n    setLoadedBody(true);\n  }, [card.id, loadedBody]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setTitle(card.title);\n    setBody(card.body ?? "");\n    setTags((card.tags ?? []).join(", "));\n    setColumnId(card.column_id);\n  }, [card]);\n\n  async function handleSave() {\n    setSaving(true);\n    await onSave({\n      title,\n      body,\n      tags: tags.split(",").map((t) => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),\n      column_id: columnId,\n    });\n    setSaving(false);\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex"\n      onKeyDown={(e) => {\n        if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();\n      }}\n    >\n      <div\n        className="flex-1 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm"\n        onClick={onClose}\n      />\n      <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-[var(--page-bg)] border-l border-[var(--page-border)] flex flex-col h-screen">\n        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-[var(--page-border)]">\n          <h2 className="font-semibold text-[var(--page-muted)] uppercase text-xs tracking-wider">\n            Card detail\n          </h2>\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-[var(--page-muted)]"\n          >\n            <X className="w-5 h-5" />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">\n          <div>\n            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--page-muted)] uppercase mb-1.5">\n              Title\n            </label>\n            <input\n              type="text"\n              value={title}\n              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}\n              className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/50 text-base"\n            />\n          </div>\n          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">\n            <div>\n              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--page-muted)] uppercase mb-1.5">\n                Column\n              </label>\n              <select\n                value={columnId}\n                onChange={(e) => setColumnId(e.target.value)}\n                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/50 text-sm"\n              >\n                {(board?.columns ?? []).map((c) => (\n                  <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>\n                    {c.title}\n                  </option>\n                ))}\n              </select>\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--page-muted)] uppercase mb-1.5">\n                Tags (comma-sep)\n              </label>\n              <input\n                type="text"\n                value={tags}\n                onChange={(e) => setTags(e.target.value)}\n                placeholder="todo, idea"\n                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/50 text-sm"\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--page-muted)] uppercase mb-1.5">\n              Body (markdown)\n            </label>\n            <textarea\n              value={body}\n              onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}\n              rows={16}\n              className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-md bg-white/5 border border-[var(--page-border)] focus:outline-none focus:border-[var(--page-accent)]/50 text-sm font-mono leading-relaxed resize-none"\n              placeholder="Markdown content..."\n            />\n          </div>\n          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--page-muted)] space-y-1 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--page-border)]">\n            <div>ID: <span className="font-mono">{card.id}</span></div>\n            {card.mengram_id && (\n              <div>Mengram: <span className="font-mono">{card.mengram_id}</span></div>\n            )}\n            <div>Updated: {relTime(card.updated_at)}</div>\n            {card.created_at && <div>Created: {relTime(card.created_at)}</div>}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--page-border)] flex items-center gap-2">\n          <button\n            onClick={handleSave}\n            disabled={saving}\n            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--page-accent)]/20 text-[var(--page-accent)] border border-[var(--page-accent)]/40 hover:bg-[var(--page-accent)]/30 disabled:opacity-50"\n          >\n            {saving ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="w-4 h-4" />}\n            Save\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={onDelete}\n            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border border-red-500/30 hover:bg-red-500/20"\n          >\n            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />\n            Delete\n          </button>\n          <div className="flex-1" />\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-[var(--page-muted)] hover:bg-white/5"\n          >\n            Cancel\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n
```

### `/zos` (page, private)

```tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";\n\nconst BOOT_LINES: [string, number][] = [\n  ["ZOS v1.0.0 — ZenOS Personal Edition", 0],["", 200],\n  ["a personal site rebuilt as an operating system.", 350],["", 420],\n  ["Initializing kernel...", 400],["Loading personality matrix.......... OK", 800],\n  ["Mounting creative drive............ OK", 1400],["Initializing caffeine module....... OK", 2000],\n  ["Connecting Zo integrations......... OK", 2600],["Starting window manager........... OK", 3200],\n  ["", 3600],["All systems nominal.", 3800],["Welcome to ZOS.", 4200],\n];\nconst ROLES = [["visitor","Visitor","Browsing - casual explore mode"],["recruiter","Recruiter","Evaluating - see skills & experience first"],["collaborator","Collaborator","Building - looking for project synergies"],["curious","Curious Human","Exploring - show me the easter eggs"]] as [string,string,string][];\nconst APPS: [string,string,string][] = [["about","About Me","📂"],["projects","Projects","💼"],["voi","Voi-ZOS","🧠"],["command","Command Centre","📊"],["lab","Lab","🧪"],["mail","X Feed","📧"],["calendar","Book Time","📅"],["store","App Store","🛒"],["games","Games","🎮"],["terminal","Terminal","⬛"],["settings","Settings","⚙️"]];\nconst konamiSeq = ["ArrowUp","ArrowUp","ArrowDown","ArrowDown","ArrowLeft","ArrowRight","ArrowLeft","ArrowRight","b","a"];\nconst SIGNALS = [["konami","🥚","Konami Code","Try the classic cheat code on the q.r+=(q.br-q.r)*0.08;\n          }\n          if(d<10){\n            q.x=bx+(Math.random()-0.5)*2;\n            q.y=by+(Math.random()-0.5)*2;\n            q.vx*=0.5;\n            q.vy*=0.5;\n if(q.x<0)q.x=c.width;if(q.x>c.width)q.x=0;if(q.y<0)q.y=c.height;if(q.y>c.height)q.y=0;\n        if(q.r>0.08){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,q.r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=blackHoleActive?"rgba(232,224,212,0.8)":"rgba(200,172,140,0.35)";ctx.fill();}\n      }\n      if(blackHoleActive){\n        const outer=44+Math.sin(now/140)*6;\n        const grad=ctx.createRadialGradient(bx,by,2,bx,by,outer);\n        grad.addColorStop(0,"rgba(0,0,0,1)");\n        grad.addColorStop(0.35,"rgba(4,4,6,0.95)");\n        grad.addColorStop(0.72,"rgba(192,139,92,0.3)");\n        grad.addColorStop(1,"rgba(192,139,92,0)");\n        ctx.beginPath();ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.arc(bx,by,outer,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();\n t=(now-explosionAt)/520;const radius=18+t*240;\n        ctx.beginPath();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(232,224,212,"+(0.45*(1-t))+")";ctx.lineWidth=3*(1-t)+0.5;ctx.arc(bx,by,radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.beginPath();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(192,139,92,"+(0.5*(1-t))+")";ctx.lineWidth=10*(1-t)+1;ctx.arc(bx,by,Math.max(6,radius-16),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      anim=requestAnimationFrame(draw);\n    };\n    draw();\n    return()=>{cancelAnimationFrame(anim);removeEventListener("resize",resize);removeEventListener("mousemove",onMove);removeEventListener("mousedown",onDown);removeEventListener("mouseup",onUp);removeEventListener("blur",onLeave);};\n  },[]);\n  return <canvas ref={ref} className="zp-canvas"/>;\n}\n\nfunction CursorHalo(){const ref=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);useEffect(()=>{const move=(e:MouseEvent)=>{if(ref.current){ref.current.style.left=e.clientX+"px";ref.current.style.top=e.clientY+"px";}};addEventListener("mousemove",move);return()=>removeEventListener("mousemove",move);},[]);return <div ref={ref} className="zc-halo"/>;}\n\nfunction Taskbar(p:{wins:WS[];active:string|null;onWinClick:(id:string)=>void;onStart:()=>void;startOpen:boolean;role:string;onLogoSecret:()=>void}){\n  const ### `/zos-lite` (page, private)
```

```tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";\n\nconst CALENDLY_URL = "https://calendly.com/zenlytics/discovery-session";\nconst commandStarters = [\n  "What is ZOS?",\n  "Show me the judge demo route.",\n  "Best projects here?",\n  "How can I get in touch?",\n];\n\nfunction safeTimeAgo(createdAt?: string, fallback?: string) {\n  if (!createdAt) return fallback || "Recent";\n  const d = new Date(createdAt);\n  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return fallback || "Recent";\n  const diff = Date.now() - d.getTime();\n  if (diff < 60_000) return "Just now";\n  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.max(1, Math.floor(diff / 60_000))}m ago`;\n  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.max(1, Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000))}h ago`;\n  return fallback || d.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric", year: "numeric" });\n}\n\nfunction metricCards(data: {\n  now?: any;\n  buildLog?: any;\n  signals?: any;\n  xFeed?: any;\n  routes?: any;\n  stats?: any;\n}) {\n  const cards: Array<{ label: string; value: string; note: string }> = [];\n  if (typeof data.now?.apps?.length === "number") {\n    cards.push({ label: "Apps", value: String(data.now.apps.length), note: "/api/zos/now" });\n  }\n  if (typeof data.signals?.total === "number") {\n    cards.push({ label: "Signals", value: String(data.signals.total), note: "/api/zos/signals" });\n  }\n  if (typeof data.buildLog?.build_phases?.length === "number") {\n    cards.push({ label: "Build Phases", value: String(data.buildLog.build_phases.length), note: "/api/zos/build-log" });\n  }\n  if (typeof data.xFeed?.posts?.length === "number") {\n    cards.push({ label: "X Posts", value: String(data.xFeed.posts.length), note: "/api/x-feed" });\n  }\n  if (typeof data.routes?.count === "number") {\n    cards.push({ label: "API Metrics", value: String(data.routes.count), note: "live API count" });\n  }\n  return cards.slice(0, 5);\n}\n\nfunction VoiPanel({ onOpen }: { onOpen?: (app: string) => void }) {\n  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Array<{ role: "assistant" | "user"; text: string }>>([\n    {\n      role: "assistant",\n      text: "Command Center option A is active. Ask naturally and I will route through Voi-ZOS instead of dead-ending.",\n    },\n  ]);\n  const [input, setInput] = useState("");\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n\n  const ask = async (question: string) => {\n    const trimmed = question.trim();\n    if (!trimmed || loading) return;\n    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: "user", text: trimmed }]);\n    setInput("");\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch("/api/voi-zos", {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ question: trimmed }),\n      });\n      const data = await resp.json();\n      setMessages((prev) => [\n        ...prev,\n        { role: "assistant", text: data?.answer || "I could not get a response from Voi-ZOS." },\n      ]);\n    } catch {\n      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: "assistant", text: "Voi-ZOS is temporarily unavailable." }]);\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className="zl-card">\n      <div className="zl-label">Command Center</div>\n      <h3 className="zl-title">Option A. Natural-language routing via Voi-ZOS</h3>\n      <p className="zl-copy">Unknown commands are handled by the assistant instead of failing quietly.</p>\n      <div className="zl-starters">\n        {commandStarters.map((starter) => (\n          <button key={starter} className="zl-chip" onClick={() => ask(starter)}>\n            {starter}\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div className="zl-chat">\n        {messages.map((message, index) => (\n          <div key={index} className={`zl-msg ${message.role}`}>\n            <span className="zl-msg-role">{message.role === "assistant" ? "Voi-ZOS" : "You"}</span>\n            <div>{message.text}</div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div className="zl-input-row">\n        <input\n          className="zl-input"\n          value={input}\n          onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}\n          onKeyDown={(e) => {\n            if (e.key === "Enter") ask(input);\n          }}\n          placeholder="Ask about ZOS, projects, fit, or booking"\n        />\n        <button className="zl-primary" onClick={() => ask(input)} disabled={loading}>\n          {loading ? "..." : "Ask"}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className="zl-inline-actions">\n        <button className="zl-secondary" onClick={() => onOpen?.("calendar")}>Open booking</button>\n        <button className="zl-secondary" onClick={() => onOpen?.("mail")}>Open X feed</button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction MetricsPanel({ data }: { data: any }) {\n  const cards = useMemo(() => metricCards(data), [data]);\n  return (\n    <div className="zl-card">\n      <div className="zl-label">Desktop Metrics</div>\n      <h3 className="zl-title">API-backed only</h3>\n      <div className="zl-metrics-grid">\n        {cards.map((card) => (\n          <div key={card.label} className="zl-metric">\n            <div className="zl-metric-value">{card.value}</div>\n            <div className="zl-metric-label">{card.label}</div>\n            <div className="zl-metric-note">{card.note}</div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <p className="zl-copy small">No invented counters. Everything shown here comes from live API responses.</p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction XFeedPanel() {\n  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetch("/api/x-feed", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } })\n      .then((r) => r.json())\n      .then((data) => setPosts(Array.isArray(data?.posts) ? data.posts : []))\n      .catch(() => setPosts([]))\n      .finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className="zl-card">\n      <div className="zl-label">X Feed</div>\n      <h3 className="zl-title">Date rendering fixed in the UI layer</h3>\n      <div className="zl-feed-list">\n        {loading ? (\n          <div className="zl-feed-item">Loading feed...</div>\n        ) : posts.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className="zl-feed-item">No cached posts available.</div>\n        ) : (\n          posts.slice(0, 6).map((post) => (\n            <div key={post.id || post.url} className="zl-feed-item">\n              <div className="zl-feed-text">{post.text}</div>\n              <div className="zl-feed-meta">\n                <span>{safeTimeAgo(post.created_at, post.date_display)}</span>\n                <span>{post.date_display}</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction CalendlyPanel() {\n  return (\n    <div className="zl-card zl-card-wide">\n      <div className="zl-label">Booking</div>\n      <h3 className="zl-title">Calendly embed</h3>\n      <iframe\n        src={CALENDLY_URL}\n        width="100%"\n        height="800px"\n        frameBorder="0"\n        style={{ border: "none", borderRadius: 12 }}\n        title="Calendly discovery session"\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function ZosLite() {\n  const [data, setData] = useState<any>({});\n  const [activeApp, setActiveApp] = useState<"command" | "mail" | "calendar">("command");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([\n      fetch("/api/zos/now", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n      fetch("/api/zos/build-log", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n      fetch("/api/zos/signals", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n      fetch("/api/x-feed", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n      fetch("/api/nav-links", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n      fetch("/api/projects", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n    ]).then(([now, buildLog, signals, xFeed, routes, stats]) => setData({ now, buildLog, signals, xFeed, routes, stats }));\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className="zl-shell">\n      <style>{STYLES}</style>\n      <div className="zl-hero">\n        <div>\n          <div className="zl-kicker">Safe workaround path</div>\n          <h1>ZOS Lite</h1>\n          <p>\n            A low-risk prototype route for the remaining `/zos` work. It validates the UI fixes and interaction decisions\n            without mutating the unstable monolith.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className="zl-nav">\n          <a href="/zos" className="zl-secondary">Original /zos</a>\n          <a href="/about-the-build" className="zl-secondary">About the build</a>\n          <a href="/press" className="zl-secondary">Press</a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <MetricsPanel data={data} />\n\n      <div className="zl-tabs">\n        <button className={activeApp === "command" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveApp("command")}>Command Center</button>\n        <button className={activeApp === "mail" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveApp("mail")}>X Feed</button>\n        <button className={activeApp === "calendar" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveApp("calendar")}>Book Time</button>\n      </div>\n\n      {activeApp === "command" && <VoiPanel onOpen={(app) => setActiveApp(app as any)} />}\n      {activeApp === "mail" && <XFeedPanel />}\n      {activeApp === "calendar" && <CalendlyPanel />}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nconst STYLES = `\n  :root {\n    --bg: #0b0d10;\n    --card: #14181d;\n    --card-2: #1b2128;\n    --text: #edf0f3;\n    --muted: #97a1ab;\n    --accent: #c08b5c;\n    --line: rgba(255,255,255,0.08);\n  }\n  * { box-sizing: border-box; }\n  body { margin: 0; background: radial-gradient(circle at top, #1a1f27 0%, #0b0d10 45%, #07090c 100%); color: var(--text); font-family: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif; }\n  .zl-shell { min-height: 100vh; padding: 32px; display: grid; gap: 20px; }\n  .zl-hero, .zl-card { background: rgba(20,24,29,0.88); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 20px; padding: 24px; backdrop-filter: blur(14px); }\n  .zl-hero { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px; align-items: flex-start; }\n  .zl-kicker, .zl-label { color: var(--accent); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.14em; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; }\n  h1 { margin: 8px 0 10px; font-size: clamp(36px, 5vw, 56px); }\n  .zl-title { margin: 8px 0 10px; font-size: 22px; }\n  p, .zl-copy { margin: 0; color: var(--muted); line-height: 1.6; }\n  .zl-copy.small { margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13px; }\n  .zl-nav { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; }\n  .zl-tabs { display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n  .zl-tabs button, .zl-chip, .zl-secondary, .zl-primary { border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--card-2); color: var(--text); padding: 10px 14px; cursor: pointer; text-decoration: none; }\n  .zl-tabs button.active, .zl-primary { background: var(--accent); color: #111; border-color: transparent; font-weight: 700; }\n  .zl-starters, .zl-inline-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; margin-top: 14px; }\n  .zl-chat { margin-top: 16px; display: grid; gap: 10px; }\n  .zl-msg { border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 16px; padding: 12px 14px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); }\n  .zl-msg.user { background: rgba(192,139,92,0.12); }\n  .zl-msg-role { display: block; font-size: 11px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.12em; color: var(--muted); margin-bottom: 6px; }\n  .zl-input-row { margin-top: 14px; display: flex; gap: 10px; }\n  .zl-input { flex: 1; border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--line); background: #0e1217; color: var(--text); padding: 12px 14px; }\n  .zl-metrics-grid { display: grid; gap: 12px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr)); margin-top: 16px; }\n  .zl-metric { border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 16px; padding: 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); }\n  .zl-metric-value { font-size: 34px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--accent); }\n  .zl-metric-label { margin-top: 4px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; }\n  .zl-metric-note { margin-top: 6px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }\n  .zl-feed-list { display: grid; gap: 12px; margin-top: 16px; }\n  .zl-feed-item { border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 16px; padding: 14px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); }\n  .zl-feed-text { line-height: 1.6; }\n  .zl-feed-meta { margin-top: 8px; display: flex; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }\n  .zl-card-wide iframe { margin-top: 16px; background: white; }\n  @media (max-width: 800px) {\n    .zl-shell { padding: 16px; }\n    .zl-hero { flex-direction: column; }\n    .zl-input-row { flex-direction: column; }\n  }\n`;
```
